# Moments and Community (3b) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Add six moment posts to the live broadcast pipeline (welcome, countdown, movers, spotlight, New on CCIP and a weekly Telegram poll), each with a 1200×675 card, posted at most twice a day besides the poll, behind a `MOMENTS` switch, without ever stopping the data run or the 3a posts.

**Architecture:** Pure moment rules (boards and movers, the spotlight pick, countdowns, the New on CCIP window, the poll's question and answer) live in `@ccip-dev/core/moments`, with the `moments.json` schema in `@ccip-dev/core/public`. After each finalize the data Worker builds `moments.json` from D1 through the same rankings as the other public files and publishes it inside `publishHistoryFiles`; Monday's early finalize first closes last week's poll so its votes are in that file. The broadcast pipeline then plans the moments after the 3a posts, from the same builder, so a caption and its card agree: it applies the budget, the carry-over and the first-run seeding, and sends each through 3a's claim and per-output keys, the poll as one more output (`telegram-poll`) after its card. The site's card Worker draws the six cards from `moments.json`, `replay.json` and `chains.json`.

**Tech Stack:** TypeScript (ESM), Vitest 4, Cloudflare Workers with D1, R2 and crons (`@cloudflare/vitest-plugin`), zod 4, `@cf-wasm/og` 0.5.0 (Satori and resvg-wasm), the Telegram Bot API (`sendPhoto`, `sendPoll`, `stopPoll`). No new dependencies.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-moments-design.md`, with the approved card mockups in `docs/superpowers/specs/assets/2026-10-12-moments/` (`mockups.mts` draws them with the real 3a card blocks). Background: the 3a spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-11-broadcasts-design.md` and plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-broadcasts.md`.

## Global Constraints

- **No links:** "No post contains a link." Names that read as domains go through `noLinks` (U+2024 for the dots, a zero-width space after `@` and after the colon of `://`), on X, on Telegram and in poll options.
- **X length:** "X counted the way X counts": at most 280 by `xLength` (twitter-text v3 weights). **Telegram captions:** at most 1,024, measured on the raw HTML.
- **Handles:** "@handles only when verified, plain names otherwise." "At most 5 @handles per post. Past 5, players are written as plain names, in the order they appear."
- **Closing tags:** "`@chainlink` goes on welcome and countdown posts; `#Chainlink $LINK` on movers and spotlight; New on CCIP carries `#CCIP` only."
- **Drop order:** "the climbers or partners line first, then the closing hashtag line."
- **Budget:** "At most **2 moment posts a day**. 3a's posts and the poll don't count". "A moment that misses its day is still planned on each of the next 2 days, then dropped."
- **Dedup keys, verbatim:** `broadcast:welcome:<selector>`, `broadcast:countdown:<metric>:<threshold>`, `broadcast:poll:<monday>`, `broadcast:movers:<monday>`, `broadcast:spotlight:<monday>`, `broadcast:roundup:<monday>`, "claimed and marked exactly as 3a's (atomic claim, per-output keys `<key>#<output>`, release on failure)". First run: `meta.broadcast:moments:seeded`.
- **Mode:** "`MOMENTS` (a Worker var) is `off`, `drafts` or `on`". "It ships as `on`". "`off` in either means nothing; `drafts` in either means drafts."
- **Poll:** "Telegram-only: there is no X draft for it." "a question is at most 300 characters and an option at most 100. Names are shortened to fit, with "…"." `poll:<monday>` "holds `{ chat_id, message_id, question_kind, options }`".
- **Cards:** routes `/og/broadcast/welcome/<selector>.png`, `/og/broadcast/countdown/<metric>/<threshold>.png`, `/og/broadcast/poll/<monday>.png`, `/og/broadcast/movers/<monday>.png`, `/og/broadcast/spotlight/<monday>.png`, `/og/broadcast/roundup/<monday>.png`, "at 1200×675, with 3a's rules: no-store data loads, 404 when the data doesn't hold the moment, 500 with no-store on a failure, never the fallback card." "Every ▲ ▼ ★ → is drawn as a glyph, as in 3a."
- **Alerts, verbatim:** `moments-publish`, `broadcast-telegram-poll:<key>`, `poll-close:<monday>`.
- **No new dependencies; free data only:** every figure comes from D1 and the public files the Worker already writes. The Workers Paid plan is already in place.
- **`.backfill/` is the owner's.** Never write to it.
- **Remote operations are owner steps.** Never run a `wrangler … --remote` command. A push to `main` deploys.
- **Commits:** conventional subjects (`feat(core): …`, `feat(worker): …`, `feat(site): …`, `docs: …`). Stage explicit paths only; never `git add -A`, `git add .` or `git commit -a`. End each message with a `Co-Authored-By:` trailer naming the model you are (the commit commands below show it as `<your model>`).
- **Tests:**
  - Core: `pnpm --filter @ccip-dev/core test`.
  - Worker: `pnpm --filter @ccip-dev/worker test` (real D1 through `@cloudflare/vitest-plugin`).
  - Site: `pnpm --filter @ccip-dev/site test` (both the node and the workerd configs).
  - Types: `pnpm typecheck`.
  - New tests use the BDD comments `// #given`, `// #when` and `// #then`.
- **Comments:** only where the why is not obvious from the code.

## Review Focus

1. **The budget across the hourly retry.** At 00:07 one moment's channel post fails and is released while another went out; the hourly retry re-plans the same set. A reader would expect the failed one to post within the hour and no third moment that day; a naive "first 2 by priority" would re-pick the sent one and starve the failed one, and a count that included `#output` rows or seeded keys would block it.
   - Test: Task 6 "gives the hourly retry only the place a failed moment left, never a third moment"; Task 6 "counts today's sent budgeted moment keys, not their output rows, the poll, a seeded key or another day's".
2. **A week with no data on a board.** When the week before has no fee data (or no rows at all for a board), every entry looks new: movers would announce a false "New #1 … taking the top spot from" nobody, and the poll's fee-chain question would have no options.
   - Tests: Task 1 "names no new #1 and no climber on a board that was empty the week before"; Task 1 "asks the next kind when last week has no data for the turn, and nothing when it has none at all"; Task 2 "is published with the history files, as of yesterday" (no fee data, so this week's poll falls through to the lane question).
3. **A Monday where the weekly fails but the poll is due.** The poll "always posts on Monday" and is "never deferred"; a failed weekly card must not hold it back, and the poll itself must not wait on anything but its own card.
   - Tests: Task 7 "posts when the weekly report's card fails"; Task 7 "holds the poll back while its card has not posted, and sends both on the next run".
4. **A poll whose message was deleted.** `stopPoll` answers 400. The result must still post on Monday, with the answer and without "N% of you called it", and the finalize must not fail.
   - Tests: Task 3 "reports a poll whose message was deleted, and an answer without counts"; Task 7 "alerts and keeps no counts when the poll cannot be closed, as when its message was deleted"; Task 7 "never throws, even when D1 and the alerter both fail"; Task 5 "leaves out "called it" for a poll that could not be closed"; Task 10 "shows the answer without shares or "called it" when last week's poll could not be closed".
5. **A token symbol with `@` or a dot, or a long name, in a moment.** New on CCIP and movers write token symbols and lane names as plain text, and the poll sends names as option text: `AIRDROP@scam`, `pump.fun` or `claim.io` must neither mention nor link, and a lane between two long chain names must fit Telegram's 100-character option.
   - Tests: Task 5 "never turns a token symbol with an @ or a domain-like name into a mention or a link, on X or Telegram"; Task 7 "follows the card in its own chat, sends the poll with no link in it, and keeps its message id for next Monday"; Task 1 "shortens an option past 100 characters with "…"".

Also checked and pinned, but less likely to bite: a chain that arrives and is spotlighted on its partial first week (Task 1 "leaves out a chain whose first message came after the week before began"); the 4-week skip across the published window (Task 1 `spotlightPicks`, Task 2 "skips the chain spotlighted the week before"); D1 scans (Task 2 and Task 6 query-plan tests).

## Decisions the spec leaves open or gets wrong

- **Rulings from the owner's brief (2026-10-12):**
  - **Poll-close ordering.** `moments.json` is published inside `publishHistoryFiles`, before the broadcasts, and Monday's card needs last week's votes. So `closeLastWeekPoll` runs in the finalize just before `publishHistoryFiles`: early run only, Monday only, only when `BROADCASTS` and `MOMENTS` allow posting, with its own isolation and its own `poll-close:<monday>` alert. It saves the counts into the `poll:<monday>` row. The hourly retry never closes polls.
  - **Telegram API**, checked on core.telegram.org/bots/api (Bot API 10.3): `sendPoll` with `chat_id`, `question`, `options` as `InputPollOption` `{ text }` and `is_anonymous`; `stopPoll` with `chat_id` and `message_id`, returning the Poll with `options[].voter_count` and `total_voter_count`. JSON bodies, the same 30 s timeout and 429 retry as `sendTelegramPhoto`.
  - **The budget count** is one query: the five budgeted kinds' keys, without `#` rows, whose value is `sent:` and today's date. The planner considers moments due today, yesterday and the day before, then applies the cap and the order.
  - **Planning** happens inside the same finalize broadcast request as 3a, after the 3a posts, from a facts source that reads the moments from D1 through the builder that writes `moments.json`. The hourly retry re-plans the same set.
  - **The poll** is a new output (`telegram-poll`) after the card's photo output, to the same chat, in the same mode. `MOMENTS` ships as `"on"`.
- **`moments.json` carries more than §5.2 lists**, so the cards and captions stay re-renderable from it alone:
  - `arrivals.chains[]` adds `messages` and `usd`, the first-day totals the welcome card and post show.
  - `arrivals.tokens[]` adds `usd` (first-day value), to pick the 5 New on CCIP names.
  - `countdowns[]` adds `previous: { threshold, day } | null`, the last milestone for the progress bar and the strip.
  - `polls[]` adds `outcome: { key, label, value, change_pct }`: the week's actual leader or fee change, which the result line needs even when the answer is "Another chain".
  - The countdown card draws the day before `updated_at` (the last finalized day).
- **Previous ranks** are within the week before's top 10 (null outside it, as §5.2 says). So an entry from outside reads "new at #9" rather than the example's "▲14 to #9", always counts as a climber (it "entered the top 10 from outside it"), and sorts as having climbed from #11.
- **A board empty in either week is not compared**, so a week without fee data names no false new #1 (Review Focus 2).
- **The dethroned #1** is the week before's #1 on the same board, from `weeks[]`; the movers card and post for the oldest published week have no week before and are not planned.
- **Fee figures:** a chain's fees are what it paid as a source chain (the fee board's figure); its messages and value count both directions (spec §5.1).
- **The spotlight** also leaves out a chain whose first message came after the Monday of the week before: its partial first week would inflate its rise. "Spotlighted in the 4 weeks before" counts picks from `SPOTLIGHT_SINCE = 2026-10-05` (the first week a spotlight can post) on, computed in week order, so a pick never changes as the published window slides.
- **Countdowns** need 7 finalized days for a pace, leave fees out until fee data reaches back to the first message (as the Records page does), and count a total sitting exactly on a threshold as past it.
- **New on CCIP:** a chain counts as "welcomed this week" when its first day is from the Sunday before through Friday (its welcome falls in the week). Lanes are ordered by first-day value; tokens too.
- **The poll:**
  - ISO week mod 3 picks `fee_chain` (0), `fees_change` (1) or `lane` (2); a kind last week's data cannot ask (no fee chains, no fee total, no lanes) falls through to the next.
  - "Within 10%" includes exactly ±10%.
  - The fee question uses `formatFeeTotal` ("$8.0K", and "$0.40" under a dollar).
  - A sent poll is described by its record (what was asked), not recomputed; `polls[]` lists this week's poll and each earlier one with a record. The first poll is the one whose week before has no record.
  - The spec's "Telegram polls close on their own after at most 10 minutes" is out of date (`open_period` now goes to 2,628,000 s); the design is unchanged: no close time, closed by `stopPoll` a week later.
  - A poll that cannot be closed alerts once and is not retried; the hourly and 06:00 runs never close a poll.
  - Seeding never covers the poll: it posts on the first Monday.
- **Copy:**
  - The welcome reads "first message on Oct 11, with @ethereum": the first partner is by value, and its direction is not known.
  - Every climber names its board ("by fees", "by value").
  - Lanes (movers, New on CCIP) and token symbols are written as plain names, as in the approved examples; a token's project is tagged in New on CCIP.
  - The 5-handle cap counts a closing @chainlink and applies to moment posts; a repeated handle is free.
  - New on CCIP shortens its lists ("+N more") before it drops its network line; the network line is its closing line.
  - Figures use the site's formatters, so "$2.43M now" prints "$2.4M now".
  - The poll's X text is empty, since it has no X draft.
- **Isolation:** each moment's delivery is wrapped, so a thrown error alerts `broadcast-run` and the next moment still goes; `moments.json` is published in its own try/catch (`moments-publish`).
- **Cost:** each finalize and each hourly retry rebuilds the moments from D1: one grouped scan each of the lane and token rows of `daily_breakdown` (under a million rows read), about 30 `topBetween` queries and a weekly chain grouping. That is far inside the Paid plan's 25 billion rows a month.
- **Docs** are their own task (Task 11), as the brief asks, since every alert and key is known by then.

### Task 1: Core moments logic, keys and the `moments.json` schema

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/core/src/moments.ts`
- Modify: `packages/core/src/public.ts` (`MomentsFileSchema` and its parts, `POLL_KINDS`, `PUBLIC_SCHEMAS`)
- Modify: `packages/core/src/broadcasts.ts` (six moment targets, keys, card paths and parsing; `BUDGETED_MOMENTS`)
- Modify: `packages/core/package.json` (the `./moments` export)
- Modify: `site/worker/cards/broadcast-cards.ts` (the six cases in `broadcastTree`, answering null for now)
- Test: `packages/core/test/moments.test.ts`, `packages/core/test/broadcasts.test.ts`, `packages/core/test/public.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (live code): `formatFeeTotal` (`format.ts`); `messageThresholds`, `valueThresholds`, `feeThresholds`, `feeCoverageComplete`, `trackedMilestones`, `MILESTONE_METRICS`, `type MilestoneMetric` (`records.ts`); `addDays`, `dayStartIso`, `isDay`, `weekStart` (`time.ts`); zod.
- Produces:
  - `@ccip-dev/core/public`: `POLL_KINDS = ['fee_chain', 'fees_change', 'lane']`, `type PollKind`; `BoardEntrySchema`, `SpotlightSchema`, `MomentsWeekSchema`, `PollSchema`, `MomentsFileSchema`; `PUBLIC_SCHEMAS['moments.json']`; types `BoardEntry { key; label: string | null; value; rank; previous_rank: number | null }`, `Spotlight`, `MomentsWeek { week; boards: { fee_chains; tokens; lanes: BoardEntry[] }; spotlight: Spotlight | null }`, `Poll { week; question_kind; question; options: { key; label }[]; answer: string | null; outcome: { key; label; value; change_pct: number | null } | null; votes: number[] | null; total_votes: number | null }`, `MomentsFile`.
  - `@ccip-dev/core/broadcasts`: `BroadcastTarget` gains `{ kind: 'welcome'; selector }`, `{ kind: 'countdown'; metric: MilestoneMetric; threshold }`, and `{ kind: 'poll' | 'movers' | 'spotlight' | 'roundup'; monday }`; `BUDGETED_MOMENTS = ['welcome', 'countdown', 'movers', 'spotlight', 'roundup'] as const`, `type BudgetedMoment`, `type MomentTarget`; `broadcastKey`, `broadcastCardPath` and `parseBroadcastPath` handle them.
  - `@ccip-dev/core/moments`:
    - types `MomentsBody` (the file without its envelope), `ChainArrival`, `LaneArrival`, `TokenArrival`, `Countdown`;
    - `isoWeek(day): number`, `clip(text, max): string`;
    - `BOARDS`, `type BoardName = 'fee_chains' | 'tokens' | 'lanes'`, `BOARD_SIZE = 10`, `CLIMB_MIN = 3`, `MAX_CLIMBERS = 3`, `rankBoard(current: { key; label; value }[], previous: { key }[]): BoardEntry[]`;
    - `interface Climber { board: BoardName; entry: BoardEntry; gain: number | null }`, `type MoversHeadline = { kind: 'leader'; board; entry; dethroned: BoardEntry | null } | { kind: 'climber'; climber: Climber }`, `interface Movers { monday; headline; climbers: Climber[] }`, `moversOf(weeks: readonly MomentsWeek[], monday): Movers | null`;
    - `SPOTLIGHT_SINCE = '2026-10-05'`, `SPOTLIGHT_MIN_MESSAGES = 100`, `SPOTLIGHT_MIN_PREVIOUS = 50`, `SPOTLIGHT_SKIP_WEEKS = 4`, `interface ChainCount { selector; messages; previous; firstDay }`, `interface SpotlightWeek { monday; chains: readonly ChainCount[] }`, `spotlightPick(week, recent: ReadonlySet<string>): string | null`, `spotlightPicks(weeks): Map<string, string | null>`;
    - `COUNTDOWN_PACE_DAYS = 7`, `countdownsOf(days: readonly DayTotals[]): Countdown[]`;
    - `ROUNDUP_DAYS = 5`, `roundupOf(m: Pick<MomentsBody, 'arrivals'>, monday): { lanes: LaneArrival[]; tokens: TokenArrival[] } | null`;
    - `POLL_QUESTION_MAX = 300`, `POLL_OPTION_MAX = 100`, `POLL_CHOICES = 3`, `FEES_BAND_PCT = 10`, `OTHER = 'other'`, `interface PollChoice { key; label }`, `interface PollData { feeChains; lanes: readonly PollChoice[]; feeTotal: number | null }`, `type PollQuestion`, `pollKindFor(monday): PollKind`, `pollQuestion(monday, data: PollData): PollQuestion | null`, `interface PollLeader { key; label; value }`, `interface PollResult { feeChain; lane: PollLeader | null; feeTotal; previousFeeTotal: number | null }`, `pollAnswer(poll, result): Pick<Poll, 'answer' | 'outcome'>`, `calledShare(poll): number | null`, `voteShares(voters: readonly number[]): number[]`;
    - `PollRecordSchema`, `type PollRecord { chat_id; message_id; question_kind; question; options; voters?: number[]; total_voters?: number }`, `pollRecordKey(monday) = 'poll:<monday>'`, `parsePollRecord(raw: string | null): PollRecord | null`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

Create `packages/core/test/moments.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  calledShare, clip, countdownsOf, isoWeek, moversOf, parsePollRecord, pollAnswer, pollKindFor, pollQuestion, pollRecordKey, rankBoard, roundupOf, spotlightPick,
  spotlightPicks, voteShares, type ChainCount, type MomentsBody, type PollData,
} from '../src/moments';
import type { BoardEntry, DayTotals, MomentsWeek } from '../src/public';

const totals = (day: string, messages: number, usd: number, fee: number | null): DayTotals => ({
  day, messages, token_messages: 0, usd_value: usd, fee_usd: fee, unique_senders: 1, median_delivery_s: null, unpriced_messages: 0, fee_link_usd: null,
});
const LAST_WEEK = ['2026-10-05', '2026-10-06', '2026-10-07', '2026-10-08', '2026-10-09', '2026-10-10', '2026-10-11'];

describe('isoWeek', () => {
  it.each([['2026-10-12', 42], ['2026-01-01', 1], ['2027-01-01', 53], ['2024-12-30', 1], ['2026-12-31', 53]])('puts %s in ISO week %i', (day, week) => {
    expect(isoWeek(day)).toBe(week);
  });
});

describe('clip', () => {
  it('keeps a text that fits and shortens a long one to the limit, ending with "…"', () => {
    // #when
    const long = clip(`${'Ethereum Mainnet Optimism Superchain '.repeat(4)}→ Base`, 100);
    // #then
    expect({ short: clip('Ethereum → Base', 100), fits: long.length <= 100, end: long.endsWith('…'), emoji: clip('🎉🎉🎉', 5) }).toEqual({
      short: 'Ethereum → Base', fits: true, end: true, emoji: '🎉🎉…',
    });
  });
});

describe('rankBoard', () => {
  it("ranks this week's top 10 and gives each its rank the week before, or null outside last week's top 10", () => {
    // #given c was #11 last week
    const previous = 'b a d e f g h i j k c'.split(' ').map((key) => ({ key }));
    const current = 'a b c'.split(' ').map((key, i) => ({ key, label: null, value: 30 - i }));
    // #when
    const board = rankBoard(current, previous);
    // #then
    expect(board.map((e) => [e.key, e.rank, e.previous_rank])).toEqual([['a', 1, 2], ['b', 2, 1], ['c', 3, null]]);
  });
});

const entry = (key: string, rank: number, previous: number | null, value = 100 - rank): BoardEntry => ({ key, label: null, value, rank, previous_rank: previous });
const week = (monday: string, boards: Partial<MomentsWeek['boards']>): MomentsWeek => ({
  week: monday,
  boards: { fee_chains: [entry('x', 1, 1)], tokens: [entry('y', 1, 1)], lanes: [entry('z', 1, 1)], ...boards },
  spotlight: null,
});
const BEFORE = week('2026-09-28', { fee_chains: [entry('bnb', 1, 1), entry('base', 2, 2)] });

describe('moversOf', () => {
  it('leads with a new #1 by fees and the chain it took the top spot from, then up to 3 climbers by places gained', () => {
    // #given Base rose from #2 to #1 by fees, Arbitrum from #9 to #3, and a token and a lane entered the top 10
    const after = week('2026-10-05', {
      fee_chains: [entry('base', 1, 2), entry('bnb', 2, 1), entry('arb', 3, 9), entry('eth', 4, 5)],
      tokens: [entry('usdc', 1, 1), entry('syrup', 7, null)],
      lanes: [entry('eth>arc', 1, 1), entry('ink>eth', 9, null)],
    });
    // #when
    const movers = moversOf([BEFORE, after], '2026-10-05');
    // #then the entrants climbed at least from #11: syrup 4 places, the lane 2
    expect({
      headline: movers?.headline.kind === 'leader' ? [movers.headline.board, movers.headline.entry.key, movers.headline.dethroned?.key] : null,
      climbers: movers?.climbers.map((c) => [c.board, c.entry.key, c.gain]),
    }).toEqual({
      headline: ['fee_chains', 'base', 'bnb'],
      climbers: [['fee_chains', 'arb', 6], ['tokens', 'syrup', null], ['lanes', 'ink>eth', null]],
    });
  });

  it('takes a new #1 on tokens when the fee board kept its leader, and leads with the top climber when no board has a new #1', () => {
    // #given
    const newToken = week('2026-10-05', { fee_chains: [entry('bnb', 1, 1)], tokens: [entry('syrup', 1, 4)] });
    const noLeader = week('2026-10-05', { fee_chains: [entry('bnb', 1, 1), entry('arb', 3, 8), entry('op', 5, 9)] });
    // #when
    const token = moversOf([BEFORE, newToken], '2026-10-05');
    const climber = moversOf([BEFORE, noLeader], '2026-10-05');
    // #then
    expect({
      token: token?.headline.kind === 'leader' ? [token.headline.board, token.headline.entry.key, token.headline.dethroned?.key] : null,
      climber: climber?.headline.kind === 'climber' ? [climber.headline.climber.entry.key, climber.headline.climber.gain] : null,
      rest: climber?.climbers.map((c) => c.entry.key),
    }).toEqual({ token: ['tokens', 'syrup', 'y'], climber: ['arb', 5], rest: ['op'] });
  });

  it('has no movers when no board has a new #1 and nothing climbed 3 places or entered the top 10', () => {
    // #given Arbitrum rose 2 places only
    const quiet = week('2026-10-05', { fee_chains: [entry('bnb', 1, 1), entry('arb', 3, 5)] });
    // #then
    expect(moversOf([BEFORE, quiet], '2026-10-05')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('names no new #1 and no climber on a board that was empty the week before', () => {
    // #given no fee data the week before, so every fee entry looks new
    const before = week('2026-09-28', { fee_chains: [] });
    const after = week('2026-10-05', { fee_chains: [entry('base', 1, null), entry('bnb', 2, null)] });
    // #then
    expect(moversOf([before, after], '2026-10-05')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('has no movers for a week whose week before is not in the file', () => {
    expect(moversOf([week('2026-10-05', { fee_chains: [entry('base', 1, 2)] })], '2026-10-05')).toBeNull();
  });
});

const chain = (selector: string, messages: number, previous: number, firstDay = '2024-01-01'): ChainCount => ({ selector, messages, previous, firstDay });

describe('spotlightPick', () => {
  it('picks the largest rise among chains with 100 messages this week and 50 the week before', () => {
    // #given C and D miss a floor, F fell, and A and B rose by the same 200%
    const chains = [chain('B', 150, 50), chain('A', 300, 100), chain('C', 99, 10), chain('D', 1_000, 40), chain('F', 120, 200)];
    // #when
    const pick = spotlightPick({ monday: '2026-10-05', chains }, new Set());
    // #then the tie goes to more messages
    expect(pick).toBe('A');
  });

  it('breaks a tie on rise and messages by selector, and skips the chains it is told to', () => {
    const chains = [chain('B', 300, 100), chain('A', 300, 100), chain('C', 200, 100)];
    expect([spotlightPick({ monday: '2026-10-05', chains }, new Set()), spotlightPick({ monday: '2026-10-05', chains }, new Set(['A']))]).toEqual(['A', 'B']);
  });

  it('leaves out a chain whose first message came after the week before began, whose partial week inflates its rise', () => {
    // #given E's first message was on Wednesday of the week before
    const chains = [chain('A', 300, 100), chain('E', 2_000, 60, '2026-09-30'), chain('G', 2_000, 60, '2026-09-28')];
    // #when
    const pick = spotlightPick({ monday: '2026-10-05', chains }, new Set());
    // #then G arrived on that week's Monday, so its week before was whole
    expect(pick).toBe('G');
  });

  it('has no pick when no chain rose', () => {
    expect(spotlightPick({ monday: '2026-10-05', chains: [chain('A', 100, 100), chain('B', 100, 150)] }, new Set())).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('spotlightPicks', () => {
  it('skips a chain spotlighted in the 4 weeks before, counting only picks from 2026-10-05 on', () => {
    // #given the same three growers every week, A fastest, then B, then C
    const mondays = ['2026-09-21', '2026-09-28', '2026-10-05', '2026-10-12', '2026-10-19', '2026-10-26', '2026-11-02', '2026-11-09'];
    const weeks = mondays.map((monday) => ({ monday, chains: [chain('A', 400, 100), chain('B', 300, 100), chain('C', 150, 100)] }));
    // #when
    const picks = spotlightPicks(weeks);
    // #then the picks before Oct 5 were never posted, so A is still picked on Oct 5
    expect([...picks]).toEqual([
      ['2026-09-21', 'A'], ['2026-09-28', 'A'], ['2026-10-05', 'A'], ['2026-10-12', 'B'], ['2026-10-19', 'C'], ['2026-10-26', null], ['2026-11-02', null], ['2026-11-09', 'A'],
    ]);
  });
});

describe('countdownsOf', () => {
  const FIRST = '2023-07-06';
  const days = (firstFee: number | null) => [totals(FIRST, 9_000, 2.3e9, firstFee), ...LAST_WEEK.map((d) => totals(d, 100, 1e7, 10_000))];

  it("gives each metric's next threshold, its total, the last 7 days' pace, the days left rounded up and the last milestone", () => {
    // #when
    const countdowns = countdownsOf(days(2_400_000));
    // #then
    expect(countdowns).toEqual([
      { metric: 'messages', threshold: 10_000, total: 9_700, per_day: 100, days_left: 3, previous: { threshold: 5_000, day: FIRST } },
      { metric: 'value', threshold: 2.5e9, total: 2.37e9, per_day: 1e7, days_left: 13, previous: { threshold: 1e9, day: FIRST } },
      { metric: 'fees', threshold: 2.5e6, total: 2.47e6, per_day: 10_000, days_left: 3, previous: { threshold: 1e6, day: FIRST } },
    ]);
  });

  it('counts a total sitting exactly on a threshold as past it', () => {
    // #given 9,300 + 700 = 10,000 messages
    const exact = [totals(FIRST, 9_300, 2.3e9, 2_400_000), ...LAST_WEEK.map((d) => totals(d, 100, 1e7, 10_000))];
    // #then
    expect(countdownsOf(exact)[0]).toMatchObject({ metric: 'messages', threshold: 20_000, days_left: 100 });
  });

  it('leaves fees out while fee data does not reach back to the first message, and gives nothing before 7 finalized days', () => {
    expect({ fees: countdownsOf(days(null)).map((c) => c.metric), short: countdownsOf(days(2_400_000).slice(0, 6)) }).toEqual({ fees: ['messages', 'value'], short: [] });
  });
});

describe('roundupOf', () => {
  const arrivals = (o: Partial<MomentsBody['arrivals']>): Pick<MomentsBody, 'arrivals'> => ({ arrivals: { chains: [], lanes: [], tokens: [], ...o } });

  it('lists the lanes and tokens that arrived Monday to Friday by first-day value, leaving out the lanes of chains welcomed that week', () => {
    // #given T and S first sent on Sunday and Wednesday, so their welcomes fall in the week; a lane on Saturday is next week's
    const m = arrivals({
      chains: [{ selector: 'T', day: '2026-10-04', number: 92, partners: ['1'], messages: 1, usd: 1 }, { selector: 'S', day: '2026-10-07', number: 93, partners: ['1'], messages: 1, usd: 1 }],
      lanes: [
        { key: '1>2', day: '2026-10-05', messages: 3, usd: 10 },
        { key: '2>3', day: '2026-10-09', messages: 3, usd: 900 },
        { key: '1>T', day: '2026-10-06', messages: 3, usd: 5_000 },
        { key: 'S>1', day: '2026-10-07', messages: 3, usd: 5_000 },
        { key: '3>1', day: '2026-10-10', messages: 3, usd: 9_000 },
      ],
      tokens: [{ key: 'g1', symbol: 'USDat', day: '2026-10-08', chains: ['1'], usd: 50 }, { key: '1:0xa', symbol: 'PST', day: '2026-10-05', chains: ['1'], usd: 70 }],
    });
    // #when
    const roundup = roundupOf(m, '2026-10-05');
    // #then
    expect({ lanes: roundup?.lanes.map((l) => l.key), tokens: roundup?.tokens.map((t) => t.symbol) }).toEqual({ lanes: ['2>3', '1>2'], tokens: ['PST', 'USDat'] });
  });

  it('has no roundup for a week with nothing new', () => {
    expect(roundupOf(arrivals({ lanes: [{ key: '1>2', day: '2026-10-03', messages: 1, usd: 1 }] }), '2026-10-05')).toBeNull();
  });
});

const DATA: PollData = {
  feeChains: [{ key: 'base', label: 'Base' }, { key: 'bnb', label: 'BNB Chain' }, { key: 'eth', label: 'Ethereum' }, { key: 'arb', label: 'Arbitrum' }],
  lanes: [{ key: 'eth>arc', label: 'Ethereum → Arc' }],
  feeTotal: 7_970,
};

describe('pollQuestion', () => {
  it('turns the question by ISO week: fee chains, then the fee total, then lanes', () => {
    // #when weeks 42, 43 and 44
    const polls = ['2026-10-12', '2026-10-19', '2026-10-26'].map((monday) => pollQuestion(monday, DATA));
    // #then
    expect(polls.map((p) => [p?.question_kind, p?.question, p?.options.map((o) => o.label)])).toEqual([
      ['fee_chain', 'Which chain will pay the most CCIP fees this week?', ['Base', 'BNB Chain', 'Ethereum', 'Another chain']],
      ['fees_change', "Will CCIP fees beat last week's $8.0K?", ['Up more than 10%', 'Within 10%', 'Down more than 10%']],
      ['lane', 'Which lane will move the most value this week?', ['Ethereum → Arc', 'Another lane']],
    ]);
    expect([pollKindFor('2026-10-12'), polls[0]?.options.map((o) => o.key)]).toEqual(['fee_chain', ['base', 'bnb', 'eth', 'other']]);
  });

  it('asks the next kind when last week has no data for the turn, and nothing when it has none at all', () => {
    // #given no fee chains last week, and then no data at all
    const noFees = { ...DATA, feeChains: [] };
    // #then
    expect({ next: pollQuestion('2026-10-12', noFees)?.question_kind, none: pollQuestion('2026-10-12', { feeChains: [], lanes: [], feeTotal: null }) }).toEqual({ next: 'fees_change', none: null });
  });

  it('shortens an option past 100 characters with "…"', () => {
    // #given a lane between two chains with very long names
    const label = `${'Ethereum Mainnet Optimism Superchain Rollup '.repeat(2)}→ ${'Arbitrum Nova Orbit Gaming Network '.repeat(2)}`;
    // #when
    const poll = pollQuestion('2026-10-26', { ...DATA, lanes: [{ key: 'a>b', label }] });
    // #then
    const option = poll!.options[0]!.label;
    expect({ fits: option.length <= 100, cut: option.length > 90, end: option.endsWith('…') }).toEqual({ fits: true, cut: true, end: true });
  });
});

describe('pollAnswer', () => {
  const feeChain = pollQuestion('2026-10-12', DATA)!;
  const feesChange = pollQuestion('2026-10-19', DATA)!;
  const result = (o: Partial<Parameters<typeof pollAnswer>[1]>) => ({ feeChain: null, lane: null, feeTotal: null, previousFeeTotal: null, ...o });

  it("answers with the leader's option, or with the other option when the leader was not listed", () => {
    expect([
      pollAnswer(feeChain, result({ feeChain: { key: 'base', label: 'Base', value: 1_800 } })),
      pollAnswer(feeChain, result({ feeChain: { key: 'celo', label: 'Celo', value: 2_100 } })),
    ]).toEqual([
      { answer: 'base', outcome: { key: 'base', label: 'Base', value: 1_800, change_pct: null } },
      { answer: 'other', outcome: { key: 'celo', label: 'Celo', value: 2_100, change_pct: null } },
    ]);
  });

  it('answers the fee question by the change against the week before, with exactly 10% counted as within', () => {
    // #when
    const answers = [8_800, 9_000, 7_000].map((feeTotal) => pollAnswer(feesChange, result({ feeTotal, previousFeeTotal: 8_000 })));
    // #then
    expect(answers.map((a) => [a.answer, a.outcome?.change_pct])).toEqual([['within', 10], ['up', 12.5], ['down', -12.5]]);
  });

  it('has no answer without the figures', () => {
    expect([pollAnswer(feeChain, result({})), pollAnswer(feesChange, result({ feeTotal: 10, previousFeeTotal: 0 }))]).toEqual([
      { answer: null, outcome: null }, { answer: null, outcome: null },
    ]);
  });
});

describe('calledShare', () => {
  const poll = { options: [{ key: 'a', label: 'A' }, { key: 'other', label: 'Another' }], answer: 'other', votes: [70, 30], total_votes: 10 };

  it("gives the answer's share of the votes, and nothing before the answer, without votes or with no voter", () => {
    expect([calledShare(poll), calledShare({ ...poll, answer: null }), calledShare({ ...poll, votes: null, total_votes: null }), calledShare({ ...poll, votes: [0, 0], total_votes: 0 })]).toEqual([30, null, null, null]);
  });
});

describe('voteShares', () => {
  it('gives each option its share of the votes in whole percent, and zeros without votes', () => {
    expect([voteShares([38, 31, 22, 9]), voteShares([1, 2]), voteShares([0, 0])]).toEqual([[38, 31, 22, 9], [33, 67], [0, 0]]);
  });
});

describe('parsePollRecord', () => {
  it('reads a sent poll\'s record, and reads a missing, broken or foreign row as no poll', () => {
    // #given
    const record = { chat_id: '@ccipdev', message_id: 7, question_kind: 'lane', question: 'Which lane will move the most value this week?', options: [{ key: 'other', label: 'Another lane' }] };
    // #then
    expect([
      parsePollRecord(JSON.stringify(record)), parsePollRecord(null), parsePollRecord('{'), parsePollRecord(JSON.stringify({ ...record, message_id: 'x' })), pollRecordKey('2026-10-05'),
    ]).toEqual([record, null, null, null, 'poll:2026-10-05']);
  });
});
```

Before `export const ChainSchema`, add the moments schema:

```ts
export const POLL_KINDS = ['fee_chain', 'fees_change', 'lane'] as const;
export type PollKind = (typeof POLL_KINDS)[number];

export const BoardEntrySchema = z.object({
  key: z.string(),
  label: z.string().nullable(),
  value: z.number(),
  rank: z.number(),
  previous_rank: z.number().nullable(),
});

export const SpotlightSchema = z.object({
  selector: z.string(),
  rank: z.number(),
  messages: z.number(),
  previous_messages: z.number(),
  usd: z.number(),
  previous_usd: z.number(),
  fee_usd: z.number().nullable(),
  previous_fee_usd: z.number().nullable(),
  partners: z.array(z.string()),
  token: z.object({ key: z.string(), label: z.string().nullable(), usd: z.number() }).nullable(),
});

export const MomentsWeekSchema = z.object({
  week: z.string(),
  boards: z.object({ fee_chains: z.array(BoardEntrySchema), tokens: z.array(BoardEntrySchema), lanes: z.array(BoardEntrySchema) }),
  spotlight: SpotlightSchema.nullable(),
});

const PollOptionSchema = z.object({ key: z.string(), label: z.string() });

export const PollSchema = z.object({
  week: z.string(),
  question_kind: z.enum(POLL_KINDS),
  question: z.string(),
  options: z.array(PollOptionSchema),
  answer: z.string().nullable(),
  outcome: z.object({ key: z.string(), label: z.string(), value: z.number(), change_pct: z.number().nullable() }).nullable(),
  votes: z.array(z.number()).nullable(),
  total_votes: z.number().nullable(),
});

export const MomentsFileSchema = z.object({
  ...envelope,
  network: z.object({ chains: z.number(), lanes: z.number() }),
  arrivals: z.object({
    chains: z.array(z.object({ selector: z.string(), day: z.string(), number: z.number(), partners: z.array(z.string()), messages: z.number(), usd: z.number() })),
    lanes: z.array(z.object({ key: z.string(), day: z.string(), messages: z.number(), usd: z.number() })),
    tokens: z.array(z.object({ key: z.string(), symbol: z.string().nullable(), day: z.string(), chains: z.array(z.string()), usd: z.number() })),
  }),
  weeks: z.array(MomentsWeekSchema),
  countdowns: z.array(
    z.object({
      metric: z.enum(MILESTONE_METRICS),
      threshold: z.number(),
      total: z.number(),
      per_day: z.number(),
      days_left: z.number(),
      previous: z.object({ threshold: z.number(), day: z.string() }).nullable(),
    }),
  ),
  polls: z.array(PollSchema),
});
```

```ts
  if (kind === 'reserve' && b === undefined && TX_RE.test(a)) return { kind: 'reserve', tx: a };
  if (kind === 'welcome' && b === undefined && SELECTOR_RE.test(a)) return { kind: 'welcome', selector: a };
  if (kind === 'countdown' && oneOf(MILESTONE_METRICS, a) && b !== undefined && THRESHOLD_RE.test(b)) return { kind: 'countdown', metric: a, threshold: Number(b) };
  if (oneOf(WEEKLY_MOMENTS, kind) && b === undefined && isDay(a) && weekStart(a) === a) return { kind, monday: a };
  return null;
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the moments logic.**

It is pure, so the Worker builder, the planner and the card Worker share it. Notes on the rules it encodes:
  - A board empty in either week is not compared, so a week without fee data claims no "new #1" (Review Focus 2).
  - An entry from outside last week's top 10 has `previous_rank` null; it always counts as a climber and sorts as having climbed from #11.
  - The spotlight leaves out a chain whose first message came after the week before began (Review Focus: a chain that arrives and is spotlighted).
  - A spotlight pick only makes a chain skip the next 4 weeks from `SPOTLIGHT_SINCE` (the first postable week) on, so the picks are a pure function of the data and never shift as the published window slides.
  - A poll kind that last week's data cannot ask falls through to the next kind (Review Focus 2).

Create `packages/core/src/moments.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { formatFeeTotal } from './format';
import { POLL_KINDS, type BoardEntry, type DayTotals, type MomentsFile, type MomentsWeek, type Poll, type PollKind } from './public';
import { feeCoverageComplete, feeThresholds, messageThresholds, MILESTONE_METRICS, trackedMilestones, valueThresholds, type MilestoneMetric } from './records';
import { addDays, dayStartIso } from './time';

export type MomentsBody = Omit<MomentsFile, 'schema_version' | 'updated_at' | 'attribution'>;
export type ChainArrival = MomentsFile['arrivals']['chains'][number];
export type LaneArrival = MomentsFile['arrivals']['lanes'][number];
export type TokenArrival = MomentsFile['arrivals']['tokens'][number];
export type Countdown = MomentsFile['countdowns'][number];

const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
const compare = (a: string, b: string) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0);
const cents = (v: number) => Math.round(v * 100) / 100;

/** The ISO 8601 week number: the week holding the year's first Thursday is week 1. */
export function isoWeek(day: string): number {
  const date = Date.parse(dayStartIso(day));
  const thursday = new Date(date + (3 - ((new Date(date).getUTCDay() + 6) % 7)) * DAY_MS);
  const yearStart = Date.UTC(thursday.getUTCFullYear(), 0, 1);
  return Math.floor((thursday.getTime() - yearStart) / DAY_MS / 7) + 1;
}

/** Shortens `text` to at most `max` UTF-16 units, as Telegram counts, ending it with "…". */
export function clip(text: string, max: number): string {
  if (text.length <= max) return text;
  let out = '';
  for (const ch of text) {
    if (out.length + ch.length + 1 > max) break;
    out += ch;
  }
  return `${out.trimEnd()}…`;
}

export const BOARDS = ['fee_chains', 'tokens', 'lanes'] as const;
export type BoardName = (typeof BOARDS)[number];
export const BOARD_SIZE = 10;
export const CLIMB_MIN = 3;
export const MAX_CLIMBERS = 3;

/** This week's top 10, each with its rank the week before, or null when it was outside that week's top 10. */
export function rankBoard(current: readonly { key: string; label: string | null; value: number }[], previous: readonly { key: string }[]): BoardEntry[] {
  const before = new Map(previous.slice(0, BOARD_SIZE).map((e, i) => [e.key, i + 1]));
  return current.slice(0, BOARD_SIZE).map((e, i) => ({ key: e.key, label: e.label, value: e.value, rank: i + 1, previous_rank: before.get(e.key) ?? null }));
}

export interface Climber {
  board: BoardName;
  entry: BoardEntry;
  /** Places gained; null for an entry that came from outside the top 10. */
  gain: number | null;
}

export type MoversHeadline =
  | { kind: 'leader'; board: BoardName; entry: BoardEntry; dethroned: BoardEntry | null }
  | { kind: 'climber'; climber: Climber };

export interface Movers {
  monday: string;
  headline: MoversHeadline;
  climbers: Climber[];
}

/** An entry from outside the top 10 climbed at least from #11. */
const climbed = (c: Climber) => c.gain ?? BOARD_SIZE + 1 - c.entry.rank;

/**
 * The week's movers against the week before: a new #1 (fees, then tokens, then lanes) or else the top climber leads,
 * and up to 3 climbers follow. A board empty in either week is not compared, so a week without data names no mover.
 */
export function moversOf(weeks: readonly MomentsWeek[], monday: string): Movers | null {
  const week = weeks.find((w) => w.week === monday);
  const before = weeks.find((w) => w.week === addDays(monday, -7));
  if (!week || !before) return null;
  const boards = BOARDS.filter((b) => week.boards[b].length > 0 && before.boards[b].length > 0);
  const climbers = boards
    .flatMap((board) =>
      week.boards[board]
        .filter((e) => e.previous_rank === null || e.previous_rank - e.rank >= CLIMB_MIN)
        .map((entry): Climber => ({ board, entry, gain: entry.previous_rank === null ? null : entry.previous_rank - entry.rank })),
    )
    .sort((a, b) => climbed(b) - climbed(a) || BOARDS.indexOf(a.board) - BOARDS.indexOf(b.board) || a.entry.rank - b.entry.rank);
  for (const board of boards) {
    const top = week.boards[board][0]!;
    if (top.previous_rank === 1) continue;
    const others = climbers.filter((c) => !(c.board === board && c.entry.key === top.key));
    return { monday, headline: { kind: 'leader', board, entry: top, dethroned: before.boards[board][0] ?? null }, climbers: others.slice(0, MAX_CLIMBERS) };
  }
  const [first, ...rest] = climbers;
  return first ? { monday, headline: { kind: 'climber', climber: first }, climbers: rest.slice(0, MAX_CLIMBERS) } : null;
}

/** The first week whose spotlight can be posted; a pick before it was never posted, so it never makes a chain skip a week. */
export const SPOTLIGHT_SINCE = '2026-10-05';
export const SPOTLIGHT_MIN_MESSAGES = 100;
export const SPOTLIGHT_MIN_PREVIOUS = 50;
export const SPOTLIGHT_SKIP_WEEKS = 4;

export interface ChainCount {
  selector: string;
  messages: number;
  previous: number;
  firstDay: string;
}

export interface SpotlightWeek {
  monday: string;
  chains: readonly ChainCount[];
}

/**
 * The fastest grower by messages, week over week. A chain needs 100 messages this week and 50 the week before, and a
 * first message before that week began (a partial first week would inflate its rise). Ties go to more messages, then
 * the selector.
 */
export function spotlightPick(week: SpotlightWeek, recent: ReadonlySet<string>): string | null {
  const weekBefore = addDays(week.monday, -7);
  const candidates = week.chains.filter(
    (c) =>
      c.messages >= SPOTLIGHT_MIN_MESSAGES && c.previous >= SPOTLIGHT_MIN_PREVIOUS && c.firstDay <= weekBefore && c.messages > c.previous && !recent.has(c.selector),
  );
  candidates.sort((a, b) => b.messages * a.previous - a.messages * b.previous || b.messages - a.messages || compare(a.selector, b.selector));
  return candidates[0]?.selector ?? null;
}

/** Each week's pick, in week order, skipping a chain picked in the 4 weeks before from SPOTLIGHT_SINCE on. */
export function spotlightPicks(weeks: readonly SpotlightWeek[]): Map<string, string | null> {
  const picks = new Map<string, string | null>();
  for (const week of [...weeks].sort((a, b) => compare(a.monday, b.monday))) {
    const recent = new Set<string>();
    for (let i = 1; i <= SPOTLIGHT_SKIP_WEEKS; i++) {
      const monday = addDays(week.monday, -7 * i);
      const pick = monday >= SPOTLIGHT_SINCE ? picks.get(monday) : undefined;
      if (pick) recent.add(pick);
    }
    picks.set(week.monday, spotlightPick(week, recent));
  }
  return picks;
}

export const COUNTDOWN_PACE_DAYS = 7;

const METRIC_OF: Record<MilestoneMetric, (d: DayTotals) => number> = {
  messages: (d) => d.messages,
  value: (d) => d.usd_value,
  fees: (d) => d.fee_usd ?? 0,
};

/** The Records page's thresholds, far enough past the total to hold the next one. */
const THRESHOLDS: Record<MilestoneMetric, (total: number) => number[]> = {
  messages: (total) => messageThresholds(Math.max(total, 1_000) * 10),
  value: (total) => valueThresholds(Math.max(total, 1e9) * 10),
  fees: (total) => feeThresholds(Math.max(total, 1e6) * 10, 1e6),
};

/** For each tracked metric, the next threshold not yet crossed, at the pace of the last 7 finalized days. */
export function countdownsOf(days: readonly DayTotals[]): Countdown[] {
  const sorted = [...days].sort((a, b) => compare(a.day, b.day));
  const recent = sorted.slice(-COUNTDOWN_PACE_DAYS);
  if (recent.length < COUNTDOWN_PACE_DAYS) return [];
  const milestones = trackedMilestones(sorted);
  return MILESTONE_METRICS.flatMap((metric) => {
    if (metric === 'fees' && !feeCoverageComplete(sorted)) return [];
    const pick = METRIC_OF[metric];
    const total = sorted.reduce((sum, d) => sum + pick(d), 0);
    const perDay = recent.reduce((sum, d) => sum + pick(d), 0) / COUNTDOWN_PACE_DAYS;
    const threshold = THRESHOLDS[metric](total).find((t) => t > total);
    if (threshold === undefined || perDay <= 0) return [];
    const last = milestones.filter((m) => m.metric === metric).at(-1);
    return [{
      metric,
      threshold,
      total: cents(total),
      per_day: cents(perDay),
      days_left: Math.ceil((threshold - total) / perDay),
      previous: last ? { threshold: last.threshold, day: last.day } : null,
    }];
  });
}

export const ROUNDUP_DAYS = 5;

/** Monday to Friday's new lanes and tokens, by first-day value; a lane of a chain whose welcome falls in the week is left out. */
export function roundupOf(m: Pick<MomentsBody, 'arrivals'>, monday: string): { lanes: LaneArrival[]; tokens: TokenArrival[] } | null {
  const friday = addDays(monday, ROUNDUP_DAYS - 1);
  const inWeek = (day: string) => day >= monday && day <= friday;
  const welcomed = new Set(m.arrivals.chains.filter((c) => c.day >= addDays(monday, -1) && c.day <= friday).map((c) => c.selector));
  const byValue = <T extends { usd: number; key: string }>(a: T, b: T) => b.usd - a.usd || compare(a.key, b.key);
  const lanes = m.arrivals.lanes.filter((l) => inWeek(l.day) && !l.key.split('>').some((s) => welcomed.has(s))).sort(byValue);
  const tokens = m.arrivals.tokens.filter((t) => inWeek(t.day)).sort(byValue);
  return lanes.length + tokens.length === 0 ? null : { lanes, tokens };
}

export const POLL_QUESTION_MAX = 300;
export const POLL_OPTION_MAX = 100;
export const POLL_CHOICES = 3;
export const FEES_BAND_PCT = 10;
export const OTHER = 'other';

export interface PollChoice {
  key: string;
  label: string;
}

/** Last week's figures a poll asks about: the top fee chains and lanes, best first, and the fee total. */
export interface PollData {
  feeChains: readonly PollChoice[];
  lanes: readonly PollChoice[];
  feeTotal: number | null;
}

export type PollQuestion = Pick<Poll, 'week' | 'question_kind' | 'question' | 'options'>;

export function pollKindFor(monday: string): PollKind {
  return POLL_KINDS[isoWeek(monday) % POLL_KINDS.length]!;
}

const QUESTIONS: Record<PollKind, (d: PollData) => { question: string; options: PollChoice[] } | null> = {
  fee_chain: (d) =>
    d.feeChains.length === 0
      ? null
      : { question: 'Which chain will pay the most CCIP fees this week?', options: [...d.feeChains.slice(0, POLL_CHOICES), { key: OTHER, label: 'Another chain' }] },
  fees_change: (d) =>
    d.feeTotal === null || d.feeTotal <= 0
      ? null
      : {
          question: `Will CCIP fees beat last week's ${formatFeeTotal(d.feeTotal)}?`,
          options: [
            { key: 'up', label: `Up more than ${FEES_BAND_PCT}%` },
            { key: 'within', label: `Within ${FEES_BAND_PCT}%` },
            { key: 'down', label: `Down more than ${FEES_BAND_PCT}%` },
          ],
        },
  lane: (d) =>
    d.lanes.length === 0
      ? null
      : { question: 'Which lane will move the most value this week?', options: [...d.lanes.slice(0, POLL_CHOICES), { key: OTHER, label: 'Another lane' }] },
};

/** The week's question: ISO week mod 3 picks the kind, and a kind last week's data cannot ask falls through to the next. */
export function pollQuestion(monday: string, data: PollData): PollQuestion | null {
  const first = POLL_KINDS.indexOf(pollKindFor(monday));
  for (let i = 0; i < POLL_KINDS.length; i++) {
    const kind = POLL_KINDS[(first + i) % POLL_KINDS.length]!;
    const asked = QUESTIONS[kind](data);
    if (asked) {
      return {
        week: monday,
        question_kind: kind,
        question: clip(asked.question, POLL_QUESTION_MAX),
        options: asked.options.map((o) => ({ key: o.key, label: clip(o.label, POLL_OPTION_MAX) })),
      };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export interface PollLeader {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  value: number;
}

/** What the poll's own week turned out to be. */
export interface PollResult {
  feeChain: PollLeader | null;
  lane: PollLeader | null;
  feeTotal: number | null;
  previousFeeTotal: number | null;
}

const optionFor = (options: readonly PollChoice[], key: string) => (options.some((o) => o.key === key) ? key : OTHER);

export function pollAnswer(poll: Pick<Poll, 'question_kind' | 'options'>, result: PollResult): Pick<Poll, 'answer' | 'outcome'> {
  const none = { answer: null, outcome: null };
  if (poll.question_kind === 'fees_change') {
    const { feeTotal, previousFeeTotal } = result;
    if (feeTotal === null || previousFeeTotal === null || previousFeeTotal <= 0) return none;
    const pct = ((feeTotal - previousFeeTotal) / previousFeeTotal) * 100;
    const answer = pct > FEES_BAND_PCT ? 'up' : pct < -FEES_BAND_PCT ? 'down' : 'within';
    return { answer, outcome: { key: 'fees', label: 'CCIP fees', value: feeTotal, change_pct: Math.round(pct * 10) / 10 } };
  }
  const leader = poll.question_kind === 'fee_chain' ? result.feeChain : result.lane;
  if (leader === null) return none;
  return { answer: optionFor(poll.options, leader.key), outcome: { key: leader.key, label: leader.label, value: leader.value, change_pct: null } };
}

/** The share of voters who picked the answer, once the poll is answered and was closed with votes. */
export function calledShare(poll: Pick<Poll, 'options' | 'answer' | 'votes' | 'total_votes'>): number | null {
  const at = poll.options.findIndex((o) => o.key === poll.answer);
  return at < 0 || poll.votes === null || !poll.total_votes ? null : (poll.votes[at] ?? null);
}

/** Each option's share of the votes, in whole percent. */
export function voteShares(voters: readonly number[]): number[] {
  const total = voters.reduce((sum, n) => sum + n, 0);
  return voters.map((n) => (total === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((n * 100) / total)));
}

/** What the Worker keeps in D1 `meta` under `poll:<monday>` once a poll is sent, with its voter counts once it is closed. */
export const PollRecordSchema = z.object({
  chat_id: z.string(),
  message_id: z.number().int(),
  question_kind: z.enum(POLL_KINDS),
  question: z.string(),
  options: z.array(z.object({ key: z.string(), label: z.string() })),
  voters: z.array(z.number()).optional(),
  total_voters: z.number().optional(),
});
export type PollRecord = z.infer<typeof PollRecordSchema>;

export const pollRecordKey = (monday: string) => `poll:${monday}`;

/** A row that is missing, not JSON or not a poll record reads as no poll. */
export function parsePollRecord(raw: string | null): PollRecord | null {
  if (raw === null) return null;
  try {
    const parsed = PollRecordSchema.safeParse(JSON.parse(raw));
    return parsed.success ? parsed.data : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
```

```ts
import { addDays, type DailyBreakdown, type Dim, type NetworkInfo } from '@ccip-dev/core';
import type { DayTotals } from '@ccip-dev/core/public';
import { NETWORKS } from '@ccip-dev/core/testing';
import { env } from 'cloudflare:workers';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { buildMoments } from '../src/moments';
import { publishHistoryFiles } from '../src/publish';
import * as store from '../src/store';
import { harness, readPublic, resetStorage, seedRegistry } from './helpers';

beforeEach(resetStorage);

const ETH = NETWORKS.ethereum.chainSelector;
const BASE = NETWORKS.base.chainSelector;
const BSC = NETWORKS.bsc.chainSelector;
const TEMPO = '7281642695469137430';
const TEMPO_NET: NetworkInfo = { name: 'tempo-mainnet', displayName: 'Tempo Mainnet', chainSelector: TEMPO, chainId: '4217', chainFamily: 'EVM', environment: 'mainnet' };
const USDAT_ETH = '0xa1a1a1a1a1a1a1a1a1a1a1a1a1a1a1a1a1a1a1a1';
const USDAT_BASE = '0xa2a2a2a2a2a2a2a2a2a2a2a2a2a2a2a2a2a2a2a2';
const USDC_BASE = '0xc1c1c1c1c1c1c1c1c1c1c1c1c1c1c1c1c1c1c1c1';
const USDC_BSC = '0xc2c2c2c2c2c2c2c2c2c2c2c2c2c2c2c2c2c2c2c2';
const LOOSE = '0xbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbb';
const NOW = '2026-10-12T00:07:00.000Z';
const LAST_DAY = '2026-10-11';

const row = (day: string, dim: Dim, key: string, messages: number, usd_value: number, fee_usd: number | null = null): DailyBreakdown => ({ day, dim, key, messages, usd_value, fee_usd });
const lane = (day: string, src: string, dst: string, messages: number, usd: number) => row(day, 'lane', `${src}>${dst}`, messages, usd);
const totals = (day: string, fee: number | null = null): DayTotals => ({
  day, messages: 1, token_messages: 0, usd_value: 1, fee_usd: fee, unique_senders: 1, median_delivery_s: null, unpriced_messages: 0, fee_link_usd: null,
});

/** Writes each day's rows as finalize would, one replaceDaily per day. */
async function seedRows(rows: DailyBreakdown[]): Promise<void> {
  const days = [...new Set(rows.map((r) => r.day))].sort();
  for (const day of days) await store.replaceDaily(env.DB, totals(day), rows.filter((r) => r.day === day), NOW);
}

async function seedRegistryChains(): Promise<void> {
  await seedRegistry([NETWORKS.ethereum, NETWORKS.base, NETWORKS.bsc, TEMPO_NET], [
    { chainSelector: ETH, address: USDAT_ETH, symbol: 'USDat', name: 'USDat', decimals: 18, groupId: 'usdat' },
    { chainSelector: BASE, address: USDAT_BASE, symbol: 'USDat', name: 'USDat', decimals: 18, groupId: 'usdat' },
    { chainSelector: BASE, address: USDC_BASE, symbol: 'USDC', name: 'USD Coin', decimals: 6, groupId: 'usdc' },
    { chainSelector: BSC, address: USDC_BSC, symbol: 'USDC', name: 'USD Coin', decimals: 6, groupId: 'usdc' },
  ]);
}

const build = (lastDay = LAST_DAY, days: DayTotals[] = []) => buildMoments(harness({ now: NOW }).c, { lastDay, days });

describe('moments.json arrivals', () => {
  beforeEach(async () => {
    // #given Tempo entered the registry on Sep 1 but sent its first message on Oct 11, to Ethereum and from Base
    await seedRegistryChains();
    await store.insertArrivals(env.DB, 'chain', [TEMPO], '2026-09-01T00:00:00.000Z', null);
    await seedRows([
      lane('2026-08-03', ETH, BASE, 10, 1_000), lane('2026-08-03', BASE, BSC, 5, 500), row('2026-08-03', 'token', `${BASE}:${USDC_BASE}`, 5, 500),
      lane('2026-09-11', BSC, ETH, 1, 10),
      lane('2026-09-12', BSC, BASE, 2, 20),
      lane('2026-10-06', ETH, BSC, 4, 2_000),
      row('2026-10-07', 'token', `${BSC}:${USDC_BSC}`, 1, 100),
      row('2026-10-08', 'token', `${ETH}:${USDAT_ETH}`, 3, 5_000),
      row('2026-10-09', 'token', `${BASE}:${USDAT_BASE}`, 1, 1_000), row('2026-10-09', 'token', `${BSC}:${LOOSE}`, 2, 70),
      lane('2026-10-11', TEMPO, ETH, 30, 400_000), lane('2026-10-11', BASE, TEMPO, 8, 12_500), lane('2026-10-11', ETH, BASE, 1, 5),
    ]);
  });

  it('dates a chain by its first message, numbers it among the chains so far, and lists its first-day partners by value', async () => {
    // #when
    const moments = await build();
    // #then
    expect({ network: moments.network, chains: moments.arrivals.chains }).toEqual({
      network: { chains: 4, lanes: 7 },
      chains: [{ selector: TEMPO, day: '2026-10-11', number: 4, partners: [ETH, BASE], messages: 38, usd: 412_500 }],
    });
  });

  it('lists the lanes whose first message fell in the last 30 days, with their first-day totals', async () => {
    // #when Sep 12 is the first of the 30 days through Oct 11
    const { lanes } = (await build()).arrivals;
    // #then
    expect(lanes).toEqual([
      { key: `${BSC}>${BASE}`, day: '2026-09-12', messages: 2, usd: 20 },
      { key: `${ETH}>${BSC}`, day: '2026-10-06', messages: 4, usd: 2_000 },
      { key: `${TEMPO}>${ETH}`, day: '2026-10-11', messages: 30, usd: 400_000 },
      { key: `${BASE}>${TEMPO}`, day: '2026-10-11', messages: 8, usd: 12_500 },
    ]);
  });

  it('dates a registry token group by its first message on any chain, and a token outside the registry on its own', async () => {
    // #when USDC's group moved in August, so its new chain is not new
    const { tokens } = (await build()).arrivals;
    // #then
    expect(tokens).toEqual([
      { key: 'usdat', symbol: 'USDat', day: '2026-10-08', chains: [ETH], usd: 5_000 },
      { key: `${BSC}:${LOOSE}`, symbol: null, day: '2026-10-09', chains: [BSC], usd: 70 },
    ]);
  });
});

describe('moments.json weeks', () => {
  const src = (day: string, key: string, messages: number, usd: number, fee: number) => row(day, 'src_chain', key, messages, usd, fee);
  const dst = (day: string, key: string, messages: number, usd: number) => row(day, 'dst_chain', key, messages, usd, null);

  beforeEach(async () => {
    // #given three old chains; Base grew fastest in the week of Oct 5 and Ethereum in the week of Oct 12
    await seedRegistryChains();
    await seedRows([
      lane('2026-08-03', ETH, BASE, 1, 1), lane('2026-08-03', BASE, BSC, 1, 1),
      src('2026-09-28', BSC, 80, 1_000, 300), src('2026-09-28', BASE, 40, 2_000, 200), src('2026-09-28', ETH, 100, 3_000, 100),
      dst('2026-09-28', BASE, 20, 1_500), dst('2026-09-28', BSC, 20, 500),
      src('2026-10-05', BASE, 150, 60_000, 500), src('2026-10-05', BSC, 100, 9_000, 300), src('2026-10-05', ETH, 250, 90_000, 50),
      dst('2026-10-05', BASE, 50, 36_400), dst('2026-10-05', ETH, 50, 1_000),
      lane('2026-10-05', ETH, BASE, 30, 9_000), lane('2026-10-05', BASE, BSC, 20, 3_000),
      row('2026-10-05', 'token', `${BASE}:${USDC_BASE}`, 10, 41_200), row('2026-10-05', 'token', `${ETH}:${USDAT_ETH}`, 4, 50_000),
      src('2026-10-12', ETH, 900, 1, 10), src('2026-10-12', BASE, 800, 1, 10), src('2026-10-12', BSC, 100, 1, 10),
    ]);
  });

  it('ranks the top 10 of each board for each of the last 8 complete weeks, with each entry’s rank the week before', async () => {
    // #when
    const { weeks } = await build();
    const latest = weeks.at(-1)!;
    // #then
    expect({
      mondays: weeks.map((w) => w.week),
      fees: latest.boards.fee_chains.map((e) => [e.key, e.value, e.rank, e.previous_rank]),
      tokens: latest.boards.tokens.map((e) => [e.label, e.rank, e.previous_rank]),
      lanes: latest.boards.lanes.map((e) => e.key),
    }).toEqual({
      mondays: ['2026-08-17', '2026-08-24', '2026-08-31', '2026-09-07', '2026-09-14', '2026-09-21', '2026-09-28', '2026-10-05'],
      fees: [[BASE, 500, 1, 2], [BSC, 300, 2, 1], [ETH, 50, 3, 3]],
      tokens: [['USDat', 1, null], ['USDC', 2, null]],
      lanes: [`${ETH}>${BASE}`, `${BASE}>${BSC}`],
    });
  });

  it("spotlights the week's fastest grower by messages sent and received, with its rank, totals, changes, partners and top token", async () => {
    // #when Base: 60 messages the week before, 200 now (+233%); Ethereum: 100, then 300 (+200%)
    const { weeks } = await build();
    // #then
    expect(weeks.at(-1)!.spotlight).toEqual({
      selector: BASE, rank: 2, messages: 200, previous_messages: 60, usd: 96_400, previous_usd: 3_500, fee_usd: 500, previous_fee_usd: 200,
      partners: [ETH, BSC], token: { key: `${BASE}:${USDC_BASE}`, label: 'USDC', usd: 41_200 },
    });
  });

  it('skips the chain spotlighted the week before', async () => {
    // #when a week later, Base grew fastest again (800 against 200) but was the Oct 5 pick
    const { weeks } = await build('2026-10-18');
    // #then Ethereum grew from 300 to 900
    expect(weeks.slice(-2).map((w) => w.spotlight?.selector)).toEqual([BASE, ETH]);
  });
});

        answer: null, outcome: null, votes: null, total_votes: null,
      },
    ]);

  it('keeps a sent poll that could not be closed without votes, and lists no poll for a week none was sent', async () => {

  });
});

describe('moments.json publish', () => {

    expect(plan.results.map((r) => r.detail).join(' | ')).toContain('USING INDEX daily_breakdown_dim');

});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them and confirm they fail.** Run `pnpm --filter @ccip-dev/worker exec vitest run test/moments.test.ts`. Expected: FAIL: `../src/moments` does not exist.

- [ ] **Step 3: Build and publish `moments.json`.**

The builder reads D1 through the same functions as the other public files: `topBetween` for every board and spotlight figure, `historyDays` rows for the countdowns and the poll's fee totals. Its new queries are cheap scans of indexed rows:
  - `firstDays` groups one dimension of `daily_breakdown` (through `daily_breakdown_dim`), so a lane's or token's first day, and a chain's (the first day it is on either end of a lane, as `replay.json` has it), come from one query each.
  - `chainWeekMessages` groups the chain rows by week in SQL, from the earlier of `SPOTLIGHT_SINCE` and the oldest published week.
  - A poll's record (`poll:<monday>`, written in Task 7) is what was asked, so a sent poll is described by it, not by a recomputation that a re-finalized day could change.

Move `lastCompleteMonday` to core, so the moments builder needs nothing from `publish.ts` (which imports the builder). At the end of `packages/core/src/time.ts`, add:

```ts
/** The Monday of the last Monday–Sunday week that ends on or before `lastDay`. */
export function lastCompleteMonday(lastDay: string): string {
  return addDays(weekStart(addDays(lastDay, 1)), -7);
}
```

```ts
/** meta values by key, for the keys that have one. */
export async function getMetas(db: D1Database, keys: string[]): Promise<Map<string, string>> {
  const values = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const chunk of chunks([...new Set(keys)], PARAM_CHUNK)) {
    const { results } = await db
      .prepare(`SELECT key, value FROM meta WHERE key IN (${placeholders(chunk.length)})`)
      .bind(...chunk)
      .all<{ key: string; value: string }>();
    for (const r of results) values.set(r.key, r.value);
  }
  return values;
}
```
