Before `export interface ReserveTransferRow`, add the moments queries:

```ts
export interface FirstDayRow {
  key: string;
  first_day: string;
}

/** Each key's first day with a message, oldest first. */
export async function firstDays(db: D1Database, dim: 'lane' | 'token'): Promise<FirstDayRow[]> {
  const { results } = await db
    .prepare('SELECT key, MIN(day) AS first_day FROM daily_breakdown WHERE dim = ? GROUP BY key ORDER BY first_day, key')
    .bind(dim)
    .all<FirstDayRow>();
  return results;
}

/** The rows of `dim` over [fromDay, toDay], one per day and key. */
export async function breakdownBetween(db: D1Database, dim: Dim, fromDay: string, toDay: string): Promise<LeaderRow[]> {
  const { results } = await db
    .prepare('SELECT day, key, messages, usd_value, fee_usd FROM daily_breakdown WHERE dim = ? AND day >= ? AND day <= ? ORDER BY day, key')
    .bind(dim, fromDay, toDay)
    .all<LeaderRow>();
  return results;
}

export interface ChainWeekRow {
  key: string;
  /** The week's Monday. */
  week: string;
  messages: number;
}

/** Each chain's messages sent or received, by the Monday of their week, over [?1, ?2]; it reads only the window's chain rows. */
export const CHAIN_WEEK_MESSAGES_SQL = `SELECT key, date(day, '-' || ((CAST(strftime('%w', day) AS INTEGER) + 6) % 7) || ' days') AS week, SUM(messages) AS messages
  FROM daily_breakdown WHERE dim IN ('src_chain', 'dst_chain') AND day >= ?1 AND day <= ?2
  GROUP BY key, week ORDER BY week, key`;

export async function chainWeekMessages(db: D1Database, fromDay: string, toDay: string): Promise<ChainWeekRow[]> {
  const { results } = await db.prepare(CHAIN_WEEK_MESSAGES_SQL).bind(fromDay, toDay).all<ChainWeekRow>();
  return results;
}

/** The registry tokens of the given groups. */
export async function tokenGroupMembers(db: D1Database, groups: string[]): Promise<{ chain: string; address: string; group_id: string; symbol: string }[]> {
  const members: { chain: string; address: string; group_id: string; symbol: string }[] = [];
  for (const chunk of chunks([...new Set(groups)], PARAM_CHUNK)) {
    const { results } = await db
      .prepare(`SELECT chain, address, group_id, symbol FROM tokens WHERE group_id IN (${placeholders(chunk.length)}) ORDER BY chain, address`)
      .bind(...chunk)
      .all<{ chain: string; address: string; group_id: string; symbol: string }>();
    members.push(...results);
  }
  return members;
}
```

Create `worker/src/moments.ts`:

```ts
import { addDays, lastCompleteMonday, normalizeAddress, weekStart, type Dim } from '@ccip-dev/core';
import {
  BOARD_SIZE, countdownsOf, parsePollRecord, pollAnswer, pollQuestion, pollRecordKey, rankBoard, SPOTLIGHT_SINCE, spotlightPicks, voteShares,
  type ChainArrival, type LaneArrival, type MomentsBody, type PollData, type PollResult, type TokenArrival,
} from '@ccip-dev/core/moments';
import { chainName, chainNameMap, laneLabel, type ChainNames } from '@ccip-dev/core/names';
import type { BoardEntry, DayTotals, MomentsWeek, Poll, Spotlight } from '@ccip-dev/core/public';
import type { RunContext } from './context';
import * as store from './store';

export const ARRIVAL_DAYS = 30;
export const MOMENT_WEEKS = 8;
export const POLL_WEEKS = 8;
const FIRST_PARTNERS = 10;
const SPOTLIGHT_PARTNERS = 3;
/** More rows than any week holds for one dimension, so topBetween returns them all. */
const ALL_ROWS = 100_000;

export interface MomentsInputs {
  /** The last finalized day; the moments are as of its end. */
  lastDay: string;
  /** history.json's day rows. */
  days: readonly DayTotals[];
}

const compare = (a: string, b: string) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0);
const cents = (v: number) => Math.round(v * 100) / 100;
const centsOrNull = (v: number | null) => (v === null ? null : cents(v));
const sum = (values: readonly number[]) => values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);

function laneEnds(key: string): [string, string] | null {
  const parts = key.split('>');
  return parts.length === 2 && parts[0] && parts[1] ? [parts[0], parts[1]] : null;
}

function splitKey(key: string): { chain: string; address: string } {
  const at = key.indexOf(':');
  return { chain: key.slice(0, at), address: key.slice(at + 1) };
}

const tokenKey = (key: string) => {
  const { chain, address } = splitKey(key);
  return `${chain}:${normalizeAddress(address)}`;
};

/**
 * Everything moments.json publishes, from D1, as of the end of `lastDay`. It reads the same rows and rankings
 * (topBetween, history.json's days) as the other public files, so a moment's numbers match the rest of the site.
 */
export async function buildMoments(c: RunContext, { lastDay, days }: MomentsInputs): Promise<MomentsBody> {
  const db = c.env.DB;
  const names = chainNameMap([...(await store.chainDisplayNames(db))].map(([selector, n]) => ({ selector, ...n })));
  const lanes = await store.firstDays(db, 'lane');
  const chainFirst = chainFirstDays(lanes);
  const since = addDays(lastDay, -(ARRIVAL_DAYS - 1));
  const recentLanes = await store.breakdownBetween(db, 'lane', since, lastDay);
  const weeks = await momentWeeks(c, lastDay, chainFirst);
  return {
    network: { chains: chainFirst.size, lanes: lanes.length },
    arrivals: {
      chains: chainArrivals(chainFirst, recentLanes, since),
      lanes: laneArrivals(lanes, recentLanes, since),
      tokens: await tokenArrivals(c, since, lastDay),
    },
    weeks,
    countdowns: countdownsOf(days),
    polls: await pollsOf(c, lastDay, days, weeks, names),
  };
}

/** A chain's first day is the first day it is on either end of a lane, as in replay.json. */
function chainFirstDays(lanes: readonly store.FirstDayRow[]): Map<string, string> {
  const first = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const lane of lanes) {
    for (const selector of laneEnds(lane.key) ?? []) {
      const day = first.get(selector);
      if (day === undefined || lane.first_day < day) first.set(selector, lane.first_day);
    }
  }
  return first;
}

function chainArrivals(chainFirst: ReadonlyMap<string, string>, recentLanes: readonly store.LeaderRow[], since: string): ChainArrival[] {
  const firstDays = [...chainFirst.values()];
  return [...chainFirst]
    .filter(([, day]) => day >= since)
    .sort(([a, dayA], [b, dayB]) => compare(dayA, dayB) || compare(a, b))
    .map(([selector, day]) => {
      const rows = recentLanes.filter((r) => r.day === day && laneEnds(r.key)?.includes(selector));
      const partners = new Map<string, number>();
      for (const r of rows) {
        const other = laneEnds(r.key)!.find((s) => s !== selector)!;
        partners.set(other, (partners.get(other) ?? 0) + r.usd_value);
      }
      return {
        selector,
        day,
        number: firstDays.filter((d) => d <= day).length,
        partners: [...partners].sort(([a, va], [b, vb]) => vb - va || compare(a, b)).slice(0, FIRST_PARTNERS).map(([s]) => s),
        messages: sum(rows.map((r) => r.messages)),
        usd: cents(sum(rows.map((r) => r.usd_value))),
      };
    });
}

function laneArrivals(lanes: readonly store.FirstDayRow[], recentLanes: readonly store.LeaderRow[], since: string): LaneArrival[] {
  return lanes
    .filter((l) => l.first_day >= since)
    .map((l) => {
      const row = recentLanes.find((r) => r.day === l.first_day && r.key === l.key);
      return { key: l.key, day: l.first_day, messages: row?.messages ?? 0, usd: cents(row?.usd_value ?? 0) };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => compare(a.day, b.day) || b.usd - a.usd || compare(a.key, b.key));
}

/** A registry group arrives with its first message on any of its chains; a token outside the registry arrives on its own. */
async function tokenArrivals(c: RunContext, since: string, lastDay: string): Promise<TokenArrival[]> {
  const db = c.env.DB;
  const first = await store.firstDays(db, 'token');
  const fresh = first.filter((t) => t.first_day >= since);
  if (fresh.length === 0) return [];
  const firstOf = new Map(first.map((t) => [tokenKey(t.key), t.first_day]));
  const usdOn = new Map((await store.breakdownBetween(db, 'token', since, lastDay)).map((r) => [`${r.day}|${tokenKey(r.key)}`, r.usd_value]));
  const groupOf = await store.tokenGroupIds(db, fresh.map((t) => splitKey(t.key)));
  const groups = [...new Set(fresh.flatMap((t) => groupOf.get(tokenKey(t.key)) ?? []))];
  const members = await store.tokenGroupMembers(db, groups);
  const out: TokenArrival[] = [];
  for (const group of groups) {
    const moved = members
      .filter((m) => m.group_id === group)
      .flatMap((m) => {
        const key = `${m.chain}:${normalizeAddress(m.address)}`;
        const day = firstOf.get(key);
        return day === undefined ? [] : [{ key, day, chain: m.chain, symbol: m.symbol }];
      });
    const day = moved.map((m) => m.day).sort()[0];
    if (day === undefined || day < since) continue;
    const onFirstDay = moved
      .filter((m) => m.day === day)
      .map((m) => ({ ...m, usd: usdOn.get(`${day}|${m.key}`) ?? 0 }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.usd - a.usd || compare(a.chain, b.chain));
    out.push({ key: group, symbol: onFirstDay[0]?.symbol ?? null, day, chains: [...new Set(onFirstDay.map((m) => m.chain))], usd: cents(sum(onFirstDay.map((m) => m.usd))) });
  }
  const ungrouped = fresh.filter((t) => !groupOf.get(tokenKey(t.key)));
  const symbols = await store.tokenSymbols(db, ungrouped.map((t) => splitKey(t.key)));
  for (const t of ungrouped) {
    const key = tokenKey(t.key);
    out.push({ key, symbol: symbols.get(key) ?? null, day: t.first_day, chains: [splitKey(key).chain], usd: cents(usdOn.get(`${t.first_day}|${key}`) ?? 0) });
  }
  return out.sort((a, b) => compare(a.day, b.day) || b.usd - a.usd || compare(a.key, b.key));
}

type Top = (dim: Dim, order: store.TopOrder, monday: string, limit: number) => Promise<store.BreakdownRow[]>;

/** topBetween over a Monday–Sunday week, each query run once per build. */
function weekTops(db: D1Database): Top {
  const cache = new Map<string, Promise<store.BreakdownRow[]>>();
  return (dim, order, monday, limit) => {
    const key = `${dim}|${order}|${monday}|${limit}`;
    let rows = cache.get(key);
    if (rows === undefined) {
      rows = store.topBetween(db, dim, monday, addDays(monday, 6), limit, order);
      cache.set(key, rows);
    }
    return rows;
  };
}

/** The last 8 complete weeks: their boards against the week before, and their spotlight. */
async function momentWeeks(c: RunContext, lastDay: string, chainFirst: ReadonlyMap<string, string>): Promise<MomentsWeek[]> {
  const db = c.env.DB;
  const top = weekTops(db);
  const latest = lastCompleteMonday(lastDay);
  const mondays = Array.from({ length: MOMENT_WEEKS }, (_, i) => addDays(latest, -7 * (MOMENT_WEEKS - 1 - i)));
  const tokenKeys = (await Promise.all(mondays.map((m) => top('token', 'value', m, BOARD_SIZE)))).flat().map((r) => r.key);
  const symbols = await store.tokenSymbols(db, tokenKeys.map(splitKey));
  const board = async (dim: Dim, order: store.TopOrder, monday: string, value: (r: store.BreakdownRow) => number, label: (key: string) => string | null): Promise<BoardEntry[]> =>
    rankBoard(
      (await top(dim, order, monday, BOARD_SIZE)).map((r) => ({ key: r.key, label: label(r.key), value: value(r) })),
      await top(dim, order, addDays(monday, -7), BOARD_SIZE),
    );
  const picks = await spotlightPicksFor(db, mondays, latest, chainFirst);
  const weeks: MomentsWeek[] = [];
  for (const monday of mondays) {
    const pick = picks.get(monday) ?? null;
    weeks.push({
      week: monday,
      boards: {
        fee_chains: await board('src_chain', 'fees', monday, (r) => cents(r.fee_usd ?? 0), () => null),
        tokens: await board('token', 'value', monday, (r) => cents(r.usd_value), (key) => symbols.get(tokenKey(key)) ?? null),
        lanes: await board('lane', 'value', monday, (r) => cents(r.usd_value), () => null),
      },
      spotlight: pick === null ? null : await spotlightOf(c, top, monday, pick),
    });
  }
  return weeks;
}

/** Picks run from the earlier of SPOTLIGHT_SINCE and the oldest published week, so the 4-week skip sees every posted pick. */
async function spotlightPicksFor(db: D1Database, mondays: readonly string[], latest: string, chainFirst: ReadonlyMap<string, string>): Promise<Map<string, string | null>> {
  const start = mondays[0]! < SPOTLIGHT_SINCE ? mondays[0]! : SPOTLIGHT_SINCE;
  const byWeek = new Map<string, Map<string, number>>();
  for (const r of await store.chainWeekMessages(db, addDays(start, -7), addDays(latest, 6))) {
    const week = byWeek.get(r.week) ?? new Map<string, number>();
    week.set(r.key, r.messages);
    byWeek.set(r.week, week);
  }
  const series = [];
  for (let monday = start; monday <= latest; monday = addDays(monday, 7)) {
    const before = byWeek.get(addDays(monday, -7));
    series.push({
      monday,
      chains: [...(byWeek.get(monday) ?? [])].map(([selector, messages]) => ({ selector, messages, previous: before?.get(selector) ?? 0, firstDay: chainFirst.get(selector) ?? monday })),
    });
  }
  return spotlightPicks(series);
}

async function spotlightOf(c: RunContext, top: Top, monday: string, selector: string): Promise<Spotlight> {
  const totals = async (week: string) => {
    const src = (await top('src_chain', 'value', week, ALL_ROWS)).find((r) => r.key === selector);
    const dst = (await top('dst_chain', 'value', week, ALL_ROWS)).find((r) => r.key === selector);
    return { messages: (src?.messages ?? 0) + (dst?.messages ?? 0), usd: (src?.usd_value ?? 0) + (dst?.usd_value ?? 0), fee: src?.fee_usd ?? null };
  };
  const now = await totals(monday);
  const before = await totals(addDays(monday, -7));
  const messagesBy = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const dim of ['src_chain', 'dst_chain'] as const) {
    for (const r of await top(dim, 'value', monday, ALL_ROWS)) messagesBy.set(r.key, (messagesBy.get(r.key) ?? 0) + r.messages);
  }
  const rank = [...messagesBy].sort(([a, x], [b, y]) => y - x || compare(a, b)).findIndex(([s]) => s === selector) + 1;
  const partners = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const r of await top('lane', 'value', monday, ALL_ROWS)) {
    const ends = laneEnds(r.key);
    if (ends === null || !ends.includes(selector)) continue;
    const other = ends[0] === selector ? ends[1] : ends[0];
    partners.set(other, (partners.get(other) ?? 0) + r.usd_value);
  }
  const token = (await top('token', 'value', monday, ALL_ROWS)).find((r) => r.key.startsWith(`${selector}:`)) ?? null;
  const symbol = token === null ? null : ((await store.tokenSymbols(c.env.DB, [splitKey(token.key)])).get(tokenKey(token.key)) ?? null);
  return {
    selector,
    rank,
    messages: now.messages,
    previous_messages: before.messages,
    usd: cents(now.usd),
    previous_usd: cents(before.usd),
    fee_usd: centsOrNull(now.fee),
    previous_fee_usd: centsOrNull(before.fee),
    partners: [...partners].sort(([a, va], [b, vb]) => vb - va || compare(a, b)).slice(0, SPOTLIGHT_PARTNERS).map(([s]) => s),
    token: token === null ? null : { key: token.key, label: symbol, usd: cents(token.usd_value) },
  };
}

/**
 * This week's poll (still open, so unanswered) and each of the 7 before it that was sent. A sent poll is described by
 * its record, which holds what was asked; its answer comes once its week is in history.json, and its votes once closed.
 */
async function pollsOf(c: RunContext, lastDay: string, days: readonly DayTotals[], weeks: readonly MomentsWeek[], names: ChainNames): Promise<Poll[]> {
  const current = weekStart(addDays(lastDay, 1));
  const mondays = Array.from({ length: POLL_WEEKS }, (_, i) => addDays(current, -7 * (POLL_WEEKS - 1 - i)));
  const records = await store.getMetas(c.env.DB, mondays.map(pollRecordKey));
  const byWeek = new Map(weeks.map((w) => [w.week, w]));
  const finalized = new Set(days.map((d) => d.day));
  const complete = (monday: string) => Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => addDays(monday, i)).every((d) => finalized.has(d));
  const feeTotal = (monday: string): number | null => {
    const week = days.filter((d) => d.day >= monday && d.day <= addDays(monday, 6) && d.fee_usd !== null);
    return week.length === 0 ? null : cents(sum(week.map((d) => d.fee_usd!)));
  };
  const chain = (e: BoardEntry) => ({ key: e.key, label: chainName(names, e.key), value: e.value });
  const lane = (e: BoardEntry) => ({ key: e.key, label: laneLabel(names, e.key), value: e.value });
  const askedFrom = (monday: string): PollData => {
    const week = byWeek.get(addDays(monday, -7));
    return { feeChains: (week?.boards.fee_chains ?? []).map(chain), lanes: (week?.boards.lanes ?? []).map(lane), feeTotal: feeTotal(addDays(monday, -7)) };
  };
  const resultOf = (monday: string): PollResult => {
    const week = byWeek.get(monday);
    const feeChain = week?.boards.fee_chains[0];
    const topLane = week?.boards.lanes[0];
    return { feeChain: feeChain ? chain(feeChain) : null, lane: topLane ? lane(topLane) : null, feeTotal: feeTotal(monday), previousFeeTotal: feeTotal(addDays(monday, -7)) };
  };
  return mondays.flatMap((monday): Poll[] => {
    const record = parsePollRecord(records.get(pollRecordKey(monday)) ?? null);
    const asked = record ?? (monday === current ? pollQuestion(monday, askedFrom(monday)) : null);
    if (asked === null) return [];
    const { answer, outcome } = complete(monday) ? pollAnswer(asked, resultOf(monday)) : { answer: null, outcome: null };
    return [{
      week: monday,
      question_kind: asked.question_kind,
      question: asked.question,
      options: asked.options,
      answer,
      outcome,
      votes: record?.voters ? voteShares(record.voters) : null,
      total_votes: record?.total_voters ?? null,
    }];
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the calls.**

Checked against the Bot API (Bot API 10.3, Aug 24, 2026, core.telegram.org/bots/api): `sendPoll` takes `chat_id`, `question` (1–300 characters), `options` (a JSON list of 1–12 `InputPollOption` objects `{ text }`, 1–100 characters each) and `is_anonymous` (default true), and returns the sent Message; `stopPoll` takes `chat_id` and `message_id` and returns the stopped Poll, whose `options[]` carry `voter_count` and which carries `total_voter_count`. Both take JSON bodies. The poll is sent without `open_period` or `close_date` (the spec's "close on their own after at most 10 minutes" is out of date: `open_period` now goes up to 2,628,000 s); next Monday's run closes it.

with:

```ts
  }
}

export interface TelegramPoll {
  question: string;
  options: readonly string[];
}

/**
 * Sends an anonymous poll, as a channel's polls are, with no close time: the next Monday's run closes it. Returns the
 * poll's message id, or what went wrong.
 */
export async function sendTelegramPoll(
  deps: Pick<Deps, 'fetch' | 'sleep'>,
  config: TelegramConfig,
  poll: TelegramPoll,
  timeoutMs = TELEGRAM_TIMEOUT_MS,
): Promise<{ messageId: number } | { problem: string }> {
  const body = { chat_id: config.chatId, question: poll.question, options: poll.options.map((text) => ({ text })), is_anonymous: true };
  const result = await callTelegram(deps, config.token, 'sendPoll', body, timeoutMs);
  if ('problem' in result) return result;
  const messageId = (result.result as { message_id?: unknown } | null)?.message_id;
  return typeof messageId === 'number' ? { messageId } : { problem: 'Telegram answered without a message id' };
}

/** Closes a poll the bot sent and returns each option's voter count, in option order, and the total; or what went wrong. */
export async function stopTelegramPoll(
  deps: Pick<Deps, 'fetch' | 'sleep'>,
  config: TelegramConfig,
  messageId: number,
  timeoutMs = TELEGRAM_TIMEOUT_MS,
): Promise<{ voters: number[]; total: number } | { problem: string }> {
  const result = await callTelegram(deps, config.token, 'stopPoll', { chat_id: config.chatId, message_id: messageId }, timeoutMs);
  if ('problem' in result) return result;
  const poll = result.result as { options?: { voter_count?: unknown }[]; total_voter_count?: unknown } | null;
  const voters = poll?.options?.map((o) => o.voter_count);
  if (!voters || !voters.every((n): n is number => typeof n === 'number') || typeof poll?.total_voter_count !== 'number') {
    return { problem: 'Telegram answered without vote counts' };
  }
  return { voters, total: poll.total_voter_count };
}

/** A JSON Bot API call with the same 429 retry and timeout as sendTelegramPhoto; returns Telegram's `result`, or what went wrong. */
async function callTelegram(
  deps: Pick<Deps, 'fetch' | 'sleep'>,
  token: string,
  method: string,
  body: Record<string, unknown>,
  timeoutMs: number,
): Promise<{ result: unknown } | { problem: string }> {
  const post = () =>
    deps.fetch(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${token}/${method}`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs),
    });
  try {
    const res = await withRetryAfter(deps, post);
    if (!res.ok) return { problem: `Telegram returned HTTP ${res.status}${await description(res)}` };
    return { result: ((await res.json()) as { result?: unknown }).result ?? null };
  } catch (error) {
    return { problem: failedRequest(error) };
  }
}

async function withRetryAfter(deps: Pick<Deps, 'sleep'>, post: () => Promise<Response>): Promise<Response> {
  const res = await post();
  if (res.status !== 429) return res;
  await deps.sleep(Math.min(await retryAfter(res), TELEGRAM_RETRY_CAP_S) * 1000);
  return post();
}

const failedRequest = (error: unknown) => `Telegram request failed (${error instanceof Error ? error.name : 'unknown error'})`;
```

```ts
import type { MomentsBody } from '@ccip-dev/core/moments';
import { NETWORKS } from '@ccip-dev/core/testing';
import { env } from 'cloudflare:workers';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import type { Tagger } from '../src/broadcasts/tags';
import * as store from '../src/store';
import { harness, resetStorage, seedRegistry } from './helpers';

const TAGS: Tagger = {
  chain: (s) => ({ name: NAMES[s]?.[0] ?? s, x: NAMES[s]?.[1] ?? null }),
  sender: (key) => ({ name: key, x: null }),
  tokenProject: (key) => (key === '2:0xp' ? { name: 'Huma', x: 'humafinance' } : null),
  groupProject: (group) => (group === 'usdat' ? { name: 'Saturn', x: 'saturn' } : null),
};
const board = (key: string, rank: number, previous: number | null, value: number, label: string | null = null) => ({ key, label, value, rank, previous_rank: previous });
const MOMENTS: MomentsBody = {
  network: { chains: 93, lanes: 933 },
  arrivals: {
    chains: [{ selector: '9', day: '2026-10-11', number: 93, partners: ['1', '2'], messages: 38, usd: 412_500 }],
    lanes: [{ key: '1>9', day: '2026-10-11', messages: 30, usd: 400_000 }, { key: '2>3', day: '2026-10-07', messages: 3, usd: 900 }],

  },

    {

        partners: ['1', '4', '3'], token: { key: '2:0xc', label: 'USDC', usd: 41.2e6 },

  ],
  countdowns: [{ metric: 'fees', threshold: 2.5e6, total: 2.43e6, per_day: 11_600, days_left: 7, previous: { threshold: 2e6, day: '2026-07-20' } }],
  polls: [
    {
      week: '2026-10-05', question_kind: 'fee_chain', question: 'Which chain will pay the most CCIP fees this week?', options: [{ key: '2', label: 'Base' }, { key: 'other', label: 'Another chain' }],
      answer: '2', outcome: { key: '2', label: 'Base', value: 1_800, change_pct: null }, votes: [38, 62], total_votes: 13,

  ],
};
const SRC: MomentsSource = { moments: MOMENTS, tags: TAGS };

describe('momentFacts', () => {
  it('welcomes a new chain with its number, first-day totals and partners as players', () => {
    expect(momentFacts(SRC, { kind: 'welcome', selector: '9' })).toEqual({
      kind: 'welcome', selector: '9', chain: { name: 'Tempo', x: null }, number: 93, day: '2026-10-11', messages: 38, usd: 412_500,
      partners: [{ name: 'Ethereum', x: 'ethereum' }, { name: 'Base', x: 'base' }],
    });
  });

  it('names the new #1, the chain it took the top spot from, and the climbers by board', () => {
    expect(momentFacts(SRC, { kind: 'movers', monday: '2026-10-05' })).toEqual({
      kind: 'movers',
      monday: '2026-10-05',
      headline: { board: 'fee_chains', name: { kind: 'chain', player: { name: 'Base', x: 'base' } }, rank: 1, gain: 1, value: 1_800 },
      newLeader: true,
      dethroned: { kind: 'chain', player: { name: 'BNB Chain', x: 'BNBCHAIN' } },
      climbers: [
        { board: 'fee_chains', name: { kind: 'chain', player: { name: 'Arbitrum', x: 'arbitrum' } }, rank: 3, gain: 6, value: 900 },
        { board: 'tokens', name: { kind: 'token', symbol: 'syrupUSDT' }, rank: 7, gain: null, value: 1e6 },
        { board: 'lanes', name: { kind: 'lane', src: { name: 'Arbitrum', x: 'arbitrum' }, dst: { name: 'Ethereum', x: 'ethereum' } }, rank: 9, gain: null, value: 1e5 },
      ],
    });
  });

  it("gives the spotlight its chain, partners and top token, and the roundup its lanes and tokens with each token's project", () => {
    // #when
    const spotlight = momentFacts(SRC, { kind: 'spotlight', monday: '2026-10-05' });
    const roundup = momentFacts(SRC, { kind: 'roundup', monday: '2026-10-05' });
    // #then
    expect({
      spotlight: spotlight?.kind === 'spotlight' ? [spotlight.chain.name, spotlight.partners.map((p) => p.name), spotlight.token] : null,
      roundup: roundup?.kind === 'roundup' ? [roundup.lanes.map((l) => `${l.src.name} → ${l.dst.name}`), roundup.tokens.map((t) => [t.symbol, t.chain.name, t.project?.x])] : null,
    }).toEqual({
      spotlight: ['Base', ['Ethereum', 'Arbitrum', 'BNB Chain'], { symbol: 'USDC', usd: 41.2e6 }],
      roundup: [['Base → BNB Chain'], [['USDat', 'Ethereum', 'saturn'], ['PST', 'Base', 'humafinance']]],
    });
  });

  it("gives the poll this week's question and last week's result", () => {
    // #when
    const facts = momentFacts(SRC, { kind: 'poll', monday: '2026-10-12' });
    // #then
    expect(facts?.kind === 'poll' ? [facts.poll.question_kind, facts.last?.answer, facts.last?.votes] : null).toEqual(['fees_change', '2', [38, 62]]);
  });

  it('has no facts for a moment the data does not hold', () => {
    expect([
      momentFacts(SRC, { kind: 'welcome', selector: '1' }),
      momentFacts(SRC, { kind: 'countdown', metric: 'fees', threshold: 5e6 }),
      momentFacts(SRC, { kind: 'movers', monday: '2026-09-28' }),
      momentFacts(SRC, { kind: 'spotlight', monday: '2026-09-28' }),
      momentFacts(SRC, { kind: 'roundup', monday: '2026-09-28' }),
      momentFacts(SRC, { kind: 'poll', monday: '2026-10-19' }),
    ]).toEqual([null, null, null, null, null, null]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Tag a token group's project.**

A New on CCIP token is a registry group (its key is the group id) or a token outside the registry (`chain:address`). The tagger already resolves groups for its entries, so a group needs only a lookup.

In `worker/src/broadcasts/tags.ts`, add `groupProject` to `Tagger`. Replace:

```ts
  tokenProject(key: string): Player | null;
}
```

with:

```ts
  tokenProject(key: string): Player | null;
  /** The project of a registry token group, through any of its tokens' entries. */
  groupProject(group: string): Player | null;
}
```

Add it to the object `createTagger` returns. Replace:

```ts
    tokenProject: (key) => projects.get(key) ?? null,
  };
```

with:

```ts
    tokenProject: (key) => projects.get(key) ?? null,
    groupProject: (group) => {
      const match = entries.find((e) => groupOf(e.selector, e.address) === group);
      return match ? { name: match.project, x: match.x } : null;
    },
  };
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the facts.**

Moments are read from D1 through the builder that writes `moments.json` (as of yesterday), so a caption and its card show the same numbers. Lanes in movers and New on CCIP are named, not tagged, as the approved copy does; the copy decides how many players to tag.

Create `worker/src/broadcasts/moment-facts.ts`:

```ts
import { addDays, dayOf } from '@ccip-dev/core';
import type { MomentTarget } from '@ccip-dev/core/broadcasts';
import { moversOf, roundupOf, type BoardName, type Climber, type Countdown, type MomentsBody } from '@ccip-dev/core/moments';
import { shortAddress } from '@ccip-dev/core/names';
import type { BoardEntry, Poll, Spotlight } from '@ccip-dev/core/public';
import type { RunContext } from '../context';
import { buildMoments } from '../moments';
import { historyDays } from '../publish';
import { createTagger, type Player, type Tagger } from './tags';

export interface WelcomeFacts {
  kind: 'welcome';
  selector: string;
  chain: Player;
  number: number;
  day: string;
  messages: number;
  usd: number;
  /** By first-day value. */
  partners: Player[];
}

export interface CountdownFacts {
  kind: 'countdown';
  countdown: Countdown;
}

export type MoverName = { kind: 'chain'; player: Player } | { kind: 'token'; symbol: string } | { kind: 'lane'; src: Player; dst: Player };

export interface Mover {
  board: BoardName;
  name: MoverName;
  rank: number;
  /** Places gained; null for an entry from outside the top 10. */
  gain: number | null;
  /** Fees for the fee board, value moved for the others. */
  value: number;
}

export interface MoversFacts {
  kind: 'movers';
  monday: string;
  /** The new #1 when there is one, otherwise the top climber. */
  headline: Mover;
  newLeader: boolean;
  /** The #1 the week before, for a new #1. */
  dethroned: MoverName | null;
  climbers: Mover[];
}

export interface SpotlightFacts {
  kind: 'spotlight';
  monday: string;
  chain: Player;
  spotlight: Spotlight;
  partners: Player[];
  token: { symbol: string; usd: number } | null;
}

export interface RoundupFacts {
  kind: 'roundup';
  monday: string;
  /** Every new lane and token of the week, by first-day value; the copy decides how many to name. */
  lanes: { key: string; src: Player; dst: Player; usd: number }[];
  tokens: { key: string; symbol: string; chain: Player; project: Player | null }[];
  network: MomentsBody['network'];
}

export interface PollFacts {
  kind: 'poll';
  monday: string;
  poll: Poll;
  /** Last week's poll, when one was sent. */
  last: Poll | null;
}

export type MomentFacts = WelcomeFacts | CountdownFacts | MoversFacts | SpotlightFacts | RoundupFacts | PollFacts;

const MOMENT_FACT_KINDS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set<MomentFacts['kind']>(['welcome', 'countdown', 'movers', 'spotlight', 'roundup', 'poll']);

export function isMomentFacts(facts: { kind: string }): facts is MomentFacts {
  return MOMENT_FACT_KINDS.has(facts.kind);
}

export interface MomentsSource {
  moments: MomentsBody;
  tags: Tagger;
}

/** The moments as of yesterday, read from D1 through the builder that writes moments.json, so a caption and its card agree. */
export async function loadMomentsSource(c: RunContext): Promise<MomentsSource> {
  const lastDay = addDays(dayOf(c.deps.now()), -1);
  const moments = await buildMoments(c, { lastDay, days: await historyDays(c) });
  const tokenKeys = moments.arrivals.tokens.filter((t) => t.key.includes(':')).map((t) => t.key);
  return { moments, tags: await createTagger(c, tokenKeys) };
}

const addressOf = (key: string) => key.slice(key.indexOf(':') + 1);

function moverName(tags: Tagger, board: BoardName, entry: BoardEntry): MoverName {
  if (board === 'fee_chains') return { kind: 'chain', player: tags.chain(entry.key) };
  if (board === 'tokens') return { kind: 'token', symbol: entry.label ?? shortAddress(addressOf(entry.key)) };
  const [src = '', dst = ''] = entry.key.split('>');
  return { kind: 'lane', src: tags.chain(src), dst: tags.chain(dst) };
}

const mover = (tags: Tagger, c: Climber): Mover => ({ board: c.board, name: moverName(tags, c.board, c.entry), rank: c.entry.rank, gain: c.gain, value: c.entry.value });

function moversFacts(src: MomentsSource, monday: string): MoversFacts | null {
  const movers = moversOf(src.moments.weeks, monday);
  if (movers === null) return null;
  const climbers = movers.climbers.map((c) => mover(src.tags, c));
  const h = movers.headline;
  if (h.kind === 'climber') return { kind: 'movers', monday, headline: mover(src.tags, h.climber), newLeader: false, dethroned: null, climbers };
  return {
    kind: 'movers',
    monday,
    headline: mover(src.tags, { board: h.board, entry: h.entry, gain: h.entry.previous_rank === null ? null : h.entry.previous_rank - 1 }),
    newLeader: true,
    dethroned: h.dethroned ? moverName(src.tags, h.board, h.dethroned) : null,
    climbers,
  };
}

function roundupFacts(src: MomentsSource, monday: string): RoundupFacts | null {
  const roundup = roundupOf(src.moments, monday);
  if (roundup === null) return null;
  const { tags } = src;
  return {
    kind: 'roundup',
    monday,
    lanes: roundup.lanes.map((l) => {
      const [a = '', b = ''] = l.key.split('>');
      return { key: l.key, src: tags.chain(a), dst: tags.chain(b), usd: l.usd };
    }),
    tokens: roundup.tokens.map((t) => ({
      key: t.key,
      symbol: t.symbol ?? shortAddress(addressOf(t.key)),
      chain: tags.chain(t.chains[0] ?? ''),
      project: t.key.includes(':') ? tags.tokenProject(t.key) : tags.groupProject(t.key),
    })),
    network: src.moments.network,
  };
}

/** The facts of one moment, or null when the data does not hold it. */
export function momentFacts(src: MomentsSource, target: MomentTarget): MomentFacts | null {
  const { moments, tags } = src;
  switch (target.kind) {
    case 'welcome': {
      const a = moments.arrivals.chains.find((c) => c.selector === target.selector);
      return a ? { kind: 'welcome', selector: a.selector, chain: tags.chain(a.selector), number: a.number, day: a.day, messages: a.messages, usd: a.usd, partners: a.partners.map((p) => tags.chain(p)) } : null;
    }
    case 'countdown': {
      const countdown = moments.countdowns.find((c) => c.metric === target.metric && c.threshold === target.threshold);
      return countdown ? { kind: 'countdown', countdown } : null;
    }
    case 'movers':
      return moversFacts(src, target.monday);
    case 'spotlight': {
      const s = moments.weeks.find((w) => w.week === target.monday)?.spotlight;
      if (!s) return null;
      const token = s.token ? { symbol: s.token.label ?? shortAddress(addressOf(s.token.key)), usd: s.token.usd } : null;
      return { kind: 'spotlight', monday: target.monday, chain: tags.chain(s.selector), spotlight: s, partners: s.partners.map((p) => tags.chain(p)), token };
    }
    case 'roundup':
      return roundupFacts(src, target.monday);
    case 'poll': {
      const poll = moments.polls.find((p) => p.week === target.monday);
      return poll ? { kind: 'poll', monday: target.monday, poll, last: moments.polls.find((p) => p.week === addDays(target.monday, -7)) ?? null } : null;
    }
  }
}
```

```ts
        'Climbers: @arbitrum ▲6 to #3 by fees · syrupUSDT ▲9 to #7 by value · Ink → Ethereum new at #9 by value\n#Chainlink $LINK',

        'Top partners: @ethereum · @arbitrum · @BNBCHAIN\n#Chainlink $LINK',
      'New on #CCIP this week\nLanes: Ethereum → Tempo · Base → Monad · Plasma → Arc\nTokens: USDat on Ethereum (@saturn) · PST on Base (@humafinance)\n' +
        'CCIP now runs 933 lanes between 93 chains',
    ]);
  });

  it('leads with the top climber when no board has a new #1, and words an entrant as new in the top 10', () => {
    // #given
    const climber: MoversFacts = { ...MOVERS, newLeader: false, dethroned: null, headline: MOVERS.climbers[2]!, climbers: [MOVERS.climbers[0]!] };
    // #then
    expect(composeMomentCopy(climber).xText).toBe('Top climber on #CCIP last week: Ink → Ethereum new at #9 by value\nAlso climbing: @arbitrum ▲6 to #3 by fees\n#Chainlink $LINK');
  });

  it('gives the poll no X text, since it has no X draft', () => {
    expect(composeMomentCopy(POLL).xText).toBe('');
  });
});

describe('moment Telegram captions', () => {
  it('says the same in fuller sentences, with bold numbers and plain names', () => {
    expect([WELCOME, COUNTDOWN, MOVERS, SPOTLIGHT, ROUNDUP].map((f) => composeMomentCopy(f).telegramHtml)).toEqual([
      '<b>Welcome to CCIP, Tempo</b>\nTempo is chain <b>#93</b> on CCIP. Its first message was on Sun, Oct 11, 2026.\n' +
        'First day: <b>38</b> messages and <b>$412.5K</b> moved.\nConnected to Ethereum, Base and Arbitrum.\n\n#CCIP #Chainlink $LINK',
      '<b>CCIP countdown: $2.5M in fees all time</b>\nAbout <b>7 days</b> away at the pace of the last 7 days, about <b>$11.6K</b> a day.\n' +
        '<b>$2.4M</b> so far.\nLast milestone: $2M on Mon, Jul 20, 2026.\n\n#CCIP #Chainlink $LINK',
      '<b>CCIP leaderboards · Oct 5–11</b>\nNew #1: Base paid the most fees last week (<b>$1.8K</b>), taking the top spot from BNB Chain.\n' +
        'Climbers: Arbitrum ▲6 to #3 by fees · syrupUSDT ▲9 to #7 by value · Ink → Ethereum new at #9 by value\n\n#CCIP #Chainlink $LINK',
      '<b>Chain spotlight: Base</b>\nBase is the fastest-growing chain on CCIP this week, and #2 by messages.\n' +
        '<b>3,412</b> messages (▲ 85% vs last week) · <b>$96.4M</b> moved (▲ 41%) · <b>$1.8K</b> in fees (▲ 27%)\n' +
        'Top partners: Ethereum · Arbitrum · BNB Chain\nTop token: USDC · $41.2M moved\n\n#CCIP #Chainlink $LINK',
      '<b>New on CCIP this week · Oct 5–9</b>\nNew lanes: Ethereum → Tempo · Base → Monad · Plasma → Arc\n' +
        'New tokens: USDat on Ethereum (Saturn) · PST on Base (Huma Finance)\nCCIP now runs <b>933</b> lanes between <b>93</b> chains.\n\n#CCIP',
    ]);
  });

  it("captions the poll card with last week's answer and how many called it, then this week's question", () => {
    expect(composeMomentCopy(POLL).telegramHtml).toBe("🔮 Last week: Base paid the most fees ($1.8K). 38% of you called it.\nThis week: will CCIP fees beat last week's $8.0K? Vote below.");
  });

  it('leaves out "called it" for a poll that could not be closed, and the last-week line for the first poll', () => {
    // #given last week's poll has its answer but no votes; then there was no poll last week
    const unclosed = { ...POLL, last: { ...LAST, votes: null, total_votes: null } };
    const first: PollFacts = { ...POLL, poll: { ...LAST, week: '2026-10-12', answer: null, outcome: null, votes: null, total_votes: null }, last: null };
    // #then the fee-chain question drops its "this week" after "This week:"
    expect([composeMomentCopy(unclosed).telegramHtml, composeMomentCopy(first).telegramHtml]).toEqual([
      "🔮 Last week: Base paid the most fees ($1.8K).\nThis week: will CCIP fees beat last week's $8.0K? Vote below.",
      '🔮 This week: which chain will pay the most CCIP fees? Vote below.',
    ]);
  });

  it('words a fee-question answer as a change, and a lane answer by value', () => {
    // #given
    const fees: Poll = { ...POLL.poll, week: '2026-10-05', answer: 'up', outcome: { key: 'fees', label: 'CCIP fees', value: 9_000, change_pct: 12.5 }, votes: [50, 25, 25], total_votes: 4 };
    const lane: Poll = { ...LAST, question_kind: 'lane', options: [{ key: '1>2', label: 'Ethereum → Base' }, { key: 'other', label: 'Another lane' }], answer: 'other', outcome: { key: '3>1', label: 'Arbitrum → Ethereum', value: 9.6e6, change_pct: null }, votes: [70, 30] };
    // #when
    const lines = [fees, lane].map((last) => composeMomentCopy({ ...POLL, last }).telegramHtml.split('\n')[0]);
    // #then
    expect(lines).toEqual(['🔮 Last week: CCIP fees rose 13% to $9.0K. 50% of you called it.', '🔮 Last week: Arbitrum → Ethereum moved the most value ($9.6M). 30% of you called it.']);
  });
});

describe('moment handles', () => {
  it('writes at most 5 handles, in the order players appear, counting the fixed ones; a repeated handle is free', () => {
    // #given one fixed @chainlink
    const name = handleWriter(1);
    // #when
    const written = ['base', 'BNBCHAIN', 'base', 'arbitrum', 'ethereum', 'optimism'].map((x) => name(p(x.toUpperCase(), x)));
    // #then
    expect(written).toEqual(['@base', '@BNBCHAIN', '@base', '@arbitrum', '@ethereum', 'OPTIMISM']);
  });

  it('names the projects past the fifth handle in New on CCIP by their names', () => {
    // #given six new tokens whose projects all have handles, and a lane
    const tokens = Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => ({ key: `t${i}`, symbol: `T${i}`, chain: p('Base', 'base'), project: p(`Project ${i}`, `project${i}`) }));
    // #when
    const text = composeMomentCopy({ ...ROUNDUP, tokens }).xText;
    // #then five tokens are named, then "+1 more"
    expect({ handles: text.match(/@\w+/g), more: text.includes('· +1 more') }).toEqual({ handles: ['@project0', '@project1', '@project2', '@project3', '@project4'], more: true });
  });
});

describe('moment fitting', () => {
  it('drops the climbers line first, then the closing hashtag line, to fit 280', () => {
    // #given long lane names among the climbers, then a long headline too
    const lane = { kind: 'lane' as const, src: p(long(80)), dst: p(long(80)) };
    const longClimbers: MoversFacts = { ...MOVERS, climbers: MOVERS.climbers.map((c) => ({ ...c, name: lane })) };
    const longHeadline: MoversFacts = { ...longClimbers, dethroned: { kind: 'chain', player: p(long(180)) } };
    // #when
    const kept = (f: MoversFacts) => composeMomentCopy(f).xText.split('\n').map((l) => l.split(':')[0]);
    // #then
    expect([kept(longClimbers), kept(longHeadline)]).toEqual([['New #1 on #CCIP', '#Chainlink $LINK'], ['New #1 on #CCIP']]);
  });

  it('names fewer lanes in New on CCIP when 5 would not fit, with "+N more" for the rest, before dropping the network line', () => {
    // #given eight new lanes between chains with long names
    const lanes = Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, i) => ({ key: `l${i}`, src: p(`Optimism Superchain ${i}`), dst: p(`Arbitrum Nova Orbit ${i}`), usd: 8 - i }));
    // #when
    const text = composeMomentCopy({ ...ROUNDUP, lanes }).xText;
    const [, lanesLine = ''] = text.split('\n');
    // #then three lanes fit beside the tokens; the network line is the closing line, so it goes
    expect({ fits: xLength(text) <= X_MAX, lanes: lanesLine.split(' → ').length - 1, more: lanesLine.endsWith('· +5 more'), lines: text.split('\n').length }).toEqual({
      fits: true, lanes: 3, more: true, lines: 3,
    });
  });

  it('keeps every moment within 280 on X and 1,024 on Telegram with long names everywhere', () => {
    // #given 40-character names on every player and symbol, a 300-character question and a 100-character answer
    const n = (x: string | null = null) => p(long(40), x);
    const facts = [
      { ...WELCOME, chain: n('tempo'), partners: [n(), n(), n()] },
      { ...SPOTLIGHT, chain: n('base'), partners: [n(), n(), n()], token: { symbol: long(40), usd: 1 } },
      { ...ROUNDUP, lanes: ROUNDUP.lanes.map((l) => ({ ...l, src: n(), dst: n() })), tokens: ROUNDUP.tokens.map((t) => ({ ...t, symbol: long(40), chain: n() })) },
      { ...POLL, poll: { ...POLL.poll, question: long(300, 'Q') }, last: { ...LAST, outcome: { ...LAST.outcome!, label: long(100) } } },
    ];
    // #when
    const copies = facts.map((f) => composeMomentCopy(f));
    // #then
    expect(copies.every((c) => xLength(c.xText) <= X_MAX && c.telegramHtml.length <= TELEGRAM_CAPTION_MAX)).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('moment links', () => {
  it('never turns a token symbol with an @ or a domain-like name into a mention or a link, on X or Telegram', () => {
    // #given a token symbol that tries to mention someone, and chain and project names that read as domains
    const roundup: RoundupFacts = {
      ...ROUNDUP,
      lanes: [{ key: 'x', src: p('pump.fun'), dst: p('Base', 'base'), usd: 1 }],
      tokens: [{ key: 'g', symbol: 'AIRDROP@scam', chain: p('xStocks.fi'), project: p('Ondo.finance') }],
    };
    const movers: MoversFacts = { ...MOVERS, climbers: [{ ...MOVERS.climbers[1]!, name: { kind: 'token', symbol: 'claim.io' } }] };
    const poll: PollFacts = { ...POLL, last: { ...LAST, outcome: { ...LAST.outcome!, label: 'pump.fun' } } };
    // #when
    const texts = [roundup, movers, poll].flatMap((f) => Object.values(composeMomentCopy(f)));
    // #then
    expect({
      links: texts.filter((t) => LINKLIKE.test(t)),
      mention: texts.some((t) => /@scam/.test(t)),
      neutralized: texts.some((t) => t.includes('AIRDROP@\u200bscam')) && texts.some((t) => t.includes('pump\u2024fun')),
    }).toEqual({ links: [], mention: false, neutralized: true });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the moment copy.**

It is the approved X text of spec §5.3, with these choices (see "Decisions" above): a welcome names its first partner "with" (its direction is not known), an entrant to a top 10 reads "new at #7 by value", every climber names its board, and lanes and token symbols are written as names. `handleWriter` caps a post at 5 @handles counting a closing @chainlink; a repeated handle is free. New on CCIP names up to 5 of each and shortens the lists, with "+N more", before it would drop its network line. The poll has only a Telegram caption.

Create `worker/src/broadcasts/moment-copy.ts`:

```ts
import { addDays } from '@ccip-dev/core';
import { formatCount, formatFeeTotal, formatShare, formatUsd } from '@ccip-dev/core/format';
import { calledShare, type BoardName, type Countdown } from '@ccip-dev/core/moments';
import { formatThresholdUsd, type MilestoneMetric } from '@ccip-dev/core/records';
import type { Poll } from '@ccip-dev/core/public';
import { arrow, bold, fitLines, longDay, noLinks, plural, shortDay, TELEGRAM_CAPTION_MAX, tgName, tgText, weekRange, X_MAX, type Copy, type Line } from './copy';
import type { CountdownFacts, MomentFacts, Mover, MoverName, MoversFacts, PollFacts, RoundupFacts, SpotlightFacts, WelcomeFacts } from './moment-facts';
import type { Player } from './tags';
import { xLength } from './x-length';

/** When a moment post is too long, its detail line (climbers, partners) goes first, then its closing line. */
export const MOMENT_DROP = { detail: 1, closing: 2 } as const;
export const MAX_HANDLES = 5;
export const ROUNDUP_SHOWN = 5;

type Name = (p: Player) => string;

/**
 * Writes players as @handles in the order the post names them, until the post holds 5 handles counting `reserved` fixed
 * ones (such as a closing @chainlink); later players are plain names. Naming a handle a second time costs nothing.
 */
export function handleWriter(reserved: number): Name {
  const used = new Set<string>();
  return (p) => {
    const handle = p.x?.toLowerCase();
    if (p.x && handle && (used.has(handle) || used.size + reserved < MAX_HANDLES)) {
      used.add(handle);
      return `@${p.x}`;
    }
    return noLinks(p.name);
  };
}

const plain: Name = (p) => noLinks(p.name);
const fitsX = (text: string) => xLength(text) <= X_MAX;
const fitsTelegram = (text: string) => text.length <= TELEGRAM_CAPTION_MAX;
const CLOSING_TG = '#CCIP #Chainlink $LINK';

function andList(items: readonly string[]): string {
  return items.length <= 1 ? (items[0] ?? '') : `${items.slice(0, -1).join(', ')} and ${items.at(-1)}`;
}

function shownList<T>(items: readonly T[], shown: number, render: (item: T) => string): string {
  const more = items.length - shown;
  return [...items.slice(0, shown).map(render), ...(more > 0 ? [`+${more} more`] : [])].join(' · ');
}

/** "Oct 5–9", or "Sep 28 – Oct 2" across a month. */
function dayRange(from: string, to: string): string {
  return from.slice(0, 7) === to.slice(0, 7) ? `${shortDay(from)}–${Number(to.slice(8))}` : `${shortDay(from)} – ${shortDay(to)}`;
}

function welcomeX(f: WelcomeFacts): Line[] {
  const name = handleWriter(1);
  const chain = name(f.chain);
  const partner = f.partners[0] ? `, with ${name(f.partners[0])}` : '';
  return [
    { text: `🎉 Welcome to #CCIP, ${chain}` },
    { text: `Chain #${f.number} on CCIP: first message on ${shortDay(f.day)}${partner}` },
    { text: `First day: ${plural(f.messages, 'message')} · ${formatUsd(f.usd)} moved` },
    { text: '@chainlink', drop: MOMENT_DROP.closing },
  ];
}

function welcomeTg(f: WelcomeFacts): Line[] {
  const chain = tgName(f.chain);
  return [
    { text: bold(`Welcome to CCIP, ${chain}`) },
    { text: `${chain} is chain ${bold(`#${f.number}`)} on CCIP. Its first message was on ${longDay(f.day)}.` },
    { text: `First day: ${bold(formatCount(f.messages))} message${f.messages === 1 ? '' : 's'} and ${bold(formatUsd(f.usd))} moved.` },
    ...(f.partners.length > 0 ? [{ text: `Connected to ${andList(f.partners.slice(0, 3).map(tgName))}.`, drop: MOMENT_DROP.detail }] : []),
    { text: '' },
    { text: CLOSING_TG, drop: MOMENT_DROP.closing },
  ];
}

const COUNTDOWN_X: Record<MilestoneMetric, (t: number) => string> = {
  messages: (t) => `${formatCount(t)} all-time #CCIP messages`,
  value: (t) => `${formatThresholdUsd(t)} moved over #CCIP all time`,
  fees: (t) => `${formatThresholdUsd(t)} in all-time #CCIP fees`,
};

const COUNTDOWN_TG: Record<MilestoneMetric, (t: number) => string> = {
  messages: (t) => `${formatCount(t)} messages all time`,
  value: (t) => `${formatThresholdUsd(t)} moved all time`,
  fees: (t) => `${formatThresholdUsd(t)} in fees all time`,
};

const amount = (c: Pick<Countdown, 'metric'>, v: number) => (c.metric === 'messages' ? formatCount(v) : formatUsd(v));
const threshold = (c: Pick<Countdown, 'metric'>, v: number) => (c.metric === 'messages' ? formatCount(v) : formatThresholdUsd(v));

function countdownX({ countdown: c }: CountdownFacts): Line[] {
  return [
    { text: `⏳ ${COUNTDOWN_X[c.metric](c.threshold)} is about ${plural(c.days_left, 'day')} away` },
    { text: `${amount(c, c.total)} now, adding about ${amount(c, c.per_day)} a day` },
    { text: '@chainlink', drop: MOMENT_DROP.closing },
  ];
}

function countdownTg({ countdown: c }: CountdownFacts): Line[] {
  return [
    { text: bold(`CCIP countdown: ${tgText(COUNTDOWN_TG[c.metric](c.threshold))}`) },
    { text: `About ${bold(plural(c.days_left, 'day'))} away at the pace of the last 7 days, about ${bold(amount(c, c.per_day))} a day.` },
    { text: `${bold(amount(c, c.total))} so far.` },
    ...(c.previous ? [{ text: `Last milestone: ${threshold(c, c.previous.threshold)} on ${longDay(c.previous.day)}.`, drop: MOMENT_DROP.detail }] : []),
    { text: '' },
    { text: CLOSING_TG, drop: MOMENT_DROP.closing },
  ];
}

const BY: Record<BoardName, string> = { fee_chains: 'by fees', tokens: 'by value', lanes: 'by value' };
const VERB: Record<BoardName, string> = { fee_chains: 'paid the most fees', tokens: 'moved the most value', lanes: 'moved the most value' };
const boardValue = (m: Pick<Mover, 'board' | 'value'>) => (m.board === 'fee_chains' ? formatFeeTotal(m.value) : formatUsd(m.value));

function moverName(n: MoverName, chain: Name, text: (s: string) => string): string {
  if (n.kind === 'chain') return chain(n.player);
  if (n.kind === 'token') return text(n.symbol);
  return `${text(n.src.name)} → ${text(n.dst.name)}`;
}

function climb(m: Mover, name: string): string {
  return m.gain === null ? `${name} new at #${m.rank} ${BY[m.board]}` : `${name} ▲${m.gain} to #${m.rank} ${BY[m.board]}`;
}

function moversLines(f: MoversFacts, chain: Name, text: (s: string) => string, tg: boolean): Line[] {
  const name = (n: MoverName) => moverName(n, chain, text);
  const headline = f.newLeader
    ? `${name(f.headline.name)} ${VERB[f.headline.board]} last week (${tg ? bold(boardValue(f.headline)) : boardValue(f.headline)})${f.dethroned ? `, taking the top spot from ${name(f.dethroned)}` : ''}`
    : climb(f.headline, name(f.headline.name));
  const climbers = f.climbers.map((m) => climb(m, name(m.name))).join(' · ');
  const lead = f.newLeader ? 'Climbers' : 'Also climbing';
  if (tg) {
    return [
      { text: bold(`CCIP leaderboards · ${weekRange(f.monday)}`) },
      { text: `${f.newLeader ? 'New #1: ' : 'Top climber: '}${headline}.` },
      ...(climbers ? [{ text: `${lead}: ${climbers}`, drop: MOMENT_DROP.detail }] : []),
      { text: '' },
      { text: CLOSING_TG, drop: MOMENT_DROP.closing },
    ];
  }
  return [
    { text: f.newLeader ? `New #1 on #CCIP: ${headline}` : `Top climber on #CCIP last week: ${headline}` },
    ...(climbers ? [{ text: `${lead}: ${climbers}`, drop: MOMENT_DROP.detail }] : []),
    { text: '#Chainlink $LINK', drop: MOMENT_DROP.closing },
  ];
}

const pctOf = (now: number, before: number | null) => (before === null || before === 0 ? null : ((now - before) / before) * 100);

function spotlightX(f: SpotlightFacts): Line[] {
  const name = handleWriter(0);
  const s = f.spotlight;
  const change = arrow(pctOf(s.messages, s.previous_messages));
  const figures = [
    `${plural(s.messages, 'message')}${change ? ` (${change} vs last week)` : ''}`,
    `${formatUsd(s.usd)} moved`,
    ...(s.fee_usd === null ? [] : [`${formatFeeTotal(s.fee_usd)} in fees`]),
  ];
  return [
    { text: `Chain spotlight: ${name(f.chain)} is the fastest-growing chain on #CCIP this week` },
    { text: figures.join(' · ') },
    ...(f.partners.length > 0 ? [{ text: `Top partners: ${f.partners.map(name).join(' · ')}`, drop: MOMENT_DROP.detail }] : []),
    { text: '#Chainlink $LINK', drop: MOMENT_DROP.closing },
  ];
}

function spotlightTg(f: SpotlightFacts): Line[] {
  const s = f.spotlight;
  const chain = tgName(f.chain);
  const change = (now: number, before: number | null, suffix = '') => {
    const a = arrow(pctOf(now, before));
    return a ? ` (${a}${suffix})` : '';
  };
  const figures = [
    `${bold(formatCount(s.messages))} messages${change(s.messages, s.previous_messages, ' vs last week')}`,
    `${bold(formatUsd(s.usd))} moved${change(s.usd, s.previous_usd)}`,
    ...(s.fee_usd === null ? [] : [`${bold(formatFeeTotal(s.fee_usd))} in fees${change(s.fee_usd, s.previous_fee_usd)}`]),
  ];
  return [
    { text: bold(`Chain spotlight: ${chain}`) },
    { text: `${chain} is the fastest-growing chain on CCIP this week, and #${s.rank} by messages.` },
    { text: figures.join(' · ') },
    ...(f.partners.length > 0 ? [{ text: `Top partners: ${f.partners.map(tgName).join(' · ')}`, drop: MOMENT_DROP.detail }] : []),
    ...(f.token ? [{ text: `Top token: ${tgText(f.token.symbol)} · ${formatUsd(f.token.usd)} moved`, drop: MOMENT_DROP.detail }] : []),
    { text: '' },
    { text: CLOSING_TG, drop: MOMENT_DROP.closing },
  ];
}

function roundupX(f: RoundupFacts, shown: number): Line[] {
  const name = handleWriter(0);
  const lanes = shownList(f.lanes, shown, (l) => `${plain(l.src)} → ${plain(l.dst)}`);
  const tokens = shownList(f.tokens, shown, (t) => `${noLinks(t.symbol)} on ${plain(t.chain)}${t.project ? ` (${name(t.project)})` : ''}`);
  return [
    { text: 'New on #CCIP this week' },
    ...(lanes ? [{ text: `Lanes: ${lanes}` }] : []),
    ...(tokens ? [{ text: `Tokens: ${tokens}` }] : []),
    { text: `CCIP now runs ${formatCount(f.network.lanes)} lanes between ${formatCount(f.network.chains)} chains`, drop: MOMENT_DROP.closing },
  ];
}

/** Names up to 5 lanes and 5 tokens, fewer when X's 280 needs it, with "+N more" for the rest. */
function roundupXText(f: RoundupFacts): string {
  for (let shown = ROUNDUP_SHOWN; shown > 1; shown--) {
    const lines = roundupX(f, shown);
    if (fitsX(lines.filter((l) => l.drop === undefined).map((l) => l.text).join('\n'))) return fitLines(lines, fitsX);
  }
  return fitLines(roundupX(f, 1), fitsX);
}

function roundupTg(f: RoundupFacts): Line[] {
  const lanes = shownList(f.lanes, ROUNDUP_SHOWN, (l) => `${tgName(l.src)} → ${tgName(l.dst)}`);
  const tokens = shownList(f.tokens, ROUNDUP_SHOWN, (t) => `${tgText(t.symbol)} on ${tgName(t.chain)}${t.project ? ` (${tgName(t.project)})` : ''}`);
  return [
    { text: bold(`New on CCIP this week · ${dayRange(f.monday, addDays(f.monday, 4))}`) },
    ...(lanes ? [{ text: `New lanes: ${lanes}` }] : []),
    ...(tokens ? [{ text: `New tokens: ${tokens}` }] : []),
    { text: `CCIP now runs ${bold(formatCount(f.network.lanes))} lanes between ${bold(formatCount(f.network.chains))} chains.` },
    { text: '' },
    { text: '#CCIP', drop: MOMENT_DROP.closing },
  ];
}

/** "Base paid the most fees ($1.8K)", or for the fee question "CCIP fees rose 12% to $9.0K". */
function outcomeText(poll: Poll): string | null {
  const o = poll.outcome;
  if (o === null) return null;
  if (poll.question_kind === 'fees_change') {
    const pct = o.change_pct ?? 0;
    return `CCIP fees ${pct >= 0 ? 'rose' : 'fell'} ${formatShare(Math.abs(pct))} to ${formatFeeTotal(o.value)}`;
  }
  return poll.question_kind === 'fee_chain' ? `${tgText(o.label)} paid the most fees (${formatFeeTotal(o.value)})` : `${tgText(o.label)} moved the most value (${formatUsd(o.value)})`;
}

function pollTg(f: PollFacts): Line[] {
  const outcome = f.last ? outcomeText(f.last) : null;
  const called = f.last ? calledShare(f.last) : null;
  const question = tgText(f.poll.question.replace(/ this week\?$/, '?'));
  const ask = `This week: ${question.charAt(0).toLowerCase()}${question.slice(1)} Vote below.`;
  return outcome === null
    ? [{ text: `🔮 ${ask}` }]
    : [{ text: `🔮 Last week: ${outcome}.${called === null ? '' : ` ${called}% of you called it.`}` }, { text: ask }];
}

function momentX(f: Exclude<MomentFacts, PollFacts | RoundupFacts>): Line[] {
  switch (f.kind) {
    case 'welcome':
      return welcomeX(f);
    case 'countdown':
      return countdownX(f);
    case 'movers':
      return moversLines(f, handleWriter(0), noLinks, false);
    case 'spotlight':
      return spotlightX(f);
  }
}

function momentTelegram(f: MomentFacts): Line[] {
  switch (f.kind) {
    case 'welcome':
      return welcomeTg(f);
    case 'countdown':
      return countdownTg(f);
    case 'movers':
      return moversLines(f, tgName, tgText, true);
    case 'spotlight':
      return spotlightTg(f);
    case 'roundup':
      return roundupTg(f);
    case 'poll':
      return pollTg(f);
  }
}

/** A moment's X text and Telegram caption. The poll has no X draft, so its X text is empty. */
export function composeMomentCopy(f: MomentFacts): Copy {
  const xText = f.kind === 'poll' ? '' : f.kind === 'roundup' ? roundupXText(f) : fitLines(momentX(f), fitsX);
  return { xText, telegramHtml: fitLines(momentTelegram(f), fitsTelegram) };
}
```

```ts
const NOW = '2026-10-13T00:07:00.000Z';
const DAY = '2026-10-12';
const PNG = [137, 80, 78, 71];
const card = (failing: string | null = null) =>
  fakeFetch((url) => {
    if (!url.startsWith('https://ccip.dev/og/')) return undefined;
    return failing !== null && url.includes(failing) ? new Response('down', { status: 500 }) : new Response(new Uint8Array(PNG), { headers: { 'content-type': 'image/png' } });
  });

function recorder(name: string, fails: (b: Broadcast) => boolean = () => false) {
  const sent: string[] = [];
  const output: BroadcastOutput = {
    name,
    send: async (b) => {
      if (fails(b)) return 'failed';
      sent.push(b.key);
      return 'sent';
    },
  };
  return { output, sent };
}

/** An old lane, then three chains whose first message was on Monday, Oct 12, and a fourth on Oct 13. */
async function seedArrivals(): Promise<void> {
  await seedRegistry([NETWORKS.ethereum, NETWORKS.base], []);
  const totals = (day: string, messages: number) => ({ day, messages, token_messages: 0, usd_value: messages, fee_usd: null, unique_senders: 1, median_delivery_s: null, unpriced_messages: 0 });
  const lane = (day: string, key: string) => ({ day, dim: 'lane' as Dim, key, messages: 1, usd_value: 1, fee_usd: null });
  await store.replaceDaily(env.DB, totals('2026-08-03', 100), [lane('2026-08-03', `${ETH}>${BASE}`)], NOW);
  await store.replaceDaily(env.DB, totals(DAY, 3), NEW.map((s) => lane(DAY, `${s}>${ETH}`)), NOW);
  await store.replaceDaily(env.DB, totals('2026-10-13', 1), [lane('2026-10-13', `9000000000000000009>${ETH}`)], NOW);
}

async function momentRun(o: { now?: string; day?: string; mode?: string; moments?: string; channelFails?: (b: Broadcast) => boolean; fetch?: typeof fetch }) {
  const channel = recorder('telegram-channel', o.channelFails);
  const drafts = recorder('x-draft');
  const preview = recorder('telegram-preview');
  const h = harness({ now: o.now ?? NOW, fetch: o.fetch ?? card(), env: { BROADCASTS: o.mode ?? 'on', MOMENTS: o.moments ?? 'on' } });
  await runBroadcasts(h.c, { kind: 'finalize', days: [o.day ?? DAY] }, { channel: [channel.output], drafts: [drafts.output], preview: [preview.output] });
  const isMoment = (key: string) => /^broadcast:(welcome|countdown|movers|spotlight|roundup|poll):/.test(key);
  const moments = (keys: string[]) => keys.filter(isMoment);
  return { channel: moments(channel.sent), drafts: moments(drafts.sent), preview: moments(preview.sent), reports: channel.sent.filter((k) => !isMoment(k)), alerts: h.alerts.map((a) => a.signature) };
}

describe('moments after finalize', () => {
  beforeEach(async () => {
    await seedArrivals();
    await store.setMeta(env.DB, MOMENTS_SEED_KEY, 'seeded:2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z');
  });

  it('posts at most 2 moments a day after the daily report, and the one left over on the next day', async () => {
    // #when
    const tuesday = await momentRun({});
    const wednesday = await momentRun({ now: '2026-10-14T00:07:00.000Z', day: '2026-10-13' });
    // #then the fourth chain arrived on Oct 13, so it is due on Wednesday too, behind the older welcome
    expect({ tuesday: [tuesday.reports, tuesday.channel], wednesday: wednesday.channel }).toEqual({
      tuesday: [['broadcast:daily:2026-10-12'], [welcome(NEW[0]!), welcome(NEW[1]!)]],
      wednesday: [welcome(NEW[2]!), welcome('9000000000000000009')],
    });
  });

  it('gives the hourly retry only the place a failed moment left, never a third moment', async () => {
    // #given at 00:07 the second welcome's channel post fails
    const first = await momentRun({ channelFails: (b) => b.key === welcome(NEW[1]!) });
    // #when the hourly run retries yesterday, twice
    const retry = await momentRun({ now: '2026-10-13T01:00:00.000Z' });
    const again = await momentRun({ now: '2026-10-13T02:00:00.000Z' });
    // #then the retry sends the channel post that failed, and the third welcome waits for tomorrow
    expect({ first: first.channel, retry: [retry.channel, retry.drafts], again: [again.channel, again.drafts] }).toEqual({
      first: [welcome(NEW[0]!)],
      retry: [[welcome(NEW[1]!)], []],
      again: [[], []],
    });
  });

  it('keeps posting the other moments when one moment fails, after the daily', async () => {
    // #given the first welcome's card answers 500
    const { reports, channel, alerts } = await momentRun({ fetch: card(`welcome/${NEW[0]}`) });
    // #then its place is not given away within the run; the next run retries it
    expect({ reports, channel, alerts }).toEqual({ reports: ['broadcast:daily:2026-10-12'], channel: [welcome(NEW[1]!)], alerts: [`broadcast-image:${welcome(NEW[0]!)}`] });
  });

  it('posts no moment with MOMENTS off, and sends moments only to the owner with MOMENTS drafts, while the reports post', async () => {
    // #when
    const off = await momentRun({ moments: 'off' });
    await resetStorage();
    await seedArrivals();
    await store.setMeta(env.DB, MOMENTS_SEED_KEY, 'seeded:2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z');
    const drafts = await momentRun({ moments: 'drafts' });
    // #then
    expect({ off: [off.reports.length, off.channel, off.drafts], drafts: [drafts.reports.length, drafts.channel, drafts.drafts, drafts.preview] }).toEqual({
      off: [1, [], []],
      drafts: [1, [], [welcome(NEW[0]!), welcome(NEW[1]!)], [welcome(NEW[0]!), welcome(NEW[1]!)]],
    });
  });
});

describe('the first moments run', () => {
  beforeEach(seedArrivals);

  it('marks every moment due today as seeded without posting it, and starts fresh the next day', async () => {
    // #when
    const first = await momentRun({});
    const seeded = await Promise.all(NEW.map(async (s) => (await store.getMeta(env.DB, welcome(s)))?.split(':')[0]));
    const next = await momentRun({ now: '2026-10-14T00:07:00.000Z', day: '2026-10-13' });
    // #then
    expect({ first: first.channel, seeded, seed: (await store.getMeta(env.DB, MOMENTS_SEED_KEY))?.startsWith('seeded:'), next: next.channel }).toEqual({
      first: [], seeded: ['seeded', 'seeded', 'seeded'], seed: true, next: [welcome('9000000000000000009')],
    });
  });
});
```

```ts
/** Moments post only as far as both switches allow: off in either is off, and drafts in either is drafts. */
export function momentMode(env: Pick<Env, 'BROADCASTS' | 'MOMENTS'>): BroadcastMode {
  const broadcasts = broadcastMode(env);
  const moments = broadcastMode({ BROADCASTS: env.MOMENTS });
  if (broadcasts === 'off' || moments === 'off') return 'off';
  return broadcasts === 'drafts' || moments === 'drafts' ? 'drafts' : 'on';
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Count the day's budget.**

Every alert and broadcast lives in `meta`, so the count reads key ranges of its primary key (never a `LIKE` over the table) and leaves out the `#output` rows and the poll (Review Focus: D1 key scans).

In `worker/src/store.ts`, after the `@ccip-dev/core` import, add:

```ts
import { BUDGETED_MOMENTS } from '@ccip-dev/core/broadcasts';
```

Before `knownIds`, add:

```ts
/**
 * The budgeted moments marked sent on day ?1: their own keys, not their `#output` rows. Each kind is a key range, so the
 * count reads meta's primary key instead of every alert and broadcast row.
 */
export const MOMENTS_SENT_SQL = `SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM meta
  WHERE (${BUDGETED_MOMENTS.map((kind) => `(key > 'broadcast:${kind}:' AND key < 'broadcast:${kind};')`).join(' OR ')})
    AND instr(key, '#') = 0 AND value >= 'sent:' || ?1 AND value < 'sent:' || ?2`;

export async function momentsSentOn(db: D1Database, day: string): Promise<number> {
  const row = await db.prepare(MOMENTS_SENT_SQL).bind(day, addDays(day, 1)).first<{ n: number }>();
  return row?.n ?? 0;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Plan the moments.**

Create `worker/src/broadcasts/moment-plan.ts`:

```ts
import { addDays, weekStart } from '@ccip-dev/core';
import { broadcastKey, BUDGETED_MOMENTS, type BudgetedMoment, type MomentTarget } from '@ccip-dev/core/broadcasts';
import { moversOf, roundupOf, type MomentsBody } from '@ccip-dev/core/moments';

export const MOMENTS_PER_DAY = 2;
/** A moment that misses its day is planned again on each of the next 2 days, then dropped. */
export const CARRY_DAYS = 2;
export const COUNTDOWN_DAYS = { min: 2, max: 7 } as const;
/** Days from a week's Monday to the day its moment is planned: movers on the Tuesday and the spotlight on the Thursday after it, New on CCIP on its own Saturday. */
export const WEEKLY_DUE = { movers: 8, spotlight: 10, roundup: 5 } as const;

export interface DueMoment {
  target: Extract<MomentTarget, { kind: BudgetedMoment }>;
  /** The day the moment is planned for; it stays due for CARRY_DAYS more. */
  due: string;
}

const compare = (a: string, b: string) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0);

/** The budgeted moments due today, or carried over from the 2 days before, that the data holds. */
export function dueMoments(m: MomentsBody, today: string): DueMoment[] {
  const inWindow = (due: string) => due >= addDays(today, -CARRY_DAYS) && due <= today;
  const out: DueMoment[] = [];
  for (const c of m.arrivals.chains) {
    const due = addDays(c.day, 1);
    if (inWindow(due)) out.push({ target: { kind: 'welcome', selector: c.selector }, due });
  }
  for (const c of m.countdowns) {
    if (c.days_left >= COUNTDOWN_DAYS.min && c.days_left <= COUNTDOWN_DAYS.max) out.push({ target: { kind: 'countdown', metric: c.metric, threshold: c.threshold }, due: today });
  }
  for (const w of m.weeks) {
    const movers = addDays(w.week, WEEKLY_DUE.movers);
    if (inWindow(movers) && moversOf(m.weeks, w.week) !== null) out.push({ target: { kind: 'movers', monday: w.week }, due: movers });
    const spotlight = addDays(w.week, WEEKLY_DUE.spotlight);
    if (inWindow(spotlight) && w.spotlight !== null) out.push({ target: { kind: 'spotlight', monday: w.week }, due: spotlight });
  }
  for (const monday of [weekStart(today), addDays(weekStart(today), -7)]) {
    const due = addDays(monday, WEEKLY_DUE.roundup);
    if (inWindow(due) && roundupOf(m, monday) !== null) out.push({ target: { kind: 'roundup', monday }, due });
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * The moments to post now: those not already sent or seeded, welcome first, then countdown, movers, spotlight and New
 * on CCIP (an older one first within a kind), as many as today's budget still allows.
 */
export function pickMoments(due: readonly DueMoment[], o: { done: ReadonlySet<string>; sentToday: number }): DueMoment[] {
  return due
    .filter((d) => !o.done.has(broadcastKey(d.target)))
    .sort(
      (a, b) =>
        BUDGETED_MOMENTS.indexOf(a.target.kind) - BUDGETED_MOMENTS.indexOf(b.target.kind) ||
        compare(a.due, b.due) ||
        compare(broadcastKey(a.target), broadcastKey(b.target)),
    )
    .slice(0, Math.max(0, MOMENTS_PER_DAY - o.sentToday));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Post them after the finalize broadcasts.**

The hourly retry re-plans the same set: the moments already sent (or seeded) are skipped, and the day's budget is what is left after the ones sent today, so a released moment gets its place back and no third moment slips in (Review Focus 1).

```ts
}

/** After the finalize posts, the moments due today, under their own switch; one moment's failure never stops the next. */
async function broadcastMoments(c: RunContext, days: readonly string[], outputs: OutputSet): Promise<void> {
  const mode = momentMode(c.env);
  if (mode === 'off' || !days.includes(addDays(dayOf(c.deps.now()), -1))) return;
  const enabled = selectOutputs(outputs, mode);
  for (const p of await planMoments(c, await loadMomentsSource(c))) {
    try {
      await deliver(c, p, enabled);
    } catch (err) {
      await c.alert('broadcast-run', `${broadcastKey(p.target)} failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
    }
  }
}

/** The budgeted moments due today that the day's budget still allows; the first run seeds them instead. */
async function planMoments(c: RunContext, src: MomentsSource): Promise<Planned[]> {
  const db = c.env.DB;
  const today = dayOf(c.deps.now());
  const due = dueMoments(src.moments, today);
  if (await seedMoments(c, due)) return [];
  const values = await store.getMetas(db, due.map((d) => broadcastKey(d.target)));
  const done = new Set([...values].filter(([, v]) => v.startsWith('sent:') || v.startsWith('seeded:')).map(([key]) => key));
  return pickMoments(due, { done, sentToday: await store.momentsSentOn(db, today) }).flatMap((d) => {
    const facts = momentFacts(src, d.target);
    return facts === null ? [] : [{ target: d.target, facts }];
  });
}

/** The first run marks every moment due today as seeded, without posting it, so switching moments on posts no old ones. */
async function seedMoments(c: RunContext, due: readonly DueMoment[]): Promise<boolean> {
  const db = c.env.DB;
  if ((await store.getMeta(db, MOMENTS_SEED_KEY)) !== null) return false;
  const value = `seeded:${c.deps.now().toISOString()}`;
  await store.markMeta(db, due.map((d) => broadcastKey(d.target)), value);
  await store.setMeta(db, MOMENTS_SEED_KEY, value);
  return true;
}
```

In `deliver`, write a moment's own copy. Replace:


with:


Create `worker/test/poll.test.ts`:

```ts
import type { Dim } from '@ccip-dev/core';
import { fakeFetch, jsonResponse, NETWORKS } from '@ccip-dev/core/testing';
import { env } from 'cloudflare:workers';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { PollFacts } from '../src/broadcasts/moment-facts';
import { telegramOutputs, type Broadcast, type BroadcastOutput } from '../src/broadcasts/outputs';
import { closeLastWeekPoll } from '../src/broadcasts/poll';
import { MOMENTS_SEED_KEY, runBroadcasts } from '../src/broadcasts/run';
import * as store from '../src/store';
import { harness, resetStorage, seedRegistry } from './helpers';

beforeEach(resetStorage);

const ETH = NETWORKS.ethereum.chainSelector;
const BASE = NETWORKS.base.chainSelector;
const MONDAY = '2026-10-12';
const NOW = '2026-10-12T00:07:00.000Z';
const POLL_KEY = 'broadcast:poll:2026-10-12';
const PNG = [137, 80, 78, 71];
const OPTIONS = [{ key: BASE, label: 'Base' }, { key: ETH, label: 'Ethereum' }, { key: 'other', label: 'Another chain' }];
const QUESTION = 'Which chain will pay the most CCIP fees this week?';

const card = (failing: string | null = null) =>
  fakeFetch((url) => {
    if (!url.startsWith('https://ccip.dev/og/')) return undefined;
    return failing !== null && url.includes(failing) ? new Response('down', { status: 500 }) : new Response(new Uint8Array(PNG), { headers: { 'content-type': 'image/png' } });
  });

/** Last week Base paid $5 in fees and Ethereum $3, on an old lane, so this week's poll asks about fee chains. */
async function seedLastWeek(): Promise<void> {
  await seedRegistry([NETWORKS.ethereum, NETWORKS.base], []);
  const totals = (day: string) => ({ day, messages: 10, token_messages: 0, usd_value: 10, fee_usd: 8, unique_senders: 1, median_delivery_s: null, unpriced_messages: 0 });
  const row = (day: string, dim: Dim, key: string, fee: number | null) => ({ day, dim, key, messages: 5, usd_value: 5, fee_usd: fee });
  await store.replaceDaily(env.DB, totals('2026-08-03'), [row('2026-08-03', 'lane', `${ETH}>${BASE}`, null)], NOW);
  await store.replaceDaily(env.DB, totals('2026-10-11'), [row('2026-10-11', 'lane', `${ETH}>${BASE}`, 8), row('2026-10-11', 'src_chain', BASE, 5), row('2026-10-11', 'src_chain', ETH, 3)], NOW);
}

function recorder(name: string, o: { after?: string; failures?: number } = {}) {
  const sent: string[] = [];
  let failures = o.failures ?? 0;
  const output: BroadcastOutput = {
    name,
    after: o.after,
    send: async (b) => {
      if (failures > 0) {
        failures -= 1;
        return 'failed';
      }
      sent.push(b.key);
      return 'sent';
    },
  };
  return { output, sent };
}

async function mondayRun(o: { now?: string; mode?: string; cardFails?: number; pollFails?: number; fetch?: typeof fetch } = {}) {
  const channel = recorder('telegram-channel', { failures: o.cardFails });
  const drafts = recorder('x-draft');
  const preview = recorder('telegram-preview');
  const channelPoll = recorder('telegram-poll', { after: 'telegram-channel', failures: o.pollFails });
  const draftsPoll = recorder('telegram-poll', { after: 'telegram-preview' });
  const h = harness({ now: o.now ?? NOW, fetch: o.fetch ?? card(), env: { BROADCASTS: 'on', MOMENTS: o.mode ?? 'on' } });
  await runBroadcasts(h.c, { kind: 'finalize', days: ['2026-10-11'] }, {
    channel: [channel.output], drafts: [drafts.output], preview: [preview.output], channelPoll: [channelPoll.output], draftsPoll: [draftsPoll.output],
  });
  const polls = (keys: string[]) => keys.filter((k) => k === POLL_KEY);
  return {
    channel: polls(channel.sent), drafts: polls(drafts.sent), preview: polls(preview.sent), channelPoll: channelPoll.sent, draftsPoll: draftsPoll.sent,
    reports: channel.sent.filter((k) => k !== POLL_KEY), alerts: h.alerts.map((a) => a.signature),
  };
}

describe('the Monday poll', () => {
  beforeEach(async () => {
    await seedLastWeek();
    await store.setMeta(env.DB, MOMENTS_SEED_KEY, 'seeded:2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z');
  });

  it('posts its card after the weekly report, then the poll to the same chat, with no X draft', async () => {
    // #when
    const run = await mondayRun();
    // #then
    expect(run).toEqual({
      channel: [POLL_KEY], drafts: [], preview: [], channelPoll: [POLL_KEY], draftsPoll: [],
      reports: ['broadcast:daily:2026-10-11', 'broadcast:weekly:2026-10-05'], alerts: [],
    });
  });

  it('sends the card as a preview and the poll to the drafts chat in drafts mode', async () => {
    const run = await mondayRun({ mode: 'drafts' });
    expect([run.channel, run.drafts, run.preview, run.channelPoll, run.draftsPoll]).toEqual([[], [], [POLL_KEY], [], [POLL_KEY]]);
  });

  it('retries a failed poll alone, without posting its card again', async () => {
    // #given the card went out and the poll failed
    const first = await mondayRun({ pollFails: 1 });
    // #when the hourly run retries
    const retry = await mondayRun({ now: '2026-10-12T01:00:00.000Z' });
    // #then
    expect({ first: [first.channel, first.channelPoll, first.alerts], retry: [retry.channel, retry.channelPoll], done: (await store.getMeta(env.DB, POLL_KEY))?.startsWith('sent:') }).toEqual({
      first: [[POLL_KEY], [], [`broadcast-telegram-poll:${POLL_KEY}`]],
      retry: [[], [POLL_KEY]],
      done: true,
    });
  });

  it('holds the poll back while its card has not posted, and sends both on the next run', async () => {
    // #given the daily, the weekly and then the poll card fail on the channel
    const first = await mondayRun({ cardFails: 3 });
    // #when
    const retry = await mondayRun({ now: '2026-10-12T01:00:00.000Z' });
    // #then the poll waited without an alert of its own
    expect({ first: [first.channelPoll, first.alerts.filter((a) => a.includes('poll'))], retry: [retry.channel, retry.channelPoll] }).toEqual({
      first: [[], [`broadcast-telegram-channel:${POLL_KEY}`]],
      retry: [[POLL_KEY], [POLL_KEY]],
    });
  });

  it("posts when the weekly report's card fails", async () => {
    // #when
    const run = await mondayRun({ fetch: card('weekly/2026-10-05') });
    // #then
    expect({ channel: run.channel, channelPoll: run.channelPoll, reports: run.reports, alerts: run.alerts }).toEqual({
      channel: [POLL_KEY], channelPoll: [POLL_KEY], reports: ['broadcast:daily:2026-10-11'], alerts: ['broadcast-image:broadcast:weekly:2026-10-05'],
    });
  });

  it('is never planned on another day, so a Tuesday retry posts no poll', async () => {
    const tuesday = await mondayRun({ now: '2026-10-13T01:00:00.000Z' });
    expect([tuesday.channel, tuesday.channelPoll]).toEqual([[], []]);
  });
});

describe('the Monday poll and the moments budget', () => {

});
```
