```ts
describe('closeLastWeekPoll', () => {
  const RECORD = { chat_id: '@ccipdev', message_id: 7, question_kind: 'fee_chain', question: QUESTION, options: OPTIONS };
  const ENV = { BROADCASTS: 'on', MOMENTS: 'on', TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN: 'bot' };
  const telegram = (answer: () => Response) => fakeFetch((url) => (url.endsWith('/stopPoll') ? answer() : undefined));
  const closed = () => jsonResponse({ ok: true, result: { total_voter_count: 13, options: [{ voter_count: 5 }, { voter_count: 8 }, { voter_count: 0 }] } });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    await store.setMeta(env.DB, 'poll:2026-10-05', JSON.stringify(RECORD));
  });

  it("closes last week's poll on Monday and keeps its voter counts in its record", async () => {
    // #given
    const fetch = telegram(closed);
    // #when
    await closeLastWeekPoll(harness({ now: NOW, fetch, env: ENV }).c);
    // #then
    expect({ body: JSON.parse(String(fetch.calls[0]?.init?.body)), record: JSON.parse((await store.getMeta(env.DB, 'poll:2026-10-05'))!) }).toEqual({
      body: { chat_id: '@ccipdev', message_id: 7 },
      record: { ...RECORD, voters: [5, 8, 0], total_voters: 13 },
    });
  });

  it('alerts and keeps no counts when the poll cannot be closed, as when its message was deleted', async () => {
    // #given
    const fetch = telegram(() => jsonResponse({ ok: false, description: 'Bad Request: message to stop poll not found' }, 400));
    const { c, alerts } = harness({ now: NOW, fetch, env: ENV });
    // #when
    await closeLastWeekPoll(c);
    // #then
    expect({ alerts, record: JSON.parse((await store.getMeta(env.DB, 'poll:2026-10-05'))!) }).toEqual({
      alerts: [{
        signature: 'poll-close:2026-10-05',
        text: 'The poll of the week of 2026-10-05 could not be closed, so its result posts without vote shares: Telegram returned HTTP 400: Bad Request: message to stop poll not found',
      }],
      record: RECORD,
    });
  });

  it('does nothing on another day, with moments off, without a bot token, or for a poll already closed', async () => {
    // #given
    const fetch = telegram(closed);
    await store.setMeta(env.DB, 'poll:2026-09-28', JSON.stringify({ ...RECORD, voters: [1, 1, 1], total_voters: 3 }));
    // #when
    await closeLastWeekPoll(harness({ now: '2026-10-13T00:07:00.000Z', fetch, env: ENV }).c);
    await closeLastWeekPoll(harness({ now: NOW, fetch, env: { ...ENV, MOMENTS: 'off' } }).c);
    await closeLastWeekPoll(harness({ now: NOW, fetch, env: { ...ENV, BROADCASTS: 'off' } }).c);
    await closeLastWeekPoll(harness({ now: NOW, fetch, env: { ...ENV, TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN: '' } }).c);
    await closeLastWeekPoll(harness({ now: '2026-10-05T00:07:00.000Z', fetch, env: ENV }).c);
    // #then
    expect(fetch.calls).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('never throws, even when D1 and the alerter both fail', async () => {
    // #given
    const h = harness({ now: NOW, fetch: telegram(closed), env: ENV, db: new Proxy(env.DB, { get: () => () => { throw new Error('D1 down'); } }) });
    const c = { ...h.c, alert: async () => { throw new Error('alerter down'); } };
    const log = vi.spyOn(console, 'error').mockImplementation(() => {});
    // #when
    const run = closeLastWeekPoll(c);
    // #then
    await expect(run).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    log.mockRestore();
  });
});
```

```ts
      if (url.startsWith('https://api.telegram.org/')) return jsonResponse({ ok: true, result: { message_id: 9 } });

  const sunday = () => fakeCcip({ messages: [listMessage({ id: 's11', sendTs: '2026-10-11T12:00:00.000Z' })] });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    // #given Sunday is due, and last week's poll was sent

    await store.setMeta(env.DB, 'poll:2026-10-05', JSON.stringify(RECORD));
  });

  it('is closed before moments.json is published, so the file carries its vote shares', async () => {
    // #when
    await runFinalize(harness({ now: '2026-10-12T00:07:00.000Z', ccip: sunday(), fetch: telegram(), env: { TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN: 'bot', TELEGRAM_CHANNEL_ID: '' } }).c, 'early');

  });

});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Send the poll after its card.**

The poll is one more output with its own per-output key (`<key>#telegram-poll`), so the claim rules stay exactly as they are: a failed poll is retried alone and its card is not posted twice. It follows the card's output in the same chat; `deliver` holds it back, without an alert, until that output has sent. Its options pass through `noLinks` and `clip` (Review Focus 5); the record is written after the poll is out, and a failed write is logged, never a reason to send the poll again.

Before `telegramOutputs`, add the poll output:

```ts
/** Sends the week's poll after its card, and keeps its message id under `poll:<monday>` so next Monday's run can close it. */
function pollOutput(c: RunContext, config: TelegramConfig, after: string): BroadcastOutput {
  return {
    name: 'telegram-poll',
    after,
    async send(b) {
      if (b.facts.kind !== 'poll') return 'failed';
      const { poll } = b.facts;
      const sent = await sendTelegramPoll(c.deps, config, {
        question: clip(noLinks(poll.question), POLL_QUESTION_MAX),
        options: poll.options.map((o) => clip(noLinks(o.label), POLL_OPTION_MAX)),
      });
      if ('problem' in sent) {
        console.error(`[broadcast] telegram-poll failed for ${b.key}: ${sent.problem}`);
        return 'failed';
      }
      const record: PollRecord = { chat_id: config.chatId, message_id: sent.messageId, question_kind: poll.question_kind, question: poll.question, options: poll.options };
      await savePollRecord(c, pollRecordKey(poll.week), record);
      return 'sent';
    },
  };
}

/** The poll is out, so a lost record only costs next week's vote shares; it is never a reason to send the poll again. */
async function savePollRecord(c: RunContext, key: string, record: PollRecord): Promise<void> {
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
    try {
      await store.setMeta(c.env.DB, key, JSON.stringify(record));
      return;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(`[broadcast] saving ${key} failed (attempt ${attempt + 1})`, err instanceof Error ? err.name : 'error');
    }
  }
}
```

Plan it on Mondays, before the budgeted moments, outside the budget and the seeding (spec §4.2: it is never deferred). Replace:

```ts
/** The budgeted moments due today that the day's budget still allows; the first run seeds them instead. */
async function planMoments(c: RunContext, src: MomentsSource): Promise<Planned[]> {
  const db = c.env.DB;
  const today = dayOf(c.deps.now());
  const due = dueMoments(src.moments, today);
  if (await seedMoments(c, due)) return [];
  const values = await store.getMetas(db, due.map((d) => broadcastKey(d.target)));
  const done = new Set([...values].filter(([, v]) => v.startsWith('sent:') || v.startsWith('seeded:')).map(([key]) => key));
  return pickMoments(due, { done, sentToday: await store.momentsSentOn(db, today) }).flatMap((d) => {
    const facts = momentFacts(src, d.target);
    return facts === null ? [] : [{ target: d.target, facts }];
  });
}
```

with:

```ts
/** Monday's poll first, outside the budget and never seeded, then the budgeted moments due today. */
async function planMoments(c: RunContext, src: MomentsSource): Promise<Planned[]> {
  const db = c.env.DB;
  const today = dayOf(c.deps.now());
  const pollFacts = weekStart(today) === today ? momentFacts(src, { kind: 'poll', monday: today }) : null;
  const poll: Planned[] = pollFacts === null ? [] : [{ target: { kind: 'poll', monday: today }, facts: pollFacts }];
  const due = dueMoments(src.moments, today);
  if (await seedMoments(c, due)) return poll;
  const values = await store.getMetas(db, due.map((d) => broadcastKey(d.target)));
  const done = new Set([...values].filter(([, v]) => v.startsWith('sent:') || v.startsWith('seeded:')).map(([key]) => key));
  const picked = pickMoments(due, { done, sentToday: await store.momentsSentOn(db, today) }).flatMap((d) => {
    const facts = momentFacts(src, d.target);
    return facts === null ? [] : [{ target: d.target, facts }];
  });
  return [...poll, ...picked];
}
```

In `deliver`, let an output wait for the one it follows. Replace:

```ts

    else failedOutputs.push(output.name);

  // Every output sent, so the broadcast closes even if an output mark was lost; releasing it would post that output again.
  // With a failed output and a lost mark, the claim stays pending: releasing would re-send the unmarked output at once.

  else if (!unmarked) await store.releaseMeta(db, key, claim);
```

with:

```ts
  const broadcast: Broadcast = { key, kind: p.target.kind, image: checked.png, facts: p.facts, ...copy };
  const sentNames = new Set(outputs.filter((o) => !remaining.includes(o)).map((o) => o.name));
  const failedOutputs: string[] = [];
  let waiting = false;
  let unmarked = false;
  for (const output of remaining) {
    // An output that follows another (the poll after its card) waits, without an alert, until that one has sent.
    if (output.after !== undefined && !sentNames.has(output.after)) {
      waiting = true;
      continue;
    }
    const result = await output.send(broadcast).catch((err) => {
      console.error(`[broadcast] output ${output.name} threw`, err instanceof Error ? err.name : 'error');
      return 'failed' as const;
    });
    if (result === 'sent') {
      sentNames.add(output.name);
      unmarked = !(await markSent(c, outputKey(output), `sent:${now.toISOString()}`)) || unmarked;
    } else failedOutputs.push(output.name);
  }
  // Every output sent, so the broadcast closes even if an output mark was lost; releasing it would post that output again.
  // With a failed output and a lost mark, the claim stays pending: releasing would re-send the unmarked output at once.
  if (failedOutputs.length === 0 && !waiting) await markSent(c, key, `sent:${now.toISOString()}`);
  else if (!unmarked) await store.releaseMeta(db, key, claim);
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Close last week's poll before `moments.json` is published.**

Monday's card needs last week's votes, and `moments.json` is published inside `publishHistoryFiles`, before the broadcasts. So the early finalize closes the poll first. The hourly and 06:00 runs never close a poll.

Create `worker/src/broadcasts/poll.ts`:

```ts
import { addDays, dayOf, weekStart } from '@ccip-dev/core';
import { parsePollRecord, pollRecordKey } from '@ccip-dev/core/moments';
import type { RunContext } from '../context';
import * as store from '../store';
import { stopTelegramPoll } from '../telegram';
import { momentMode } from './outputs';

/**
 * Monday's early finalize runs this before it publishes moments.json: it closes last week's poll and keeps its voter
 * counts in the poll's record, so this week's card can show who called it. A poll that cannot be closed (deleted, or a
 * failed call) keeps no counts and alerts; nothing here ever fails the finalize.
 */
export async function closeLastWeekPoll(c: RunContext): Promise<void> {
  const today = dayOf(c.deps.now());
  const token = c.env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN;
  if (weekStart(today) !== today || momentMode(c.env) === 'off' || !token) return;
  const monday = addDays(today, -7);
  const key = pollRecordKey(monday);
  let problem: string | null;
  try {
    const record = parsePollRecord(await store.getMeta(c.env.DB, key));
    if (record === null || record.voters !== undefined) return;
    const closed = await stopTelegramPoll(c.deps, { token, chatId: record.chat_id }, record.message_id);
    if ('problem' in closed) problem = closed.problem;
    else if (closed.voters.length !== record.options.length) problem = `Telegram counted ${closed.voters.length} options for ${record.options.length}`;
    else {
      await store.setMeta(c.env.DB, key, JSON.stringify({ ...record, voters: closed.voters, total_voters: closed.total }));
      return;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    problem = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
  }
  try {
    await c.alert(`poll-close:${monday}`, `The poll of the week of ${monday} could not be closed, so its result posts without vote shares: ${problem}`);
  } catch (alertErr) {
    console.error('[broadcast] the poll-close alert failed', alertErr instanceof Error ? alertErr.name : 'error');
  }
}
```

```ts
const board = (key: string, rank: number, previous: number | null, value: number, label: string | null = null) => ({ key, label, value, rank, previous_rank: previous });

export const MOMENT_FILES: Record<string, unknown> = { 'moments.json': MOMENTS, 'replay.json': MOMENT_REPLAY, 'chains.json': MOMENT_CHAINS };

/** The broadcast fixtures, with moments.json and Tempo's chains.json and replay.json in place of theirs. */
export async function loadMoments<N extends PublicFileName>(name: N): Promise<PublicFile<N>> {
  return name in MOMENT_FILES ? (PUBLIC_SCHEMAS[name].parse(MOMENT_FILES[name]) as PublicFile<N>) : loadFixture(name);
}

/** loadMoments with one file's top-level fields replaced. */
export const withMoments = (patch: Partial<MomentsFile>): typeof loadMoments => async (name) => (name === 'moments.json' ? ({ ...MOMENTS, ...patch } as never) : loadMoments(name));
```

```ts
    expect({ words: texts(card), gold: JSON.stringify(card).split(GOLD_RIM).length - 1 }).toEqual({
      words: [
        'T', 'E', 'Tempo', 'Ethereum', 'ccip.dev', 'New chain · Sun, Oct 11, 2026', 'WELCOME TO CCIP', 'Chain #4 on CCIP', 'Tempo',
        'First message ', 'Oct 11, 2026 ', 'with ', 'Ethereum', 'First day: ', '38 messages ', '· ', '$412.5K ', 'moved',
        'Connected to ', 'Ethereum', ' · ', 'Base', 'Every chain: ', 'ccip.dev/chains',
      ],
      gold: 1,
    });
  });

  it('has no card for a chain moments.json does not list, or whose first day replay.json lacks', async () => {
    // #given replay.json ends on Oct 10
    const stale: typeof loadMoments = async (name) => (name === 'replay.json' ? ({ ...MOMENT_REPLAY, days: MOMENT_REPLAY.days.slice(0, -1) } as never) : loadMoments(name));
    // #then
    expect([await broadcastTree({ kind: 'welcome', selector: ETH }, inputs), await broadcastTree({ kind: 'welcome', selector: TEMPO }, { ...inputs, load: stale })]).toEqual([null, null]);
  });
});

describe('countdown card', () => {
  it('shows the next milestone big, its days left at the pace, and the way there from the last one', async () => {
    // #when
    const card = await tree({ kind: 'countdown', metric: 'fees', threshold: 2.5e6 });
    // #then $2.43M is 86% of the way from $2M to $2.5M: 516 of the bar's 600 px
    expect({ words: texts(card).slice(texts(card).indexOf('ccip.dev')), bar: JSON.stringify(card).includes('"width":516,"height":18') }).toEqual({
      words: [
        'ccip.dev', 'Countdown · Mon, Oct 12, 2026', 'COUNTDOWN', 'All-time fees paid to CCIP', '$2.5M', 'About ', '7 days ', 'away at ', '$11.6K ', 'a day',
        '$2M · Jul 20', '$2.4M now', '$2.5M', 'Last milestone: ', '$2M', ' on Jul 20, 2026', 'Every record: ', 'ccip.dev/records',
      ],
      bar: true,
    });
  });

  it('starts the bar from zero before the first milestone, and has no card for a threshold moments.json does not count down to', async () => {
    // #given a first milestone with none before it
    const first = withMoments({ countdowns: [{ metric: 'messages', threshold: 1_000, total: 400, per_day: 100, days_left: 6, previous: null }] });
    // #when
    const card = await tree({ kind: 'countdown', metric: 'messages', threshold: 1_000 }, first);
    // #then
    expect({ words: texts(card).filter((w) => ['0', '400 now', '1,000', 'All-time now '].includes(w)), missing: await broadcastTree({ kind: 'countdown', metric: 'fees', threshold: 5e6 }, inputs) }).toEqual({
      words: ['1,000', '0', '400 now', '1,000', 'All-time now '],
      missing: null,
    });
  });
});

describe('moment cards', () => {
  it('write no glyph the card fonts lack', async () => {
    // #when
    const targets: BroadcastTarget[] = [{ kind: 'welcome', selector: TEMPO }, { kind: 'countdown', metric: 'fees', threshold: 2.5e6 }];
    const words = (await Promise.all(targets.map(async (t) => texts(await tree(t))))).flat();
    // #then
    expect({ words: words.length > 0, missing: words.filter((w) => MISSING_GLYPHS.test(w)) }).toEqual({ words: true, missing: [] });
  });
});
```

At the end of the file, add the moved helpers and the moment building blocks (pill, text column, headline text, the hub sky):

```ts
/** "Oct 5 – 11, 2026", or "Sep 28 – Oct 4, 2026" across a month. */
export function cardWeek(monday: string): string {
  const sunday = addDays(monday, 6);
  const end = monday.slice(0, 7) === sunday.slice(0, 7) ? String(Number(sunday.slice(8))) : MONTH_DAY.format(date(sunday));
  return `${MONTH_DAY.format(date(monday))} – ${end}, ${sunday.slice(0, 4)}`;
}

export const MILESTONE_TITLES: Record<MilestoneMetric, string> = {
  messages: 'All-time messages on CCIP',
  value: 'All-time value moved over CCIP',
  fees: 'All-time fees paid to CCIP',
};

export async function iconsFor(sky: OrbitSky, icon: BroadcastInputs['icon']): Promise<Map<string, string | null>> {
  return new Map(await Promise.all(sky.coins.map(async (c) => [c.selector, await icon(c.selector)] as const)));
}

export const namesOf = (replay: ReplayFile, chains: PublicFile<'chains.json'>): ChainNames => chainNameMap([...replay.chains, ...chains.chains]);

export const hasReplayDay = (replay: ReplayFile, day: string) => replay.days.some((d) => d.day === day);

type SkyExtra = { dim?: boolean; gold?: string | null; starFree?: readonly StarFreeZone[] };

/** The day's (or week's) chains and lanes from replay.json, around the busiest chain. */
export function skyFor(replay: ReplayFile, names: ChainNames, from: string, to: string, extra: SkyExtra = {}): OrbitSky {
  const weights = windowWeights(replay, from, to);
  return orbitSky({
    chains: [...weights.chains].map(([selector, value]) => ({ selector, value, name: chainName(names, selector) })),
    lanes: weights.lanes,
    seed: dayNumber(to),
    ...extra,
  });
}

/**
 * One chain at the hub of its own sky with the chains it shared a lane with over [from, to] around it. Messages count
 * with value, so a partner on an unpriced lane still shows, and the hub always outweighs its partners.
 */
export function hubSky(replay: ReplayFile, names: ChainNames, hub: string, from: string, to: string, extra: SkyExtra = {}): OrbitSky {
  const lanes = windowWeights(replay, from, to).lanes.filter((l) => l.src === hub || l.dst === hub);
  const partners = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const l of lanes) {
    const other = l.src === hub ? l.dst : l.src;
    partners.set(other, (partners.get(other) ?? 0) + l.usd + l.messages);
  }
  const top = Math.max(1, ...partners.values());
  return orbitSky({
    chains: [{ selector: hub, value: top * 3, name: chainName(names, hub) }, ...[...partners].map(([selector, value]) => ({ selector, value, name: chainName(names, selector) }))],
    lanes: lanes.map((l) => ({ src: l.src, dst: l.dst, usd: l.usd + l.messages })),
    seed: dayNumber(to),
    ...extra,
  });
}

export const PILLS = {
  gold: { from: '#ffcf5a', to: '#ff9f43', ink: '#1a1205' },
  welcome: { from: '#7de3ff', to: '#4a7ff0', ink: '#04101f' },
  violet: { from: '#c6a8ff', to: '#7d5cff', ink: '#120a26' },
  green: { from: '#7fd4a0', to: '#2bb673', ink: '#04160c' },
} as const;

export function pill(text: string, colors: (typeof PILLS)[keyof typeof PILLS], icon: GlyphName | null = null): VNode {
  return row([icon ? glyph(icon, 18, colors.ink, { right: 8 }) : null, text], {
    alignSelf: 'flex-start', padding: '8px 16px', borderRadius: 999, backgroundImage: `linear-gradient(90deg, ${colors.from}, ${colors.to})`,
    color: colors.ink, fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 20, letterSpacing: 3,
  });
}

export const MOMENT_COLUMN_WIDTH = 660;

/** The left text column of a moment card, on the open sky. */
export function momentColumn(...children: (VNode | null)[]): VNode {
  return h('div', { style: { position: 'absolute', left: 52, top: 118, width: MOMENT_COLUMN_WIDTH, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' } }, ...children);
}

/** A name or figure at headline size, with a soft glow. */
export function bigText(text: string, size: number, glow = 'rgba(74,127,240,0.45)'): VNode {
  return h('div', { style: { fontSize: size, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: -3, lineHeight: 1.05, color: '#ffffff', textShadow: `0 0 40px ${glow}`, marginTop: 6, maxWidth: MOMENT_COLUMN_WIDTH } }, text);
}

/** A line inside the text column that wraps instead of running into the sky. */
export function columnLine(children: readonly Child[], style: Record<string, unknown> = {}): VNode {
  return row(children, { marginTop: 10, alignSelf: 'flex-start', maxWidth: MOMENT_COLUMN_WIDTH, flexWrap: 'wrap', fontSize: 26, color: COLORS.muted, ...style });
}

/** Headline size for a record or milestone figure. */
export function bigSize(text: string): number {
  return text.length <= 7 ? 132 : text.length <= 10 ? 104 : 80;
}

/** Headline size for a name: large for a short one, smaller as it grows, so it stays on one line in the column. */
export function nameSize(text: string): number {
  return text.length <= 9 ? 96 : text.length <= 14 ? 72 : 52;
}
```

Each reads `moments.json`, `replay.json` and `chains.json` (through `broadcastCard`, so with no-store) and answers null, so 404, when `moments.json` lacks the moment or `replay.json` lacks the day it draws. The countdown draws the day before `moments.json`'s `updated_at`, the last finalized day. Every ▲ ▼ ★ → is a glyph.

Create `site/worker/cards/moment-cards.ts`:

```ts
import { formatCount, formatUsd, formatUtcDay } from '@ccip-dev/core/format';
import type { Countdown } from '@ccip-dev/core/moments';
import { chainName } from '@ccip-dev/core/names';
import { formatThresholdUsd, type MilestoneMetric } from '@ccip-dev/core/records';
import { addDays } from '@ccip-dev/core/time';
import { h, type Child, type VNode } from '../h';
import {
  bigSize, bigText, bold, broadcastFrame, cardDay, COLORS, columnLine, date, eyebrow, hasReplayDay, hubSky, iconsFor, MILESTONE_TITLES, momentColumn, MONTH_DAY,
  nameSize, namesOf, pill, PILLS, row, skyFor, TEXT_COLUMN, type BroadcastInputs,
} from './broadcast-frame';
import { CHAINS_CTA, RECORDS_CTA } from './cta';
import { skyNodes } from './orbit-sky';

const BAR_WIDTH = 600;

/** A new chain gold at the hub of its own first day, linked to its first partners. */
export async function welcomeTree(selector: string, inputs: BroadcastInputs): Promise<VNode | null> {
  const [moments, replay, chains] = await Promise.all([inputs.load('moments.json'), inputs.load('replay.json'), inputs.load('chains.json')]);
  const arrival = moments.arrivals.chains.find((c) => c.selector === selector);
  if (arrival === undefined || !hasReplayDay(replay, arrival.day)) return null;
  const names = namesOf(replay, chains);
  const sky = hubSky(replay, names, selector, arrival.day, arrival.day, { gold: selector, starFree: [TEXT_COLUMN] });
  const name = chainName(names, selector);
  const partners = arrival.partners.map((p) => chainName(names, p));
  return broadcastFrame({
    background: skyNodes(sky, await iconsFor(sky, inputs.icon)),
    eyebrow: `New chain · ${cardDay(arrival.day)}`,
    body: momentColumn(
      pill('WELCOME TO CCIP', PILLS.welcome, 'star'),
      eyebrow(`Chain #${arrival.number} on CCIP`, { marginTop: 24 }),
      bigText(name, nameSize(name)),
      columnLine(['First message ', bold(`${formatUtcDay(arrival.day)} `), ...(partners[0] ? ['with ', bold(partners[0])] : [])]),
      columnLine(['First day: ', bold(`${formatCount(arrival.messages)} message${arrival.messages === 1 ? '' : 's'} `), '· ', bold(`${formatUsd(arrival.usd)} `), 'moved'], { fontSize: 24, color: COLORS.soft }),
    ),
    insight: partners.length === 0 ? [] : ['Connected to ', ...partners.slice(0, 3).flatMap((p, i): Child[] => [...(i > 0 ? [' · '] : []), bold(p)])],
    cta: CHAINS_CTA,
  });
}

const amount = (metric: MilestoneMetric, v: number) => (metric === 'messages' ? formatCount(v) : formatUsd(v));
const thresholdText = (metric: MilestoneMetric, v: number) => (metric === 'messages' ? formatCount(v) : formatThresholdUsd(v));

/** How far the total has come from the last milestone (or zero) to this one. */
function progressBar(c: Countdown): VNode {
  const from = c.previous?.threshold ?? 0;
  const share = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (c.total - from) / (c.threshold - from)));
  const start = c.previous ? `${thresholdText(c.metric, from)} · ${MONTH_DAY.format(date(c.previous.day))}` : '0';
  return h(
    'div',
    { style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', marginTop: 26, width: BAR_WIDTH } },
    h(
      'div',
      { style: { display: 'flex', width: BAR_WIDTH, height: 18, borderRadius: 9, backgroundColor: '#18202e', overflow: 'hidden' } },
      h('div', { style: { width: Math.max(18, Math.round(BAR_WIDTH * share)), height: 18, borderRadius: 9, backgroundImage: 'linear-gradient(90deg, #4a7ff0, #7de3ff)' } }),
    ),
    row(
      [h('span', { style: { color: COLORS.muted } }, start), h('span', { style: { color: COLORS.soft, fontWeight: 700 } }, `${amount(c.metric, c.total)} now`), h('span', { style: { color: COLORS.muted } }, thresholdText(c.metric, c.threshold))],
      { justifyContent: 'space-between', width: BAR_WIDTH, fontSize: 20, marginTop: 8 },
    ),
  );
}

/** The next milestone, its days left at the last 7 days' pace, and the way there from the last one, as of moments.json. */
export async function countdownTree(metric: MilestoneMetric, threshold: number, inputs: BroadcastInputs): Promise<VNode | null> {
  const [moments, replay, chains] = await Promise.all([inputs.load('moments.json'), inputs.load('replay.json'), inputs.load('chains.json')]);
  const countdown = moments.countdowns.find((c) => c.metric === metric && c.threshold === threshold);
  const asOf = addDays(moments.updated_at.slice(0, 10), -1);
  if (countdown === undefined || !hasReplayDay(replay, asOf)) return null;
  const names = namesOf(replay, chains);
  const sky = skyFor(replay, names, asOf, asOf, { dim: true, starFree: [TEXT_COLUMN] });
  const value = thresholdText(metric, threshold);
  const days = `${countdown.days_left} day${countdown.days_left === 1 ? '' : 's'}`;
  return broadcastFrame({
    background: skyNodes(sky, await iconsFor(sky, inputs.icon)),
    eyebrow: `Countdown · ${cardDay(moments.updated_at)}`,
    body: momentColumn(
      pill('COUNTDOWN', PILLS.violet),
      eyebrow(MILESTONE_TITLES[metric], { marginTop: 24 }),
      bigText(value, bigSize(value)),
      columnLine(['About ', bold(`${days} `), 'away at ', bold(`${amount(metric, countdown.per_day)} `), 'a day']),
      progressBar(countdown),
    ),
    insight: countdown.previous
      ? ['Last milestone: ', bold(thresholdText(metric, countdown.previous.threshold)), ` on ${formatUtcDay(countdown.previous.day)}`]
      : ['All-time now ', bold(amount(metric, countdown.total))],
    cta: RECORDS_CTA,
  });
}
```

Create `site/worker/cards/board-cards.ts`:

```ts
import { formatCount, formatFeeTotal, formatShare, formatUsd } from '@ccip-dev/core/format';
import { moversOf, type BoardName, type Climber } from '@ccip-dev/core/moments';
import { chainName, laneLabel, shortAddress, type ChainNames } from '@ccip-dev/core/names';
import type { BoardEntry } from '@ccip-dev/core/public';
import { addDays } from '@ccip-dev/core/time';
import { h, type Child, type VNode } from '../h';
import {
  bigText, bold, boldArrows, broadcastFrame, cardWeek, COLORS, columnLine, eyebrow, hasReplayDay, hubSky, iconsFor, momentColumn, nameSize, namesOf, pill, PILLS, row,
  skyFor, TEXT_COLUMN, type BroadcastInputs,
} from './broadcast-frame';
import { BOARDS_CTA, CHAINS_CTA } from './cta';
import { glyph, withArrows } from './glyphs';
import { skyNodes } from './orbit-sky';

const BOARD_TITLES: Record<BoardName, string> = {
  fee_chains: 'Most fees paid to CCIP this week',
  tokens: 'Most value moved by a token this week',
  lanes: 'Most value moved on a lane this week',
};
const BY: Record<BoardName, string> = { fee_chains: 'by fees', tokens: 'by value', lanes: 'by value' };

const addressOf = (key: string) => key.slice(key.indexOf(':') + 1);

function entryName(board: BoardName, entry: BoardEntry, names: ChainNames): string {
  if (board === 'fee_chains') return chainName(names, entry.key);
  if (board === 'tokens') return entry.label ?? shortAddress(addressOf(entry.key));
  return laneLabel(names, entry.key);
}

/** The chain to light gold: a token's chain, a lane's source. */
function goldOf(board: BoardName, key: string): string {
  if (board === 'fee_chains') return key;
  return board === 'tokens' ? key.slice(0, key.indexOf(':')) : (key.split('>')[0] ?? key);
}

const valueText = (board: BoardName, value: number) => (board === 'fee_chains' ? `${formatFeeTotal(value)} in fees` : `${formatUsd(value)} moved`);

/** A name at headline size; a lane's arrow is drawn as a glyph, since the card fonts have none. */
function headlineName(text: string): VNode {
  const size = nameSize(text);
  if (!text.includes(' → ')) return bigText(text, size, 'rgba(255,207,90,0.35)');
  return row(withArrows(text, Math.round(size * 0.55), '#ffffff'), { fontSize: size, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: -2, color: '#ffffff', marginTop: 6, flexWrap: 'wrap', maxWidth: 660 });
}

function climberRow(c: Climber, names: ChainNames): VNode {
  const gain: Child[] = c.gain === null ? ['new'] : [glyph('up', 16, COLORS.up, { right: 6 }), String(c.gain)];
  return row(
    [
      h('div', { style: { width: 64, fontFamily: 'JetBrains Mono', fontWeight: 800, color: COLORS.strong, fontSize: 26 } }, `#${c.entry.rank}`),
      h('div', { style: { display: 'flex', width: 300, overflow: 'hidden' } }, boldArrows(entryName(c.board, c.entry, names), 18)),
      h('div', { style: { width: 150, color: COLORS.muted } }, BY[c.board]),
      row(gain, { color: COLORS.up }),
    ],
    { fontSize: 24, marginTop: 10 },
  );
}

/** The week's new #1 (or top climber) big, gold in the week's sky, with up to 3 climbers under it. */
export async function moversTree(monday: string, inputs: BroadcastInputs): Promise<VNode | null> {
  const [moments, replay, chains] = await Promise.all([inputs.load('moments.json'), inputs.load('replay.json'), inputs.load('chains.json')]);
  const movers = moversOf(moments.weeks, monday);
  const week = moments.weeks.find((w) => w.week === monday);
  const sunday = addDays(monday, 6);
  if (movers === null || week === undefined || !hasReplayDay(replay, sunday)) return null;
  const names = namesOf(replay, chains);
  const head = movers.headline;
  const lead = head.kind === 'leader' ? { board: head.board, entry: head.entry } : { board: head.climber.board, entry: head.climber.entry };
  const sky = skyFor(replay, names, monday, sunday, { gold: goldOf(lead.board, lead.entry.key), starFree: [TEXT_COLUMN] });
  const detail: Child[] =
    head.kind === 'leader'
      ? [`${valueText(head.board, head.entry.value)}`, ...(head.dethroned ? [' · took #1 from ', boldArrows(entryName(head.board, head.dethroned, names), 18)] : [])]
      : [head.climber.gain === null ? `New in the top 10 at #${head.climber.entry.rank}` : `Up ${head.climber.gain} places to #${head.climber.entry.rank}`, ` · ${valueText(head.climber.board, head.climber.entry.value)}`];
  const topToken = week.boards.tokens[0];
  const busiest = week.boards.lanes[0];
  return broadcastFrame({
    background: skyNodes(sky, await iconsFor(sky, inputs.icon)),
    eyebrow: `Leaderboards · ${cardWeek(monday)}`,
    body: momentColumn(
      head.kind === 'leader' ? pill('NEW #1', PILLS.gold, 'star') : pill('TOP CLIMBER', PILLS.gold, 'up'),
      eyebrow(head.kind === 'leader' ? BOARD_TITLES[lead.board] : `Biggest climb ${BY[lead.board]} this week`, { marginTop: 24 }),
      headlineName(entryName(lead.board, lead.entry, names)),
      columnLine(detail),
      movers.climbers.length === 0
        ? null
        : h(
            'div',
            { style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', marginTop: 22, paddingTop: 12, borderTop: '1px solid #1f2a3c', width: 600 } },
            eyebrow(head.kind === 'leader' ? 'Climbers' : 'Also climbing', { fontSize: 16 }),
            ...movers.climbers.map((c) => climberRow(c, names)),
          ),
    ),
    insight: [
      ...(topToken ? ['Top token: ', bold(entryName('tokens', topToken, names))] : []),
      ...(topToken && busiest ? [' · '] : []),
      ...(busiest ? ['Busiest lane: ', boldArrows(laneLabel(names, busiest.key), 16)] : []),
    ],
    cta: BOARDS_CTA,
  });
}

const pctOf = (now: number | null, before: number | null) => (now === null || before === null || before === 0 ? null : ((now - before) / before) * 100);

function spotlightStat(label: string, value: string, pct: number | null): VNode {
  const up = pct !== null && pct >= 0;
  return h(
    'div',
    { style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', width: 160, marginTop: 10 } },
    h('div', { style: { fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 2, color: COLORS.muted, textTransform: 'uppercase' } }, label),
    h('div', { style: { fontSize: 36, fontWeight: 800, fontFamily: 'JetBrains Mono', marginTop: 4 } }, value),
    row(pct === null ? [] : [glyph(up ? 'up' : 'down', 13, up ? COLORS.up : COLORS.down, { right: 5 }), formatShare(Math.abs(pct))], { fontSize: 17, color: up ? COLORS.up : COLORS.down, marginTop: 2, height: 22 }),
  );
}

const sideLabel = (text: string) => h('span', { style: { color: COLORS.muted, fontSize: 16, letterSpacing: 2, fontWeight: 700, width: 160, flexShrink: 0 } }, text);

/** The week's fastest grower gold at the hub of its own sky, its week against the one before in a glass panel. */
export async function spotlightTree(monday: string, inputs: BroadcastInputs): Promise<VNode | null> {
  const [moments, replay, chains] = await Promise.all([inputs.load('moments.json'), inputs.load('replay.json'), inputs.load('chains.json')]);
  const s = moments.weeks.find((w) => w.week === monday)?.spotlight ?? null;
  const sunday = addDays(monday, 6);
  if (s === null || !hasReplayDay(replay, sunday)) return null;
  const names = namesOf(replay, chains);
  const sky = hubSky(replay, names, s.selector, monday, sunday, { gold: s.selector });
  const partners = s.partners.map((p) => chainName(names, p));
  const panel = h(
    'div',
    {
      style: {
        position: 'absolute', left: 52, top: 112, width: 540, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', padding: '24px 30px',
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(10,13,19,0.8)', border: '1px solid #233047', borderRadius: 22,
      },
    },
    row([glyph('up', 16, COLORS.up, { right: 8 }), 'FASTEST GROWER · VS LAST WEEK'], { fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: 3, color: COLORS.up }),
    row(
      [h('span', { style: { fontSize: 56, fontWeight: 800, color: COLORS.strong, letterSpacing: -1.5, maxWidth: 300, overflow: 'hidden' } }, chainName(names, s.selector)), h('span', { style: { fontSize: 24, color: COLORS.muted, marginLeft: 16 } }, `#${s.rank} by messages`)],
      { alignItems: 'flex-end', marginTop: 6 },
    ),
    h(
      'div',
      { style: { display: 'flex', marginTop: 4 } },
      spotlightStat('Messages', formatCount(s.messages), pctOf(s.messages, s.previous_messages)),
      spotlightStat('Value moved', formatUsd(s.usd), pctOf(s.usd, s.previous_usd)),
      spotlightStat('Fees', formatFeeTotal(s.fee_usd), pctOf(s.fee_usd, s.previous_fee_usd)),
    ),
    h(
      'div',
      { style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', marginTop: 16, paddingTop: 12, borderTop: '1px solid #1f2a3c' } },
      partners.length === 0 ? null : row([sideLabel('TOP PARTNERS'), ...partners.flatMap((p, i): Child[] => [...(i > 0 ? [' · '] : []), bold(p)])], { fontSize: 20 }),
      s.token === null ? null : row([sideLabel('TOP TOKEN'), bold(`${s.token.label ?? shortAddress(addressOf(s.token.key))} `), h('span', { style: { color: COLORS.muted } }, `· ${formatUsd(s.token.usd)} moved`)], { fontSize: 20, marginTop: 8 }),
    ),
  );
  return broadcastFrame({
    background: skyNodes(sky, await iconsFor(sky, inputs.icon)),
    eyebrow: `Chain spotlight · ${cardWeek(monday)}`,
    body: panel,
    insight: [glyph('up', 16, COLORS.up, { right: 8 }), 'Fastest-growing chain on CCIP this week'],
    cta: CHAINS_CTA,
  });
}
```

```ts
        'Base', '38%', 'Ethereum', '31%', 'Arbitrum One', '22%', 'Another chain', '9%', '38% of you called it',
        'This week', "Will CCIP fees beat last week's $29?", 'A', 'Up more than 10%', 'B', 'Within 10%', 'C', 'Down more than 10%',
        'Vote in the poll below · results next Monday', 'Every week: ', 'ccip.dev/history',
      ],
      goldBars: 1,
    });
  });

  it('shows the answer without shares or "called it" when last week\'s poll could not be closed', async () => {
    // #given
    const [last, current] = MOMENTS.polls;
    const unclosed = withMoments({ polls: [{ ...last!, votes: null, total_votes: null }, current!] });
    // #when
    const words = texts(await tree({ kind: 'poll', monday: POLL_MONDAY }, unclosed));
    // #then
    expect({ answer: words.includes('· $1.8K'), shares: words.filter((w) => w.endsWith('%') && !w.includes(' ')), called: words.some((w) => w.includes('called it')) }).toEqual({
      answer: true, shares: [], called: false,
    });
  });

  it('shows only this week\'s question, wider, for the first poll', async () => {
    // #given no poll was sent last week
    const first = withMoments({ polls: [MOMENTS.polls[1]!] });
    // #when
    const card = await tree({ kind: 'poll', monday: POLL_MONDAY }, first);
    // #then
    expect({ last: texts(card).includes('Last week'), wide: JSON.stringify(card).includes('"width":1096,"backgroundColor":"rgba(16,24,44,0.85)"') }).toEqual({ last: false, wide: true });
  });

  it('draws a lane option with an arrow glyph, and has no card for a week without a poll', async () => {
    // #given this week asks about lanes
    const lanes = withMoments({ polls: [{ ...MOMENTS.polls[1]!, question_kind: 'lane', options: [{ key: `${ETH}>${BASE}`, label: 'Ethereum → Base' }, { key: 'other', label: 'Another lane' }] }] });
    // #when
    const words = texts(await tree({ kind: 'poll', monday: POLL_MONDAY }, lanes));
    // #then
    expect({ option: words.includes('Ethereum') && words.includes('Base'), arrow: words.some((w) => w.includes('→')), missing: await broadcastTree({ kind: 'poll', monday: '2026-10-19' }, inputs) }).toEqual({
      option: true, arrow: false, missing: null,
    });
  });
});
```

The poll card puts last week's answer, each option's share (the answer gold) and "N% of you called it" beside this week's lettered options; a poll that could not be closed shows its answer without shares, and the first poll shows its question alone, wider. In `site/worker/cards/broadcast-frame.ts`, build `cardWeek` on a new `cardDays`. Replace:

```ts
/** "Oct 5 – 11, 2026", or "Sep 28 – Oct 4, 2026" across a month. */
export function cardWeek(monday: string): string {
  const sunday = addDays(monday, 6);
  const end = monday.slice(0, 7) === sunday.slice(0, 7) ? String(Number(sunday.slice(8))) : MONTH_DAY.format(date(sunday));
  return `${MONTH_DAY.format(date(monday))} – ${end}, ${sunday.slice(0, 4)}`;
}
```

with:

```ts
/** "Oct 5 – 9, 2026", or "Sep 28 – Oct 2, 2026" across a month. */
export function cardDays(from: string, to: string): string {
  const end = from.slice(0, 7) === to.slice(0, 7) ? String(Number(to.slice(8))) : MONTH_DAY.format(date(to));
  return `${MONTH_DAY.format(date(from))} – ${end}, ${to.slice(0, 4)}`;
}

/** "Oct 5 – 11, 2026", or "Sep 28 – Oct 4, 2026" across a month. */
export function cardWeek(monday: string): string {
  return cardDays(monday, addDays(monday, 6));
}
```

Create `site/worker/cards/week-cards.ts`:

```ts
import { formatCount, formatFeeTotal, formatUsd, formatUtcDay } from '@ccip-dev/core/format';
import { calledShare, roundupOf } from '@ccip-dev/core/moments';
import { chainName, laneLabel, shortAddress, type ChainNames } from '@ccip-dev/core/names';
import type { Poll, PollKind } from '@ccip-dev/core/public';
import { addDays } from '@ccip-dev/core/time';
import { h, type Child, type VNode } from '../h';
import {
  bold, boldArrows, broadcastFrame, cardDays, COLORS, eyebrow, hasReplayDay, iconsFor, momentColumn, namesOf, pill, PILLS, row, skyFor, TEXT_COLUMN, type BroadcastInputs,
} from './broadcast-frame';
import { HISTORY_CTA, REPLAY_CTA } from './cta';
import { glyph, withArrows } from './glyphs';
import { skyNodes } from './orbit-sky';

export const ROUNDUP_SHOWN = 5;

const plural = (n: number, word: string) => `${formatCount(n)} new ${word}${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}`;

function roundupColumn(title: string, items: readonly VNode[], total: number, width: number): VNode {
  return h(
    'div',
    { style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', width } },
    eyebrow(title, { fontSize: 18 }),
    ...items,
    total > items.length ? row([`+${total - items.length} more`], { fontSize: 22, marginTop: 10, color: COLORS.muted }) : null,
  );
}

const item = (children: readonly Child[]) => row(children, { fontSize: 24, marginTop: 10, color: COLORS.soft, maxWidth: 320, overflow: 'hidden' });

/** Monday to Friday's new lanes and tokens, up to 5 of each, over a dimmed sky of those days. */
export async function roundupTree(monday: string, inputs: BroadcastInputs): Promise<VNode | null> {
  const [moments, replay, chains] = await Promise.all([inputs.load('moments.json'), inputs.load('replay.json'), inputs.load('chains.json')]);
  const roundup = roundupOf(moments, monday);
  const friday = addDays(monday, 4);
  if (roundup === null || !hasReplayDay(replay, friday)) return null;
  const names = namesOf(replay, chains);
  const sky = skyFor(replay, names, monday, friday, { dim: true, starFree: [TEXT_COLUMN] });
  const lanes = roundup.lanes.slice(0, ROUNDUP_SHOWN).map((l) => item([boldArrows(laneLabel(names, l.key), 18)]));
  const tokens = roundup.tokens.slice(0, ROUNDUP_SHOWN).map((t) => item([bold(`${t.symbol ?? shortAddress(t.key.slice(t.key.indexOf(':') + 1))} `), `on ${chainName(names, t.chains[0] ?? '')}`]));
  return broadcastFrame({
    background: skyNodes(sky, await iconsFor(sky, inputs.icon)),
    eyebrow: `This week · ${cardDays(monday, friday)}`,
    body: momentColumn(
      pill('NEW ON CCIP', PILLS.green),
      h(
        'div',
        { style: { display: 'flex', marginTop: 24 } },
        lanes.length === 0 ? null : roundupColumn(plural(roundup.lanes.length, 'lane'), lanes, roundup.lanes.length, 330),
        tokens.length === 0 ? null : roundupColumn(plural(roundup.tokens.length, 'token'), tokens, roundup.tokens.length, 330),
      ),
    ),
    insight: ['CCIP now runs ', bold(`${formatCount(moments.network.lanes)} lanes`), ' between ', bold(`${formatCount(moments.network.chains)} chains`)],
    cta: REPLAY_CTA,
  });
}

const PAST_QUESTION: Record<PollKind, string> = {
  fee_chain: 'Which chain paid the most fees?',
  fees_change: 'Did CCIP fees beat the week before?',
  lane: 'Which lane moved the most value?',
};

/** An option or answer label; a lane's arrow is drawn as a glyph. */
const labelNode = (text: string, size: number, color: string) => row(withArrows(text, size, color), { color });

function answerRow(poll: Poll): VNode | null {
  const o = poll.outcome;
  if (o === null) return null;
  const label = poll.question_kind === 'fees_change' ? (poll.options.find((x) => x.key === poll.answer)?.label ?? o.label) : o.label;
  const value = poll.question_kind === 'lane' ? formatUsd(o.value) : formatFeeTotal(o.value);
  return row([glyph('star', 20, COLORS.gold, { right: 10 }), labelNode(label, 20, COLORS.strong), h('span', { style: { color: COLORS.muted, marginLeft: 8 } }, `· ${value}`)], {
    fontSize: poll.question_kind === 'lane' ? 28 : 34, fontWeight: 800, marginTop: 10, flexWrap: 'wrap',
  });
}

function shareBar(label: string, pct: number, answer: boolean): VNode {
  return row(
    [
      h('div', { style: { display: 'flex', width: 190, marginRight: 12, flexShrink: 0, overflow: 'hidden', fontWeight: 700 } }, labelNode(label, 14, answer ? COLORS.strong : COLORS.soft)),
      h('div', { style: { display: 'flex', width: 180, height: 14, borderRadius: 7, backgroundColor: '#18202e', flexShrink: 0 } }, h('div', { style: { width: Math.round(1.8 * pct), height: 14, borderRadius: 7, backgroundColor: answer ? COLORS.gold : '#3a4a68' } })),
      h('span', { style: { marginLeft: 12, color: answer ? COLORS.gold : COLORS.muted, fontWeight: 700 } }, `${pct}%`),
    ],
    { fontSize: 20, marginTop: 8 },
  );
}

const PANEL = { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', padding: '24px 28px', borderRadius: 22 };

function lastWeekPanel(last: Poll): VNode {
  const called = calledShare(last);
  return h(
    'div',
    { style: { ...PANEL, width: 500, backgroundColor: 'rgba(10,13,19,0.82)', border: '1px solid #233047' } },
    eyebrow('Last week', { fontSize: 18 }),
    h('div', { style: { fontSize: 24, color: COLORS.soft, marginTop: 10 } }, PAST_QUESTION[last.question_kind]),
    answerRow(last),
    ...(last.votes === null ? [] : last.options.map((o, i) => shareBar(o.label, last.votes![i] ?? 0, o.key === last.answer))),
    called === null ? null : h('div', { style: { fontSize: 22, color: COLORS.gold, fontWeight: 700, marginTop: 14 } }, `${called}% of you called it`),
  );
}

function thisWeekPanel(poll: Poll, width: number): VNode {
  const letter = (i: number) =>
    h('div', { style: { width: 34, height: 34, borderRadius: 17, backgroundColor: '#1d2a44', color: '#9cc0ff', fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 18, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', marginRight: 12, flexShrink: 0 } }, String.fromCharCode(65 + i));
  return h(
    'div',
    { style: { ...PANEL, width, backgroundColor: 'rgba(16,24,44,0.85)', border: '1px solid #2c4a8a' } },
    eyebrow('This week', { fontSize: 18, color: '#9cc0ff' }),
    h('div', { style: { fontSize: 34, fontWeight: 800, color: COLORS.strong, marginTop: 10, lineHeight: 1.2 } }, poll.question),
    ...poll.options.map((o, i) => row([letter(i), labelNode(o.label, 18, COLORS.strong)], { fontSize: 24, fontWeight: 700, marginTop: 10 })),
  );
}

/** Last week's answer and how the votes went beside this week's question; the first poll shows its question alone, wider. */
export async function pollTree(monday: string, inputs: BroadcastInputs): Promise<VNode | null> {
  const [moments, replay, chains] = await Promise.all([inputs.load('moments.json'), inputs.load('replay.json'), inputs.load('chains.json')]);
  const poll = moments.polls.find((p) => p.week === monday);
  const last = moments.polls.find((p) => p.week === addDays(monday, -7) && p.answer !== null) ?? null;
  const sunday = addDays(monday, -1);
  if (poll === undefined || !hasReplayDay(replay, sunday)) return null;
  const names: ChainNames = namesOf(replay, chains);
  const sky = skyFor(replay, names, addDays(monday, -7), sunday, { dim: true, starFree: [{ x: 40, y: 40, width: 1120, height: 560 }] });
  return broadcastFrame({
    background: skyNodes(sky, await iconsFor(sky, inputs.icon)),
    eyebrow: `Prediction · Week of ${formatUtcDay(monday)}`,
    body: h(
      'div',
      { style: { position: 'absolute', left: 52, top: 112, width: 1096, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between' } },
      last === null ? null : lastWeekPanel(last),
      thisWeekPanel(poll, last === null ? 1096 : 560),
    ),
    insight: ['Vote in the poll below · results next Monday'],
    cta: HISTORY_CTA,
  });
}
```

**Interfaces:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Document moments.**

In `docs/runbook.md`, before `## Incidents and alerts`, add:

```markdown
## Moments

After the broadcasts of each 00:07 finalize, the Worker posts "moments": a welcome for each new chain, milestone countdowns, leaderboard movers, a weekly chain spotlight, a weekly "New on CCIP" roundup and a weekly Telegram poll. They come from `moments.json`, which the finalize publishes next to `history.json`; each post's card reads that file too. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-moments-design.md`.

- **Rhythm** (UTC):
  - Welcome: the day after a chain's first message (its first day on either end of a lane, as in `replay.json`).
  - Countdown: on any day when the next all-time messages, value or fees milestone is 2 to 7 days away at the last 7 days' pace; once per milestone.
  - Monday: the poll, after the weekly report: its card, then the poll itself in the same chat. It has no X draft.
  - Tuesday: movers, for the week that ended on Sunday. Thursday: the spotlight, for the same week. Saturday: New on CCIP, for Monday to Friday.
  - Movers, the spotlight and New on CCIP skip a week with nothing to say.
- **Budget:** at most 2 moments a day, taken in the order welcome, countdown, movers, spotlight, New on CCIP. A moment that misses its day is planned again on each of the next 2 days, then dropped. The daily, weekly, record, milestone and Reserve posts and the poll do not count. The count is the moment keys (not their `#` rows) whose value is `sent:` with today's date, so a moment that failed and was released gets its place back at the next hourly run.
- **Switch:** `MOMENTS` under `[vars]` in `worker/wrangler.toml` is `off`, `drafts` or `on` (shipped `on`), and works like `BROADCASTS` for moments only. A moment posts only as far as both allow: `off` in either stops moments; `drafts` in either sends them to the drafts chat only (the X draft and the preview, and for the poll its card as the preview, then the poll). To stop moments, set `MOMENTS = "off"` and push.
- **First run:** without `broadcast:moments:seeded`, the first run marks every moment due that day `seeded:<time>` without posting it, then sets that key. The poll is not seeded: it posts on the first Monday.
- **Keys** in D1 `meta`, claimed and marked like the other broadcasts, with `#<output>` rows: `broadcast:welcome:<selector>`, `broadcast:countdown:<metric>:<threshold>`, `broadcast:poll:<monday>`, `broadcast:movers:<monday>`, `broadcast:spotlight:<monday>`, `broadcast:roundup:<monday>`. The poll's outputs are `telegram-channel` then `telegram-poll` in `on`, and `telegram-preview` then `telegram-poll` in `drafts`; the poll waits until its card has posted, so a failed poll is retried alone and its card is not posted twice.
- **The poll's row:** `poll:<monday>` (the Monday of the week the poll asks about) holds `{ chat_id, message_id, question_kind, question, options }` once the poll is sent. Monday's 00:07 finalize closes last week's poll with `stopPoll` before it publishes `moments.json`, and adds `voters` and `total_voters`; `moments.json` turns them into shares. A poll that cannot be closed keeps no counts and alerts `poll-close:<monday>`; its result is posted without "N% of you called it". The hourly and 06:00 runs never close a poll, so if the 00:07 run fails entirely, last week's poll stays open and its result posts without shares.
  `pnpm --filter @ccip-dev/worker exec wrangler d1 execute ccip-dev --remote --command "SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key = 'poll:<monday>'"`
- **Re-post one:** delete its key and its `#` rows outside 23:55–00:30 UTC, as for the other broadcasts. The next hourly run posts it again while it is still due (its day or the 2 after), within that day's budget. Deleting a sent moment's key also frees its place in the budget of the day it was sent. Re-posting the poll sends a second Telegram poll and overwrites `poll:<monday>`, so only do it when the first poll message was deleted.
  `pnpm --filter @ccip-dev/worker exec wrangler d1 execute ccip-dev --remote --command "DELETE FROM meta WHERE key = 'broadcast:movers:<monday>' OR key LIKE 'broadcast:movers:<monday>#%'"`
- **A card:** `https://ccip.dev/og/broadcast/welcome/<selector>.png`, `…/countdown/<metric>/<threshold>.png`, and `…/poll/<monday>.png`, `…/movers/<monday>.png`, `…/spotlight/<monday>.png`, `…/roundup/<monday>.png`. One that `moments.json` does not hold answers 404.
```

In the alerts table, after the `day-leaders-publish` row, add:

```markdown
| `moments-publish` | `moments.json` was not published; the other history files still were. The moment cards read it, so that day's moments answer 404 and are not posted until a later publish works (the hourly runs retry them while they are due). Read the error in the alert and the finalize logs. |
```

After the `broadcast-telegram-preview:<key>` row, add:

```markdown
| `broadcast-telegram-poll:<key>` | The week's poll was not sent after its card posted. The Worker logs give Telegram's answer. The key is released and the next hourly run sends the poll alone; its card is not posted again. |
| `poll-close:<monday>` | Last week's poll (the week of `<monday>`) could not be closed at Monday's 00:07: its message was deleted, the bot lost its rights in the chat, or the call failed. This week's card shows last week's answer without vote shares. Nothing to retry; the poll stays open in Telegram until someone closes it by hand. |
```

In `docs/methodology.md`, before `## Coverage`, add:

```markdown
## Moments

Besides the daily and weekly reports, @ccipdev posts moments: short posts about one chain, token or week. Each is computed from the same data as the site, after each day is finalized.

- **A new chain** is welcomed the day after its first CCIP message, on either side of a lane. Its number is how many chains had sent or received a message by that day. Its first partners are the chains on its lanes that day, by value.
- **A countdown** names the next all-time milestone (the messages, value and fee thresholds of the Records page) when it is 2 to 7 days away at the pace of the last 7 days. Under 2 days the milestone post itself is about to come.
- **Movers** compare last week's top 10 (Monday to Sunday) with the week before's, on three boards: chains by fees paid, tokens by value and lanes by value. A new #1 leads (fees first, then tokens, then lanes); otherwise the biggest climb does. Climbers gained 3 places or more, or entered the top 10.
- **The spotlight** is the chain whose messages (sent and received) grew the most against the week before, among chains with at least 100 messages last week and 50 the week before, and whose first message came before that week began. A chain is not spotlighted again within 4 weeks.
- **New on CCIP** lists the lanes and tokens whose first message came Monday to Friday. A token is counted by its registry group, so a token's new chain is not a new token. A lane of a newly welcomed chain is left out.
- **The poll** asks, in turn by week, which chain will pay the most fees, whether fees will beat last week's total (by more than 10%, within 10%, or less), or which lane will move the most value. The answer is taken from our data once the week is complete.
- **Tags:** at most 5 @handles a post, and only verified ones.
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Check the new sections.** Run `grep -c "^## Moments" docs/runbook.md docs/methodology.md`. Expected: `docs/runbook.md:1` and `docs/methodology.md:1`.

```bash
git add docs/runbook.md docs/methodology.md
git commit -m "docs: moments in the runbook and the methodology" -m "Co-Authored-By: <your model> <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

### Task 12: Local preview of the moment cards (not committed)

Tasks 8–10 draw the cards from fixtures. This task draws them from the real code against live data, for the controller to look at next to the mockups. Until Task 2 is deployed, `data.ccip.dev` has no `moments.json`, so the script builds a rough one from the live public files with the real core rules (`rankBoard`, `spotlightPick`, `countdownsOf`, `pollQuestion`, `pollAnswer`); where live data has no such moment that week (no new chain in 30 days, no new lane that week), it borrows one, for looking only. Nothing here is committed.

**Files:** none in the repo. Scratch only: `<scratch>/preview-moments.mts` and `<scratch>/cards/*.png`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 (`countdownsOf`, `pollAnswer`, `pollQuestion`, `rankBoard`, `roundupOf`, `spotlightPick`, `voteShares`, `MomentsFileSchema` through `PUBLIC_SCHEMAS`), Tasks 8–10 (`broadcastTree` and the six trees), and the live code `iconHref`, `CARD_FONTS`, `@cf-wasm/og`.
- Produces: six PNGs to look at.

- [ ] **Step 1: Save the script.** Save this as `preview-moments.mts` in your scratch directory and replace every `<repo>` with the absolute repo path.

```ts
import { mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { render } from '<repo>/site/node_modules/@cf-wasm/og/dist/node.js';
import { countdownsOf, pollAnswer, pollQuestion, rankBoard, roundupOf, spotlightPick, voteShares } from '<repo>/packages/core/src/moments.ts';
import { chainName, chainNameMap, laneLabel } from '<repo>/packages/core/src/names.ts';
import { PUBLIC_SCHEMAS } from '<repo>/packages/core/src/public.ts';
import { addDays, lastCompleteMonday, weekStart } from '<repo>/packages/core/src/time.ts';
import { iconHref } from '<repo>/site/src/lib/chain-icons.ts';
import { broadcastTree } from '<repo>/site/worker/cards/broadcast-cards.ts';
import { CARD_FONTS } from '<repo>/site/worker/fonts.ts';

const OUT = process.argv[2];
if (!OUT) throw new Error('usage: tsx preview-moments.mts <out dir>');
const get = async (name: string) => {
  const res = await fetch(`https://data.ccip.dev/v1/${name}`, { headers: { 'user-agent': 'curl/8.7.1' } });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${name}: HTTP ${res.status}`);
  return res.json();
};
const names = ['history.json', 'replay.json', 'chains.json', 'day-leaders.json', 'top/token.json'];
const files: Record<string, any> = Object.fromEntries(await Promise.all(names.map(async (n) => [n, await get(n)])));
const replay = files['replay.json'];
const lastDay: string = replay.days.at(-1).day;
const latest = lastCompleteMonday(lastDay);
const chainNames = chainNameMap([...replay.chains, ...files['chains.json'].chains]);
const laneKey = (i: number) => `${replay.chains[replay.lanes[i][0]].selector}>${replay.chains[replay.lanes[i][1]].selector}`;
const laneTotals = (from: string, to: string) => {
  const out = new Map<string, { messages: number; usd: number; day: string }>();
  for (const d of replay.days) {
    if (d.day < from || d.day > to) continue;
    for (const [lane, messages, usd] of d.lanes) {
      const t = out.get(laneKey(lane)) ?? { messages: 0, usd: 0, day: d.day };
      out.set(laneKey(lane), { messages: t.messages + messages, usd: t.usd + usd, day: t.day });
    }
  }
  return out;
};
const chainTotals = (from: string, to: string) => {
  const out = new Map<string, { messages: number; usd: number }>();
  for (const [key, t] of laneTotals(from, to)) {
    for (const s of key.split('>')) {
      const c = out.get(s) ?? { messages: 0, usd: 0 };
      out.set(s, { messages: c.messages + t.messages, usd: c.usd + t.usd });
    }
  }
  return out;
};
const sortedLanes = (from: string, to: string) => [...laneTotals(from, to)].sort((a, b) => b[1].usd - a[1].usd).map(([key, t]) => ({ key, label: null, value: Math.round(t.usd) }));
const feeWeek = (monday: string) => (files['day-leaders.json'].weeks.find((w: any) => w.week === monday)?.fee_chains ?? []).map((l: any) => ({ key: l.key, label: null, value: l.fee_usd ?? 0 }));

// A rough moments.json from the live public files, for looking at the cards before the Worker publishes the real one.
const since = addDays(lastDay, -29);
const firstLane = new Map<string, string>();
for (const d of replay.days) for (const [lane] of d.lanes) if (!firstLane.has(laneKey(lane))) firstLane.set(laneKey(lane), d.day);
const newest = [...replay.chains].sort((a: any, b: any) => b.first_day.localeCompare(a.first_day))[0];
const arrivalOf = (c: any, number: number) => {
  const lanes = [...laneTotals(c.first_day, c.first_day)].filter(([k]) => k.split('>').includes(c.selector));
  const partners = [...new Set(lanes.sort((a, b) => b[1].usd - a[1].usd).map(([k]) => k.split('>').find((s) => s !== c.selector)!))];
  return { selector: c.selector, day: c.first_day, number, partners, messages: lanes.reduce((s, [, t]) => s + t.messages, 0), usd: lanes.reduce((s, [, t]) => s + t.usd, 0) };
};
const chainArrivals = replay.chains.flatMap((c: any, i: number) => (c.first_day >= since ? [arrivalOf(c, i + 1)] : []));
const laneArrivals = [...firstLane].filter(([, day]) => day >= since).map(([key, day]) => ({ key, day, messages: laneTotals(day, day).get(key)!.messages, usd: laneTotals(day, day).get(key)!.usd }));
const tokens7d = files['top/token.json'].windows['7d'];
const tokenBoard = tokens7d.slice(0, 10).map((t: any) => ({ key: t.key, label: t.symbol ?? null, value: t.usd ?? 0 }));
const weekOf = (monday: string, previous: string, spotlight: any) => ({
  week: monday,
  boards: {
    fee_chains: rankBoard(feeWeek(monday), feeWeek(previous)),
    tokens: monday === latest ? rankBoard(tokenBoard, []) : rankBoard(tokenBoard.slice(1), tokenBoard),
    lanes: rankBoard(sortedLanes(monday, addDays(monday, 6)), sortedLanes(previous, addDays(previous, 6))),
  },
  spotlight,
});
const now = chainTotals(latest, addDays(latest, 6));
const prev = chainTotals(addDays(latest, -7), addDays(latest, -1));
const counts = [...now].map(([selector, t]) => ({ selector, messages: t.messages, previous: prev.get(selector)?.messages ?? 0, firstDay: replay.chains.find((c: any) => c.selector === selector)?.first_day ?? latest }));
const pick = spotlightPick({ monday: latest, chains: counts }, new Set()) ?? [...now].sort((a, b) => b[1].messages - a[1].messages)[0]![0];
const fee = feeWeek(latest).find((f: any) => f.key === pick);
const partners = [...new Set([...laneTotals(latest, addDays(latest, 6))].filter(([k]) => k.split('>').includes(pick)).sort((a, b) => b[1].usd - a[1].usd).map(([k]) => k.split('>').find((s) => s !== pick)!))].slice(0, 3);
const token = tokens7d.find((t: any) => t.key.startsWith(`${pick}:`));
const spotlight = {
  selector: pick,
  rank: [...now].sort((a, b) => b[1].messages - a[1].messages).findIndex(([s]) => s === pick) + 1,
  messages: now.get(pick)!.messages, previous_messages: prev.get(pick)?.messages ?? 0, usd: now.get(pick)!.usd, previous_usd: prev.get(pick)?.usd ?? 0,
  fee_usd: fee?.value ?? null, previous_fee_usd: feeWeek(addDays(latest, -7)).find((f: any) => f.key === pick)?.value ?? null,
  partners, token: token ? { key: token.key, label: token.symbol ?? null, usd: token.usd ?? 0 } : null,
};
const weeks = [weekOf(addDays(latest, -14), addDays(latest, -21), null), weekOf(addDays(latest, -7), addDays(latest, -14), null), weekOf(latest, addDays(latest, -7), spotlight)];
const current = weekStart(addDays(lastDay, 1));
const feeTotal = (monday: string) => files['history.json'].days.filter((d: any) => d.day >= monday && d.day <= addDays(monday, 6)).reduce((s: number, d: any) => s + (d.fee_usd ?? 0), 0);
const askedFrom = (monday: string) => {
  const w = weeks.find((x) => x.week === addDays(monday, -7))!;
  return {
    feeChains: w.boards.fee_chains.map((e) => ({ key: e.key, label: chainName(chainNames, e.key) })),
    lanes: w.boards.lanes.map((e) => ({ key: e.key, label: laneLabel(chainNames, e.key) })),
    feeTotal: feeTotal(addDays(monday, -7)),
  };
};
const lastAsked = pollQuestion(addDays(current, -7), askedFrom(addDays(current, -7)))!;
const lastWeek = weeks.find((w) => w.week === addDays(current, -7))!;
const answered = pollAnswer(lastAsked, {
  feeChain: lastWeek.boards.fee_chains[0] ? { key: lastWeek.boards.fee_chains[0].key, label: chainName(chainNames, lastWeek.boards.fee_chains[0].key), value: lastWeek.boards.fee_chains[0].value } : null,
  lane: lastWeek.boards.lanes[0] ? { key: lastWeek.boards.lanes[0].key, label: laneLabel(chainNames, lastWeek.boards.lanes[0].key), value: lastWeek.boards.lanes[0].value } : null,
  feeTotal: feeTotal(addDays(current, -7)),
  previousFeeTotal: feeTotal(addDays(current, -14)),
});
const voters = [17, 14, 10, 4].slice(0, lastAsked.options.length);
const moments: any = {
  schema_version: 1,
  updated_at: `${addDays(lastDay, 1)}T00:07:00.000Z`,
  attribution: files['history.json'].attribution,
  network: { chains: replay.chains.length, lanes: replay.lanes.length },
  arrivals: { chains: chainArrivals.length > 0 ? chainArrivals : [arrivalOf(newest, replay.chains.length)], lanes: laneArrivals, tokens: [] },
  weeks,
  countdowns: countdownsOf(files['history.json'].days),
  polls: [
    { ...lastAsked, ...answered, votes: voteShares(voters), total_votes: voters.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) },
    { ...pollQuestion(current, askedFrom(current))!, answer: null, outcome: null, votes: null, total_votes: null },
  ],
};
if (roundupOf(moments, latest) === null) {
  // No lane arrived that week: borrow the week's three biggest lanes, for looking only.
  moments.arrivals.lanes = sortedLanes(latest, addDays(latest, 4)).slice(0, 3).map((l) => ({ key: l.key, day: latest, messages: 1, usd: l.value }));
}
files['moments.json'] = moments;

const load = async (name: keyof typeof PUBLIC_SCHEMAS) => (PUBLIC_SCHEMAS[name] as any).parse(files[name]);
const icon = async (selector: string) => {
  const href = iconHref(selector);
  if (!href) return null;
  const bytes = await readFile(path.join('<repo>/site/public', href));
  return `data:${href.endsWith('.png') ? 'image/png' : 'image/svg+xml'};base64,${bytes.toString('base64')}`;
};
const countdown = moments.countdowns[0];
const targets: [string, any][] = [
  ['welcome', { kind: 'welcome', selector: moments.arrivals.chains.at(-1).selector }],
  ...(countdown ? [['countdown', { kind: 'countdown', metric: countdown.metric, threshold: countdown.threshold }] as [string, any]] : []),
  ['movers', { kind: 'movers', monday: latest }],
  ['spotlight', { kind: 'spotlight', monday: latest }],
  ['roundup', { kind: 'roundup', monday: latest }],
  ['poll', { kind: 'poll', monday: current }],
];
await mkdir(OUT, { recursive: true });
for (const [name, target] of targets) {
  const started = performance.now();
  const tree = await broadcastTree(target, { load: load as any, icon });
  if (!tree) {
    console.log(`${name}: no card (404)`);
    continue;
  }
  const png = await render(tree as never, { width: 1200, height: 675, fonts: CARD_FONTS as never, loadAdditionalAsset: async () => [] }).asPng();
  await writeFile(path.join(OUT, `${name}.png`), png.image);
  console.log(`${name}: ${path.join(OUT, `${name}.png`)} in ${Math.round(performance.now() - started)} ms`);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Render the cards.** From `site/`, run `../node_modules/.bin/tsx <scratch>/preview-moments.mts <scratch>/cards`. Expected: one line per card, `welcome`, `countdown`, `movers`, `spotlight`, `roundup` and `poll`, each with its path and a render time of about 1–2 s. A card that prints "no card (404)" means the rough data has no such moment; say so in the report.

- [ ] **Step 3: Compare them with the mockups.** Open each PNG with the Read tool next to its mockup in `docs/superpowers/specs/assets/2026-10-12-moments/` (`welcome.png`, `countdown.png`, `movers.png`, `spotlight.png`, `roundup.png`, `poll.png`). Check the pill, the headline, the lines and the strip of each against spec §7.2; that no text runs into the sky or out of its panel; that every ▲ ▼ ★ → is a glyph (no "NO GLYPH" box); and that the sky is dimmed on the countdown, New on CCIP and poll cards. The data differs from the mockups' (they were drawn from illustrative numbers), so compare layout and style, not figures. Describe what differs in the report. Do not commit the script or the PNGs.

## Self-review notes

- **Spec coverage:**
  - §1 owner decisions: `moments.json` from D1 after each finalize (Task 2), planning from it (Tasks 4, 6) and cards from it (Tasks 8–10); budget and bundling (Task 6); the weekly rhythm (Task 6 `WEEKLY_DUE`, Task 7 for Monday); the poll turning by week and Telegram-only (Tasks 1, 7); the spotlight as fastest grower (Tasks 1, 2); the six cards in the C2 style (Tasks 8–10, Task 12 to compare); the copy (Task 5).
  - §2 success criteria: once each, on its day, with its card, only when the data holds (Tasks 4, 6, 7; claims unchanged); no link, verified handles, at most 5 (Task 5); cards match the mockups (Tasks 8–10, Task 12); isolation (Tasks 2, 6, 7); no flood on the first run (Task 6); the switch (Task 6).
  - §3 scope: everything "In" has a task; nothing "Out" is built.
  - §4.1 when: Tasks 6 and 7. §4.2 budget: Task 6. §4.3 dedup and first run: Tasks 1 and 6. §4.4 mode: Task 6.
  - §5.1 rules: welcome, countdown, movers, spotlight, New on CCIP and the poll (Tasks 1, 2). §5.2 `moments.json` and its isolated publish (Tasks 1, 2). §5.3 copy (Task 5).
  - §6 the poll: close (Tasks 3, 7), answer (Tasks 1, 2), card (Task 10), `sendPoll` and the saved id (Tasks 3, 7), outputs (Task 7), limits (Tasks 1, 7).
  - §7.1 routes (Tasks 1, 8–10). §7.2 per type: welcome and countdown (Task 8), movers and spotlight (Task 9), New on CCIP and poll (Task 10).
  - §8 docs: Task 11. §9 testing: every listed test has a home (core: Task 1; Worker with real D1: Tasks 2, 4, 6, 7; copy: Task 5; cards: Tasks 8–10). §10 rollout: `MOMENTS = "on"` (Task 6) and the runbook (Task 11). §10a risks: the 5-handle cap (Task 5), `stopPoll` failure (Tasks 3, 7, 10), quiet weeks (Tasks 1, 6).
- **Placeholders:** none; every code step shows its code and every test step its test. The only fill-in is `<your model>` in the commit trailer, by design.
- **Names across tasks:**
  - Task 1: `BUDGETED_MOMENTS`, `MomentTarget`; `MomentsFile`, `MomentsWeek`, `BoardEntry`, `Spotlight`, `Poll`, `PollKind`; `MomentsBody`, `rankBoard`, `moversOf`, `Climber`, `spotlightPick(s)`, `SPOTLIGHT_SINCE`, `countdownsOf`, `roundupOf`, `pollQuestion`, `pollAnswer`, `calledShare`, `voteShares`, `clip`, `POLL_QUESTION_MAX`, `POLL_OPTION_MAX`, `PollRecord`, `pollRecordKey`, `parsePollRecord`.
  - Task 2: `lastCompleteMonday` (core), `buildMoments`, `store.getMetas`, `store.firstDays`, `store.breakdownBetween`, `store.chainWeekMessages`, `store.tokenGroupMembers`, `TTL.moments`.
  - Task 3: `sendTelegramPoll`, `stopTelegramPoll`.
  - Task 4: `Tagger.groupProject`, the moment facts types, `isMomentFacts`, `loadMomentsSource`, `momentFacts`.
  - Task 5: `composeMomentCopy`, `handleWriter`, `MOMENT_DROP`.
  - Task 6: `dueMoments`, `pickMoments`, `momentMode`, `store.momentsSentOn`, `MOMENTS_SEED_KEY`.
  - Task 7: `BroadcastOutput.after`, `OutputSet.channelPoll`/`draftsPoll`, `selectPollOutputs`, `closeLastWeekPoll`.
  - Tasks 8–10: `hubSky`, `pill`, `PILLS`, `momentColumn`, `bigText`, `columnLine`, `nameSize`, `cardDays`, `welcomeTree`, `countdownTree`, `moversTree`, `spotlightTree`, `roundupTree`, `pollTree`, `CHAINS_CTA`, `BOARDS_CTA`, `REPLAY_CTA`, `HISTORY_CTA`.
- **Checked on 2026-10-12:**
  - Every task's code was prototyped in a scratch worktree of this repo and its tests run against it; the full suites passed at the end (core 526, Worker 409, site 1,095 node and 9 workerd), and `pnpm typecheck` was clean.
  - The plan's own steps were then replayed task by task on a fresh worktree: each task's new tests failed before its implementation and every suite passed after it, and the end state matched the prototype file for file.
  - The Bot API's `sendPoll` and `stopPoll` were read from core.telegram.org/bots/api (Bot API 10.3, Aug 24, 2026).
  - The six cards were rendered from the real code against live data with Task 12's script and compared with the mockups by eye. That pass fixed the poll card's share labels running into their bars and a lane answer wrapping its value onto a second line.
  - `EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN` in the Worker's D1: the budget count reads `sqlite_autoindex_meta_1 (key>? AND key<?)`; the weekly chain grouping reads `daily_breakdown_dim`.
